<a id="s0005"></a>
## The Open Group

原文：The Open Group。PDF 物理页：[7]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

The Open Group（原文此标题连续出现两次）。

The Open Group 是一个全球性联盟，通过在900多家成员组织之间建立协作、包容和相互尊重的文化，借助技术标准与开源倡议推动业务目标的实现。成员来自多个行业，包括客户、系统与解决方案供应商、工具供应商、集成商、学术机构和顾问。

The Open Group 的使命是推动实现无边界信息流（Boundaryless Information Flow™），具体通过：

- 与客户合作，收集、理解并应对当前和新出现的需求，制定政策并分享最佳实践。
- 与供应商、联盟和标准组织合作，形成共识、促进互操作性，演进并集成规范和开源技术。
- 提供完整的服务集合，提高联盟的运营效率。
- 开发并运行业界领先的认证服务，鼓励采购经过认证的产品。

更多信息见 www.opengroup.org。The Open Group 出版广泛的技术文档，多数着眼于标准与指南的制定，也包括白皮书、技术研究、认证与测试文档，以及业务类出版物。详情见 www.opengroup.org/library。

The Open Group

The Open Group

**G242:p0007:c003 · PDF 7**

The Open Group is a global consortium that enables the achievement of business objectives through technology standards and open source initiatives by fostering a culture of collaboration, inclusivity, and mutual respect among our diverse group of 900+ memberships. Our membership includes customers, systems and solutions suppliers, tool vendors, integrators, academics, and consultants across multiple industries.

**G242:p0007:c004 · PDF 7**

The mission of The Open Group is to drive the creation of Boundaryless Information Flow™ achieved by:

**G242:p0007:c005 · PDF 7**

• Working with customers to capture, understand, and address current and emerging requirements, establish policies, and share best practices

**G242:p0007:c006 · PDF 7**

• Working with suppliers, consortia, and standards bodies to develop consensus and facilitate interoperability, to evolve and integrate specifications and open source technologies

**G242:p0007:c007 · PDF 7**

• Offering a comprehensive set of services to enhance the operational efficiency of consortia

**G242:p0007:c008 · PDF 7**

• Developing and operating the industry’s premier certification service and encouraging procurement of certified products

**G242:p0007:c009 · PDF 7**

Further information on The Open Group is available at www.opengroup.org.

**G242:p0007:c010 · PDF 7**

The Open Group publishes a wide range of technical documentation, most of which is focused on development of Standards and Guides, but which also includes white papers, technical studies, certification and testing documentation, and business titles. Full details are available at www.opengroup.org/library.

<a id="s0007"></a>
## 本文档

原文：This Document。PDF 物理页：[8]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本文档是关于运用系统性的企业架构方法构建环境可持续信息系统的TOGAF®系列指南，由The Open Group制定并批准。

本文档提出一种企业架构方法，降低组织信息系统的环境影响，并为实现2015年《巴黎协定》（COP21）目标作出贡献。

更多信息、工具、指南及其他资源见www.opengroup.org/architecture。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 指南提出降低信息系统环境影响的企业架构方法。
  - 适用边界：本指南不是已执行项目证据，也不是覆盖所有可持续议题的认证规范。
  - PDF 8：This document proposes an Enterprise Architecture approach to reduce the organization’s information system environmental impact and contribute to reaching the 2015 Paris Agreement (COP21) objective.

**G242:p0008:c001 · PDF 8**

**G242:p0008:c002 · PDF 8**

This document proposes an Enterprise Architecture approach to reduce the organization’s information system environmental impact and contribute to reaching the 2015 Paris Agreement (COP21) objective.

**G242:p0008:c003 · PDF 8**

More information is available, along with tools, guides, and other resources, at www.opengroup.org/architecture.

<a id="s0008"></a>
## 关于TOGAF系列指南

原文：About the TOGAF® Series Guides。PDF 物理页：[8]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF® 系列指南说明如何使用TOGAF标准，以及如何调整它以满足具体需要。

系列指南预期是TOGAF标准中发展最快的部分，定位为标准的指导部分。TOGAF基础内容预期保持长期稳定，而使用指导可以针对行业、架构风格、用途和问题。例如，支持扩展企业转型所需的利益相关者、关注点、视图和支撑模型，可能与将内部IT环境迁移到云所需的内容显著不同；两者都使用ADM，都从架构愿景开始，开发目标架构，并走向实施与迁移计划。TOGAF基础内容仍是跨行业、领域和风格的必要支架。


### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0008:c004 · PDF 8**

About the TOGAF® Series Guides

**G242:p0008:c005 · PDF 8**

The TOGAF® Series Guides contain guidance on how to use the TOGAF® Standard and how to adapt it to fulfill specific needs.

**G242:p0008:c006 · PDF 8**

The TOGAF® Series Guides are expected to be the most rapidly developing part of the TOGAF® Standard and are positioned as the guidance part of the standard. While the TOGAF® Fundamental Content is expected to be long-lived and stable, guidance on the use of the TOGAF® Standard can be industry, architectural style, purpose, and problem-specific. For example, the stakeholders, concerns, views, and supporting models required to support the transformation of an extended enterprise may be significantly different than those used to support the transition of an in-house IT environment to the cloud; both will use the Architecture Development Method (ADM), start with an Architecture Vision,

**G242:p0008:c007 · PDF 8**

®

**G242:p0008:c008 · PDF 8**

and develop a Target Architecture on the way to an Implementation and Migration Plan. The TOGAF Fundamental Content remains the essential scaffolding across industry, domain, and style.

</details>

<a id="s0009"></a>
## 术语

原文：Terminology。PDF 物理页：[8]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本文档使用以下术语：

- 信息与通信技术（ICT）：指包括数据中心、网络和终端在内的整个行业。这与The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组用语一致，也可用IT作为同义词。
- 信息系统：指某一企业支撑其运营所需的ICT。
- 数字（Digital）：也作为更通用的词语使用。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ICT涵盖数据中心、网络及终端；信息系统按特定企业运营范围界定。
  - 适用边界：生成范围图需覆盖终端和网络，不能只画云或数据中心。
  - PDF 8：• Information and Communication Technology (ICT) to speak about the full sector that includes data centers, networks, and terminals
  - PDF 8：• Information system is used to speak about the ICT required by a given enterprise to support its operations

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0008:c009 · PDF 8**

Terminology

**G242:p0008:c010 · PDF 8**

This document uses the following terminology:

**G242:p0008:c011 · PDF 8**

• Information and Communication Technology (ICT) to speak about the full sector that includes data centers, networks, and terminals

**G242:p0008:c012 · PDF 8**

It is aligned with the wording used by the Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group. Its synonym could be IT.

**G242:p0008:c013 · PDF 8**

• Information system is used to speak about the ICT required by a given enterprise to support its operations

**G242:p0008:c014 · PDF 8**

• Digital is also used as a more common term

</details>

## 本文件使用的约定

原文：Conventions Used in this Document。PDF 物理页：[9]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本文件使用以下约定，帮助识别重要信息并避免对意图产生误解：

- 省略号（…）：表示延续，例如未列完的示例列表或前文的延续。
- 粗体：突出特定术语。
- 斜体：表示强调，也可指向其他外部文件。

此外，“注”框突出有用或有趣的信息；带阴影的“示例cc-nn”框提供与正文有关的例子。

## 关于作者

原文：About the Authors。PDF 物理页：[11]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**The Shift Project™ Lean-ICT 工作组（Lean-ICT Work Group）**

本文档是 The Open Group 国际标准在数字可持续性这一具体主题上的贡献。作者认为它是 The Shift Project 报告《Lean-ICT：迈向数字节制》（Lean-ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety）[Shift Project, 2019] 的自然演进。该报告包含一章“引导组织走向可持续信息系统”，讨论如何利用系统化的企业架构方法来应对长期能源转型，并说明这需要在组织 ICT 部门内采取短期行动，进而提出了一种交付这些行动的转型方法。The Shift Project 是一家法国智库，成立于 2008 年，倡导向后碳经济转型。作为一家以科学客观性服务公共利益的非营利组织，它致力于为欧洲能源转型的讨论提供信息并施加影响。其工作可视为《增长的极限》[Limits to Growth] 的延续。2018 年，The Shift Project 组建了一个由 ICT 专业人士组成的工作组，并发布了首份报告《Lean-ICT：迈向数字节制》，旨在将数字转型的条件正式化，使其成为实现 2015 年《巴黎协定》[COP21] 气候变化目标的手段；该报告初版的主要作者为 Arnaud Gueguen、Dayan Brunie、Xavier Verne、Axel Hurgon、Maureen Delaloi、Sylvain Baudoin、François Maitre、Hugues Ferreboeuf、Jean-Baptiste Piccirillo 和 Céline Lescop。

**AXA 数字可持续性社区（AXA Digital Sustainability Community）**

在 AXA 赞助 The Shift Project 2020 年 10 月报告之后，本文档依托 AXA 数字可持续性团队的贡献编写而成。AXA 于 2020 年初启动数字可持续性项目群，并于 2021 年在其主要实体（法国、德国、比利时、英国和爱尔兰、日本、美国、香港、西班牙、瑞士和意大利）推广实施。本文档部分受到 AXA 数字可持续性项目群经验的启发。作为全球最大的保险公司之一，AXA 的宗旨是“保护重要之事，助力人类进步”（www.axa.com）。

**Céline Lescop——作者**

Céline 拥有计算机科学硕士学位（1996 年，www.ensiie.fr）。1999 年至 2012 年，她在制药行业担任数据项目负责人（Galderma 和 Astrazeneca）；2012 年至 2015 年，她在 Arismore（The Open Group，法国）担任架构师兼 TOGAF® 培训师；自 2016 年起在 AXA 担任企业架构师，领导数据架构实践，并自 2020 年起领导 AXA 数字可持续性项目群。Céline 自 2019 年起参加 The Shift Project Lean-ICT 工作组。

**Jean-Baptiste Piccirillo——作者**

Jean-Baptiste 拥有计算机科学硕士学位（2010 年，www.ensiie.fr）。他于 2015 年之前在 Arismore（The Open Group，法国）担任架构师，2015 年至 2018 年在 AXA 担任数据科学家，目前是 Rhapsodies Conseil（www.rhapsodiesconseil.fr）的数据架构、数据管理与数字可持续性顾问，为 AXA 提供支持。Jean-Baptiste 自 2020 年起参加 The Shift Project Lean-ICT 工作组。

原文措辞说明：Jean-Baptiste履历中的“until 2015”连续重复；中文合并表达同一日期，英文源单元保留。

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0011:c000 · PDF 11**

About the Authors

**G242:p0011:c001 · PDF 11**

The Shift Project™ Lean-ICT Work Group

**G242:p0011:c002 · PDF 11**

This document is a contribution to the international standards of The Open Group on the specific topic of Digital sustainability. The authors consider it a natural evolution of the Shift Project report: Lean- ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety [Shift Project, 2019]. This report contains a chapter named “Guiding Organizations Towards a Sustainable Information System”, which discusses how the systemic Enterprise Architecture approach can be leveraged to deal with long-term energy transition. It demonstrates that this needs short-term actions within an organization’s ICT department and proposes a transformation approach to deliver these. The Shift Project is a French think tank, created in 2008, which advocates the shift to a post-carbon economy. As a non-profit organization committed to serving general interest through scientific objectivity, it is dedicated to informing and influencing the debate on energy transition in Europe. Its work can be considered as a continuity of “The Limits to Growth” [Limits to Growth]. In 2018, The Shift Project set up a Work Group of ICT professionals and published a first report “Lean-ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety” that aimed at formalizing the conditions of a digital transition as an enabler to reach the 2015 Paris Agreement [COP21] objectives on climate change; the main authors of the initial version of which are Arnaud Gueguen, Dayan Brunie, Xavier Verne, Axel Hurgon, Maureen Delaloi, Sylvain Baudoin, François Maitre, Hugues Ferreboeuf, Jean-Baptiste Piccirillo, and Céline Lescop.

**G242:p0011:c003 · PDF 11**

AXA® Digital Sustainability Community

**G242:p0011:c004 · PDF 11**

As a follow-up of the sponsoring of The Shift Project’s October 2020 report by AXA, this document was authored relying on contributions from the AXA Digital Sustainability team. AXA started a Digital Sustainability program at the beginning 2020 and rolled it out across its main entities: France, Germany, Belgium, United Kingdom and Ireland, Japan, US, Hong Kong, Spain, Switzerland, and Italy in

**G242:p0011:c005 · PDF 11**

2021. This document was partially inspired by the experience of the AXA Digital Sustainability program. As one of the largest global insurers, the purpose of AXA is to act for human progress by protecting what matters (www.axa.com).

**G242:p0011:c006 · PDF 11**

Céline Lescop – Author

**G242:p0011:c007 · PDF 11**

Céline holds a Master’s Degree (1996) in Computer Science (www.ensiie.fr). She was a Data Project Leader in the pharmaceutical industry from 1999 until 2012 (Galderma and Astrazeneca), an Architect and TOGAF® trainer from 2012 to 2015 at Arismore (The Open Group, France), and an Enterprise Architect at AXA® leading the Data Architecture practice since 2016 and AXA Digital Sustainability program since 2020. Céline has participated in The Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group since 2019.

**G242:p0011:c008 · PDF 11**

Jean-Baptiste Piccirillo – Author

**G242:p0011:c009 · PDF 11**

Jean-Baptiste holds a Master’s degree (2010) in Computer Science (www.ensiie.fr). He was an Architect until 2015 for Arismore (The Open Group, France) until 2015, a Data Scientist for AXA® from 2015 to 2018, and he is currently a Data Architecture, Data Management, and digital sustainability Consultant for Rhapsodies Conseil (www.rhapsodiesconseil.fr) supporting AXA. Jean-Baptiste has taken part in The Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group since 2020.

</details>

### 语义规则与适用边界

- SEC 2022条目描述提议中的气候披露规则。
  - 适用边界：提案状态和适用时间必须保留，不据此断言当前法律义务。
  - PDF 17：for Investors, US Securities & Exchange Commission (SEC), March 2022; refer to: www.sec.gov/news/press-release/2022-46 (In addition, a registrant would be required to disclose GHG emissions from upstream and downstream activities in its value chain (Scope 3), if material or if the registrant has set a GHG emissions target or goal that includes Scope 3 emissions.)

## 引言：ICT 环境影响的系统性视角

原文：1. Introduction: A Systemic Point of View on the Environmental Impact of ICT。PDF 物理页：[19, 20]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

2015 年《巴黎协定》[COP21] 要求签署国——从工业化国家到低收入国家（198 个国家中的 195 个，代表超过 98% 的人为排放）——共同努力遏制温室气体（GHG）排放。该协定在团结全球应对气候变化的努力方面是一个历史性时刻；此后承诺有所变化，一些国家退回了其义务。尽管有这些变化，《巴黎协定》仍是国际合作的框架，因而影响着包括信息与通信技术（ICT）在内的所有经济部门的全球气候政策。

事实上，化石燃料占全球能源消费的 80% [Energy Mix]，是人为（即由人类活动造成的）温室气体排放的主要来源。全球能源消费的任何增长，都会妨碍实现这一历史性的重大挑战：减缓全球变暖。2021 年，政府间气候变化专门委员会（IPCC）第六次评估报告¹再次强调了这一承诺的重要性。

数字技术已成为经济与社会发展的必需。数字转型被视为国家和企业的关键——数字对象与界面已逐渐融入我们生活的每个方面。数字转型也常被视为帮助许多其他部门降低能耗的关键工具，以及使污染性活动去物质化的“显而易见”的解决方案（“IT for Green”，运用信息技术促进其他领域绿色化）。

更快、更大、更智能：ICT 带来的指数级增长伴随着对进步的期望。它使人难以重新审视这种扩张计划、考虑更务实的做法。然而，商业思维正在发生转变，并可能扩大——因为预期的进步无法与印刷术的发明、1840 年代铁路与动力机械的到来、1920 年代电力的普及，或抗生素、疫苗等健康创新所引发的革命相提并论。

ICT 的影响并非新发现，其第一批杠杆在 30 多年前就开始实施。1992 年，美国环境保护署推出 Energy Star（能源之星）——一个自愿性标签计划，旨在促进和评估显示器、温控设备及其他技术的能效。其结果是休眠模式在消费电子产品中被广泛采用 [Wikipedia, Green Computing]。

随着对环境影响的认知不断深入，可以看出：数字技术在某些情况下可以支持《巴黎协定》所需的转型，但它带来的好处往往分散且难以度量，而其当前的环境影响是真实、巨大且在增长的 [Lancaster, 2021]。绿色转型与数字转型常常相互矛盾，因为数字技术具有可观的环境足迹，与绿色转型的目标相悖。使 ICT 朝着与《巴黎协定》兼容的方向发展，需要高度关注其环境影响，并转向可持续的 ICT 市场。这还要求社会对业务用例做出选择，聚焦于食品生产、教育、健康、气候与生物多样性演变监测等关键活动。ICT 专业人员将发挥作用：提供关于环境影响的准确科学数据以引导公民，并为负责监管的政府、非政府组织（NGO）以及愿意减少环境影响的公民提供指导。

在一些司法辖区和组织中，ICT 部门现在已被授权转向可持续 ICT。在尚未强制的地方，ICT 部门仍有必要考虑其活动的环境影响，以及社会对其接受度的演变与趋势。

为说明为何要重视构建“环境可持续的信息系统”，以下导言各节概述 ICT 的环境影响。关于如何将其最小化的指导见 1.1 节。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- IT for Green指数字技术促进其他行业减少资源或能耗。
  - 适用边界：与降低ICT自身足迹分开核算；潜在避免排放不自动抵减组织实际排放。
  - PDF 19：Digital technologies have become essential for economic and social development. The digital transition is perceived as critical for countries and companies with digital objects and interfaces which have gradually become part of every aspect of our lives. The digital transition is also often considered as a key tool to help reduce energy consumption in many other sectors and a “no-brainer” solution to dematerialize polluting activities (“IT for Green”).

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0019:c000 · PDF 19**

1. Introduction: A Systemic Point of View on the

**G242:p0019:c001 · PDF 19**

Environmental Impact of ICT

**G242:p0019:c002 · PDF 19**

The 2015 Paris Agreement [COP21] committed signatories, from industrialized to low-income (195 countries out of 198, representing over 98% of anthropogenic emissions), to working together to curb Greenhouse Gas (GHG) emissions. While the agreement marked a historic moment in uniting global efforts to combat climate change, commitments have since shifted, with some nations stepping back from their obligations. Despite these changes, the Paris Agreement remains a framework for international cooperation. As such, it influences global climate policies across all economic sectors, including Information and Communication Technologies (ICT).

**G242:p0019:c003 · PDF 19**

Indeed, fossil fuels represent 80% of worldwide energy consumption [Energy Mix]. They are the main sources of anthropogenic, meaning caused by humans, GHG emissions. Any increase in global energy consumption inhibits achievement of this historic and vital challenge: mitigating global warming. The importance of this commitment has recently been reinforced in 2021 by the Intergovernmental Panel

**G242:p0019:c004 · PDF 19**

[1]

**G242:p0019:c005 · PDF 19**

on Climate Change (IPCC) Sixth Assessment Report.

**G242:p0019:c006 · PDF 19**

Digital technologies have become essential for economic and social development. The digital transition is perceived as critical for countries and companies with digital objects and interfaces which have gradually become part of every aspect of our lives. The digital transition is also often considered as a key tool to help reduce energy consumption in many other sectors and a “no-brainer” solution to dematerialize polluting activities (“IT for Green”).

**G242:p0019:c007 · PDF 19**

Faster, bigger, smarter: the exponential growth that ICT have generated came with hope for progress. It makes it difficult to reconsider this expansion plan and consider a more practical approach. Nevertheless, a shift in business mindset emerges and may expand as the expected progress is not comparable with the revolution induced by the invention of printing, the arrival of railways and power machinery in the 1840s, the expansion of electricity in the 1920s, or health innovations like antibiotics or vaccines.

**G242:p0019:c008 · PDF 19**

The impact of ICT is not a new discovery, and its first levers started to be implemented over 30 years ago. In 1992, the U.S. Environmental Protection Agency launched Energy Star, a voluntary labeling program designed to promote and evaluate the energy efficiency in monitors, climate control equipment, and other technologies. This resulted in the widespread adoption of sleep mode among consumer electronics [Wikipedia, Green Computing].

**G242:p0019:c009 · PDF 19**

As the understanding of environmental impact progresses, it appears that Digital can, in some cases, support the transition required by the 2015 Paris Agreement, but the advantages it brings are often diffuse and difficult to measure, while its current environmental impact is real, huge, and growing [Lancaster, 2021]. Both the green and the digital transitions often contradict each other, as digital technologies have substantial environmental footprints that go against the targets of the green transition. Developing ICT in a direction compatible with the 2015 Paris Agreement requires a strong focus on its environmental impact and a shift to a sustainable ICT market. It will also require a selection by the society of the business use cases that focus on vital activities such as food production, education, health, and tracking of climate, biodiversity evolution, etc. ICT professionals will have a role

**G242:p0020:c000 · PDF 20**

to play in providing the accurate scientific data about environmental impact to guide citizens, and in providing guidance to governments responsible for regulation, Non-Governmental Organizations (NGOs) and citizens willing to reduce environmental impacts.

**G242:p0020:c001 · PDF 20**

In some jurisdictions and organizations, ICT departments now have a mandate to shift towards sustainable ICT. Where this is not yet mandated, it is still necessary for ICT departments to consider the environmental impacts of their activities as well as the evolution and trends in their societal acceptance.

**G242:p0020:c002 · PDF 20**

To explain why it is important to focus on building “Environmentally Sustainable Information Systems”, the following introductory sections provide an overview of ICT environmental impact. Guidance on how to minimize it is provided in Section 1.1.

</details>

<a id="1.1"></a>
## ICT 增长与温室气体排放

原文：1.1. ICT Growth and GHG Emissions。PDF 物理页：[20, 21, 22, 23]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

2007 年，Gartner® 研究副总裁 Simon Mingay 发表研究 [Gartner] 指出：“全球 ICT 行业占全球二氧化碳（CO2）排放的 2%——与航空业相当——Gartner 警告这是不可持续的。”

2020 年，ICT 占全球温室气体排放的 3.5%，且每年增长 6%，已超过航空业 [Shift Project, 2019]。

没有其他部门增长如此之快，而这种增长与《巴黎协定》要求的下降不相容。ICT 参与者应调整其方法，把可持续性作为所建系统的一种质量，并有勇气重塑 ICT 业务，使其可持续。

**我们对数字去物质化的理解有误**

乍看之下，数字技术似乎使业务去物质化：通过减少人员流动、纸张使用以及获取服务所需的中间环节，组织变得更轻、更灵活。但另一方面，由此产生的信息流被物质化了——它们被存储进服务器、通过网络设备传输、在终端用户屏幕上显示。所有这些活动都耗能、使用稀有金属，并在其生命周期的每一步产生污染：从设备制造、使用阶段耗电，到报废管理。

**我们的信息系统过于庞大和复杂**

在数十年的内部流程数字化、绩效指标计算与业务增长支撑的驱动下，计算系统如今被用于每一个组织流程。这种数字化的效率以及随之而来的创新数量增长，几乎没有为合理化留下空间。今天，几乎所有的组织实体都因不同架构、应用、数据与技术的部分受控堆叠而承受着技术臃肿（technological bloat）之苦。

这些被设想以“敏捷”方式支撑业务发展的战略性 ICT 系统，多年来已变得日益超配，限制了其实现最初目标的能力。直到现在，ICT 格局的合理化常常被推迟——尽管某些行业已经开始——原因在于 2002 年互联网泡沫破裂与 2008 年金融危机。然而，整个 ICT 系统的转型昂贵、有风险且耗时。ICT 合理化计划往往因需满足客户和各类业务方（被期望带来短期业务价值）的期望而被降级。ICT 部门很快将需要实施新战略来应对这些问题，同时避免过于突然或剧烈的转变。与业务方进行解释、沟通并确定优先级，将是成功的关键。

**……而且这在规模上显而易见！**

Shift Project 在其 2019 年 Lean-ICT 报告 [Shift Project, 2019] 中描述了数字技术在宏观层面的环境影响。今天，随着数据使用量呈现巨大增长，所谓大数据所驱动的流量与数据消费引发了重大疑问。

根据 Exploding Topics 主持的一项研究 [Exploding Topics]：“存储数据的规模将在 2025 年达到 181 ZB（泽字节），即比 2018 年存储量多 5.5 倍”；见图 1-1。《世界的数字化——从边缘到核心》[IDC, 2018] 是更早的参考，给出了类似的数量级。由于每增加一个字节都需要能量来存储，这会产生额外的碳排放。鉴于当今的 CO2 减排目标，ICT 专业人员必须通过清理现有数据存量、并把新增存储能力仅用于最有用的数据用例，来努力稳定这一趋势。

![图 1-1：2010–2025 年全球年度产生的数据量 [Exploding Topics]。（中文重绘）](../sources/retained/togaf-standard-10th-zh/figs/zh/g242_fig1-1_zh.svg)

> **注**：一个泽字节（zettabyte）等于 10²¹ 字节，足以存储 1,200 万段 4K 视频。（参见：en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Byte#Multiple-byte_units）

**系统思维与 ICT 的演进**

产生 ICT 增长与温室气体排放的底层动力是复杂的，系统思维（Systems Thinking）[Systems Thinking] 有助于表征 ICT 系统的结构。

图 1-2 展示了这种正在起作用的增强型反馈回路（reinforcing feedback loop）。

![图 1-2：表征 ICT 行业动态的因果回路图 [The Shift Project，Lean-ICT 工作组使用 Vensim® 软件绘制]。（中文重绘）](../sources/retained/togaf-standard-10th-zh/figs/zh/g242_fig1-2_zh.svg)

该图还突出了以下几点：

- **数字使用与基础设施之间的增强型反馈回路 R1**：业务对使用的要求越多，部署的基础设施就越多；而基础设施一旦部署，运营方对盈利的追求就会导致对使用的推广，从而进一步增加使用。
- **R1 本应由 B1 平衡**：基础设施越多，成本越高；成本越高，使用越少。但在 ICT 的情形中，基础设施以及数字服务所服务的业务两者性能的提升，都会降低成本并使其有利可图。
- **因此 R1 未被平衡**，产生了在 ICT 中观察到的指数级动态，以及生产和使数字设备所需的终端能源消费每年 6.2% 的增长 [Shift Project, 2021]。
- **R2 表明**，性能提升也为 ICT 行业带来收入，使其能够投资于性能改进，这又强化了当前不稳定的动态。

系统分析得出以下结论：

- 那些提高燃烧化石燃料、消耗金属的活动的性能的数字使用，应被减少，并转向生物圈再生型（biosphere regenerative）业务。
- 无约束地优化 ICT 服务不会减少污染，因为它会产生反弹效应：服务变得更便宜、更易获取，需求随之增长。应考虑环境紧迫性，有辨别力地发展数字使用，以遏制甚至逆转其增长。
- 稳定数字使用的增长需要在不同层面采取有力行动——从监管机构，到运营商，再到使用数字服务的组织。这还要求社会愿望的转变，走向生物圈再生式创新。

ICT 行业当前的动态在污染方面是重大风险，对数字能力本身也是如此。

以不稳定方式增长的系统，在其所依赖的某种资源变得稀缺时会遇到临界点（tipping point）；它们要么转向另一个稳定状态，要么崩溃。数字环境正在接近极限的症状（2021、2022 年）越来越频繁地出现在新闻中，或表现为不可持续的电力需求增长，或表现为计算机芯片短缺。社会面临的挑战是组织数字系统的稳定与下降，以便能够保障最重要、最关键的用例。系统思维告诉我们，未能做好准备将使我们意外地面对极限，并可能造成危险的混乱。

图1-1：2010–2025年全球年度产生的数据量 [Exploding Topics]。 图1-2：表征ICT行业动态的因果回路图；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组使用Vensim软件绘制。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 2025年181 ZB是指南引用的历史预测。
  - 适用边界：正文stored与图注generated annually口径不同；存量、年度流量不得混用为输入。
  - PDF 21：According to a study led by Exploding Topics [Exploding Topics]: “the volume of stored data will reach 181 ZB (zettabytes) in 2025; i.e. 5.5 times more than that what was stored in 2018”; see Figure 1-1. The Digitization of the World – From Edge to Core [IDC, 2018] is an older reference, providing a similar order of magnitude. As each additional byte requires energy to be stored, this generates additional carbon emissions. Given today’s goal of CO2 emissions reduction, ICT professionals must try to stabilize this trend by cleansing current data stocks and dedicating new storage capabilities to only the most useful data use-cases only.
- 泽字节是10²¹字节，视频数量只是说明规模的例子。
  - 适用边界：未给时长、压缩或编码，不能建立每4K视频固定字节换算。
  - PDF 22：One zettabyte is equal to 1021 bytes, and is sufficient to store 12 million 4K videos.
- 效率提升可能因使用规模扩张产生反弹效应。
  - 适用边界：不能以单次请求能耗下降证明总量下降；应同时观察单位强度和总活动量。
  - PDF 23：• Optimization of ICT services without constraints will not allow reduction of pollution as it creates a rebound effect through which demand grows as the service becomes cheaper and more easily accessible

**G242:p0020:c003 · PDF 20**

1.1. ICT Growth and GHG Emissions

**G242:p0020:c004 · PDF 20**

In 2007, Simon Mingay, Research Vice-President at Gartner®, published a study [Gartner] observing that: “The ICT industry worldwide accounts for 2pc of global carbon dioxide (CO2) emissions – equal to that of the aviation industry – which analyst firm Gartner warns is unsustainable.”

**G242:p0020:c005 · PDF 20**

In 2020, ICT was responsible for 3.5% of worldwide GHG emissions, increasing by 6% per year. It has already overtaken the aviation industry [Shift Project, 2019].

**G242:p0020:c006 · PDF 20**

No other sector is increasing as fast, and this growth is incompatible with the decrease required by the 2015 Paris Agreement. ICT actors should adapt their approach, integrate sustainability as a quality of the systems they build, and be courageous in reinventing the ICT business to make it sustainable.

**G242:p0020:c007 · PDF 20**

We have the wrong idea about digital dematerialization

**G242:p0020:c008 · PDF 20**

At first glance, digital technology seems to dematerialize the business. By reducing the physical flows of people, the use of paper, and the number of proxies required to access a service, organizations are made lighter, making them more flexible. But on the other hand, the resulting information flows are materialized, stored into servers, transported through network equipment, and displayed on end-user screens. All these activities are energy-intensive, use rare metals, and generate pollution at each step of their lifecycle, from equipment manufacture, power consumption during usage, to end-of-life management.

**G242:p0020:c009 · PDF 20**

Our information systems are too big and complex

**G242:p0020:c010 · PDF 20**

Driven by decades of internal process digitalization, performance indicators calculation, and business growth support, computing systems are now used in every organizational process. The efficiency of this digitalization and the related growing number of innovations has left little room for rationalization. Today, almost all organizational entities suffer from technological bloat due to a partially controlled stacking of different architectures, applications, data, and technologies.

**G242:p0020:c011 · PDF 20**

These strategic ICT systems, envisioned to support business development in an “Agile” way, have grown increasingly oversized over the years, limiting their ability to meet their initial purpose. Until

**G242:p0021:c000 · PDF 21**

now, rationalization of the ICT landscape has been often deferred, even if started in some sectors, because of the bursting of the dot-com bubble in 2002 and the financial crisis in 2008. However, the transformation of an entire ICT system is expensive, risky, and time-consuming. ICT rationalization programs are often deprioritized to meet the expectations of customers and different business actors that are expected to bring short term business value. ICT departments will soon need to implement a new strategy that addresses these issues while avoiding a transition that is too sudden or abrupt. Explanation, communication, and prioritization with the business actors will be key to success.

**G242:p0021:c001 · PDF 21**

… and it shows on the scale!

**G242:p0021:c002 · PDF 21**

In its 2019 Lean-ICT Report [Shift Project, 2019], the Shift Project describes the environmental impact of digital technology at macro level. Today, with data usage showing tremendous growth, the traffic and consumption of data driven by so-called Big Data raises substantial questions.

**G242:p0021:c003 · PDF 21**

According to a study led by Exploding Topics [Exploding Topics]: “the volume of stored data will reach 181 ZB (zettabytes) in 2025; i.e. 5.5 times more than that what was stored in 2018”; see Figure 1-1. The Digitization of the World – From Edge to Core [IDC, 2018] is an older reference, providing a similar order of magnitude. As each additional byte requires energy to be stored, this generates additional carbon emissions. Given today’s goal of CO2 emissions reduction, ICT professionals must try to stabilize this trend by cleansing current data stocks and dedicating new storage capabilities to only the most useful data use-cases only.

**G242:p0021:c004 · PDF 21**

Figure 1-1: Global Data Generated Annually 2010-2025 [Exploding Topics]

**G242:p0022:c000 · PDF 22**

One zettabyte is equal to 1021 bytes, and is sufficient to store 12 million 4K videos.

**G242:p0022:c001 · PDF 22**

(Refer to: en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Byte#Multiple-byte_units)

**G242:p0022:c002 · PDF 22**

Systems Thinking and ICT Evolution

**G242:p0022:c003 · PDF 22**

The underlying dynamics that generate the ICT growth and GHG emissions are complex and Systems Thinking [Systems Thinking] can help to represent the ICT system structure.

**G242:p0022:c004 · PDF 22**

Figure 1-2 is an illustration of this reinforcing feedback loop at work.

**G242:p0022:c005 · PDF 22**

Figure 1-2: Causal Loop Diagram Representing the Dynamic of the ICT Sector [The Shift Project, from the Work

**G242:p0022:c006 · PDF 22**

Group Leveraging Vensim® Software]

**G242:p0022:c007 · PDF 22**

[2]

**G242:p0023:c000 · PDF 23**

This diagram also highlights:

**G242:p0023:c001 · PDF 23**

• A reinforcing feedback loop R1 between digital usages and infrastructure

**G242:p0023:c002 · PDF 23**

The more usage is required from the business, the more infrastructure is deployed; and when the infrastructure is deployed, the operator’s search for profitability leads to usage promotion and therefore to its increase.

**G242:p0023:c003 · PDF 23**

• R1 should be balanced by B1: the more infrastructure, the higher the cost, and the higher the cost, the less the usage

**G242:p0023:c004 · PDF 23**

But in the case of ICT, the performance improvement of both the infrastructure and the business that Digital Services serve decrease the cost and makes it profitable.

**G242:p0023:c005 · PDF 23**

• Consequently, R1 is not balanced, generating the exponential dynamics observed in ICT and the yearly 6.2% growth [Shift Project, 2021] in final energy consumption required to produce and use digital equipment

**G242:p0023:c006 · PDF 23**

• R2 shows that increased performance also generates revenues for the ICT industry allowing investments in performance improvement, which reinforce the current unstable dynamics

**G242:p0023:c007 · PDF 23**

The systemic analysis leads to the following conclusions:

**G242:p0023:c008 · PDF 23**

• Digital usages that are increasing the performance of activities burning fossil fuels and consuming metals should be reduced and shifted toward biosphere regenerative businesses

**G242:p0023:c009 · PDF 23**

• Optimization of ICT services without constraints will not allow reduction of pollution as it creates a rebound effect through which demand grows as the service becomes cheaper and more easily accessible

**G242:p0023:c010 · PDF 23**

Digital usages should be developed with discernment to contain or even reverse their increase considering the environmental urgency.

**G242:p0023:c011 · PDF 23**

• Stabilizing the growth of digital usages requires strong actions at different levels, from regulators to operators and organizations using digital services

**G242:p0023:c012 · PDF 23**

It also requires a change in society’s aspiration to progress towards biosphere regenerative innovation.

**G242:p0023:c013 · PDF 23**

The current dynamic of the ICT sector is a major risk in terms of pollution, but also for the digital capability itself.

**G242:p0023:c014 · PDF 23**

Systems that grow in an unstable way meet tipping points when a resource on which they rely on becomes scarce. They then either shift towards another stable state or they collapse. Symptoms that the digital environment is approaching limits appear ever more often in the news (2021, 2022), either as the result of unsustainable growing electricity demands or shortages of computer chips. The challenge for society is to organize the stabilization and decrease of digital systems to be able to guarantee the most important and vital use cases. Systems Thinking tells us that failure to prepare will lead us to face limits by surprise and may create dangerous chaos.

</details>

## ICT 的金属消耗

原文：1.2. ICT Consumption of Metals。PDF 物理页：[24]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

绿色与数字世界所需的技术使用了元素周期表中的大多数元素。其中一些元素——钴、锂、铜、镍、铟、铂族金属、多晶硅和稀土——是稀缺的，或可能因需求上升、资源枯竭或冲突而变得稀缺。金属是任何硬件的原材料，也是通过太阳能电池板与风电场实现向可再生电力能源转型所必需的。循环经济可以降低开采此类金属的需求，但没有一种金属实现100%回收，有些甚至尚未得到回收 [UNEP, 2011]。ICT 专业人员对此主题的知识应得到提升，方法之一是发展基于资源消耗比较现有解决方案的方法。本文档不对此详述，但有若干参考文献可供查阅：Green European Foundation [GEF, 2021]、国际能源署 [IEA, 2021]、联合国贸易和发展会议 [UNCTAD]。

> **脚注**：金属的回收率远低于再利用潜力。在研究的 60 种金属中，不到三分之一的回收率超过50%，而其中许多对清洁技术至关重要，例如混合动力汽车电池或风力涡轮机中的磁体。[UNEP 2011]


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 金属实际回收率与技术上的可回收性不同。
  - 适用边界：原文none are 100% recycled不可译成没有任何金属可100%回收。
  - PDF 24：The technologies required for a green and digital world use most of the elements in the periodic table. Some of these elements – Cobalt, Lithium, Copper, Nickel, Indium, Platinum-group metals, Polysilicon, and rare earths – are scarce or may become so due to rising demand, depletion, or conflict. Metals are the raw materials of any hardware. They are also required for energy transition towards renewable electricity via solar panels and wind farms. The creation of a circular economy may lower the need to extract such metals but none of them are 100% recycled and, for some, recycling is impossible [UNEP, 2011].[3] ICT professionals’ knowledge of this topic should be increased, relying on the development of approaches to compare existing solutions based on their consumption of resources. It will not be detailed in this document, but several references are available and should be consulted: Green European Foundation [GEF, 2021], International Energy Agency [IEA, 2021], United Nations Conference on Trade and Development [UNCTAD].

**G242:p0024:c000 · PDF 24**

1.2. ICT Consumption of Metals

**G242:p0024:c001 · PDF 24**

The technologies required for a green and digital world use most of the elements in the periodic table. Some of these elements – Cobalt, Lithium, Copper, Nickel, Indium, Platinum-group metals, Polysilicon, and rare earths – are scarce or may become so due to rising demand, depletion, or conflict. Metals are the raw materials of any hardware. They are also required for energy transition towards renewable electricity via solar panels and wind farms. The creation of a circular economy may lower the need to extract such metals but none of them are 100% recycled and, for some, recycling is impossible [UNEP, 2011].[3] ICT professionals’ knowledge of this topic should be increased, relying on the development of approaches to compare existing solutions based on their consumption of resources. It will not be detailed in this document, but several references are available and should be consulted: Green European Foundation [GEF, 2021], International Energy Agency [IEA, 2021], United Nations Conference on Trade and Development [UNCTAD].

## 电子废弃物

原文：1.3. Electronic Waste。PDF 物理页：[24]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

根据联合国《2020 年全球电子废弃物监测》[E-Waste, 2020]，2019 年全球产生了创纪录的 5,360 万公吨（MT）电子废弃物（e-waste，即带电池或插头的废弃产品），五年内增长 21%。这份新报告还预测，到 2030 年全球电子废弃物将达到 74 MT（原文如此；上文为53.6 million Metric Tonnes，附录又将MT定义为公吨；不能据此直接确定此处数量级），即在 16 年内几乎翻倍。

Statista 2023 年关于电子废弃物的研究证实了这一上升趋势，并预测：“2010 年至 2019 年间，电子废弃物产生量增长约 60%，且这一增长没有放缓迹象。到 2030 年，年电子废弃物产生量预计将达到惊人的 7,500 万公吨。”[E-Waste Statista]

这使电子废弃物成为世界上增长最快的家庭废弃物流，主要驱动因素是电气电子设备消费率上升、生命周期短以及维修选择有限。2019 年，仅有 **17.4%** 的电子废弃物被收集并回收，且回收效率很低。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 电子废弃物预测需保留来源年、预测年及原文单位。
  - 适用边界：53.6 million Metric Tonnes与74 MT的数量级语义未统一，生成计算前需澄清。
  - PDF 24：A record 53.6 million Metric Tonnes (MT) of electronic waste (e-waste) – discarded products with a battery or plug – were generated worldwide in 2019, up 21% in just five years, according to the UN’s Global E-waste Monitor 2020 [E-Waste, 2020]. This new report also predicts that global e-waste – discarded products with a battery or plug – will reach 74 MT by 2030, representing almost a doubling of e-waste in just 16 years.

**G242:p0024:c002 · PDF 24**

1.3. Electronic Waste

**G242:p0024:c003 · PDF 24**

A record 53.6 million Metric Tonnes (MT) of electronic waste (e-waste) – discarded products with a battery or plug – were generated worldwide in 2019, up 21% in just five years, according to the UN’s Global E-waste Monitor 2020 [E-Waste, 2020]. This new report also predicts that global e-waste – discarded products with a battery or plug – will reach 74 MT by 2030, representing almost a doubling of e-waste in just 16 years.

**G242:p0024:c004 · PDF 24**

The 2023 Statista study on e-waste confirms this increasing trend forecasting that: “Between 2010 and 2019, e-waste generation increased by roughly 60 percent, and this growth shows no signs of slowing down. By 2030, annual e-waste production is on track to reach a staggering 75 million metric tons.” [E- Waste Statista].

**G242:p0024:c005 · PDF 24**

This makes e-waste the world’s fastest-growing domestic waste stream, fueled mainly by higher consumption rates of electric and electronic equipment, short lifecycles, and limited options for repair. In 2019, only 17.4% of e-waste was collected and recycled, and recycling efficiency is very low.

</details>

## ICT 的水消耗

原文：1.4. ICT Water Consumption。PDF 物理页：[24, 25]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

此外，ICT 消耗大量水，因为水是半导体与芯片制造的必要组成部分，而芯片每天都用于计算机、手机和汽车。整个工艺过程都使用超纯水，包括去除硅片中的杂质。例如，单片 8 英寸晶圆（约 100 颗芯片的基础）可能需多达 **7,500 升**超纯水。因此，半导体生产商正着力于回收、再利用和减少其运营所需水量 [Intel, 2019]。

> **脚注**：硅谷计算机巨头 Intel 建设其最大水回收设施的计划，在该公司获得 1.2 亿美元免税债券后得以推进 [Intel, 2019]。

> **本章脚注**：
>
> 1. 参见：www.ipcc.ch/。
> 2. Vensim 是用于改进真实系统性能的工业级仿真软件。Vensim 用于开发、分析和打包动态反馈模型；参见：vensim.com/。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 单片8英寸晶圆最多7500升用水是原文制造案例。
  - 适用边界：不能作为全部芯片型号、工艺或当前供应商的固定系数。
  - PDF 24：Additionally, ICT consumes important amounts of water, as it is an essential component for the manufacturing of semiconductors and chips which are used every day in computers, cell phones, and automobiles. Ultrapure water is used throughout the process, including the removal of impurities from silicon wafers. For example, a single 8-inch wafer, which is the foundation for approximately 100 chips, may require up to 7,500 liters of ultrapure water. As a result, semiconductor producers are focusing on ways to recycle, reuse, and reduce the amount of water needed for their operations [Intel,

## ArchiMate® 规范

原文：2.1. The ArchiMate® Specification。PDF 物理页：[26]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

为描述数字可持续性能力，本文档使用 ArchiMate 建模记法 [C226]。

为满足本文档中特定的建模需求，我们选取了 ArchiMate 建模记法元素的一个子集。该子集及其每个概念的定义见图 2-1。

本文档所使用各概念的 ArchiMate 定义如下：

**战略层（Strategy Layer）**

- **能力（Capability）**：能力表示一个主动结构元素（如组织、人员或系统）所拥有的本领。

  能力以通用的、高层的方式表达，通常由组织、人员、流程、信息与技术的组合来实现。例如业务规划、客户管理或资产管理。

**业务层（Business Layer）**

- **业务功能（Business Function）**：业务功能表示基于一组选定标准（通常是所需的业务资源和/或能力）的业务行为集合，它与某一组织紧密对齐，但不一定由该组织显式治理。

图2-1：本文档使用的ArchiMate建模记法元素。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig2-1](../figures/G242/fig2-1.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 能力描述组织、人员或系统拥有的能力，并由人员、流程、信息及工具组合实现。
  - 适用边界：业务功能、组织部门、应用组件与能力不是同一类模型元素。
  - PDF 26：• Capability: A capability represents an ability that an active structure element, such as an organization, person, or system, possesses.
  - PDF 26：Capabilities are expressed in general, high-level terms that are typically realized by a combination of organization, people, processes, information, and technology. For example, business planning, customer management, or asset management.

## 企业架构与 TOGAF® 标准

原文：2.2. Enterprise Architecture and the TOGAF® Standard。PDF 物理页：[26, 27]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

企业架构与 TOGAF® 标准提供了本文档建议使用的框架、工具与技术，以协助组织开发和维护数字可持续性能力。

在 TOGAF® 标准支持下，组织企业架构能力达到较高成熟度，是数字可持续性能力的基础，可提供：

- 覆盖组织全部范围的利益相关者分析；
- 对高层战略与交付物的一致抽象，以支撑规划与分析；
- 识别实现未来状态的清晰路线图；
- 把 ICT 资产与其他资产及其所支撑的业务联系起来的可追溯性；
- 对影响评估、风险/价值分析与组合管理的支持；
- 利益相关者需求之间的关联，确保每个利益相关者群体的需要都得到满足，且每一方都了解适当的语境。

  这些关联对于支撑“节制”（sufficiency）决策、并在 ICT 使能的业务可持续性与 ICT 资产的环境影响之间取得平衡至关重要。

- 具备语境与分析能力，从而以最佳业务价值做出正确的数字可持续性决策，并在整个信息系统格局中交付可持续性。

本文档使用了 TOGAF® 标准其他部分的以下方面：

- **能力**：采用 TOGAF 标准的定义——“IT 部门、IT 专业人员或 IT 系统所拥有的本领”；数字可持续性能力在第 3 章描述。
- **架构治理**：即 TOGAF 标准中所述的监视与指导架构相关工作的实践，用于确保数字可持续性能力的交付及其随时间演进；见 3.9 节。
- **TOGAF ADM**：第 4 章提出利用 TOGAF 架构开发方法（ADM）[C220] 的转型方法。
- **架构原则**：即通用规则与指南，旨在持久且很少修订，用以指导并支撑组织履行其数字可持续性使命的方式；见 3.9.3 节。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ICT资产需追溯到其他资产及所支撑的业务。
  - 适用边界：只给技术资源列表不足以判断应保留或削减哪些使用。
  - PDF 27：• Traceability that links ICT assets to other assets and to the business they support
- 架构需权衡业务通过数字技术获得的可持续收益与ICT自身足迹。
  - 适用边界：不能把两种作用相互抵销后隐去假设、基线和边界。
  - PDF 27：Those links are essentials to support sufficiency decisions and balance between the sustainability enabled in the business by ICT and the environmental impact of ICT assets.
- 本指南用IT部门/专业人员/系统语境描述能力。
  - 适用边界：不应以该处IT限定覆盖通用TOGAF能力概念或排除业务能力。
  - PDF 27：• Capability: The TOGAF Standard definition: “An ability that IT departments, IT professionals, or IT system possesses; digital sustainability” is described in Chapter 3

## The Open Group Open Footprint®（OFP）论坛

原文：2.3. The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum。PDF 物理页：[28]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

组织需要使用有充分依据的数据模型来采集排放相关数据、对这些数据进行计算，并生成可用于决策与监管报告的排放声明与洞察。

这并非易事：从零构建排放数据模型需要大量专业领域知识；而把数据映射到该模型，对复杂、资产密集的组织尤其具有挑战性。

此外，确保排放数据包含正确的来源信息（指向数据源并说明计算方法），对于建立排放声明的信任、满足日趋严格的审计要求是必要的。

The Open Group Open Footprint®（OFP）论坛的目标是产出这样一个足够丰富、可跨行业使用的模型，以及标准 API 和参考开源实现。这项工作还包括支撑从活动数据生成排放估算所需的排放因子与计算方法，例如计算在数据中心使用一台服务器所产生的二氧化碳当量（CO2e）排放，以供监管报告使用。这些标准将有助于缓解范围 1/2/3 温室气体排放相关的数据共享难题。

这些标准在发布后，可与 3.6 节所述的 ICT 足迹度量方法配合使用。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 排放数据必须记录来源及计算方法，以便理解、验证和改进。
  - 适用边界：无来源的单个CO2e数字不满足生成证据条件。
  - PDF 28：In addition, making sure that emissions data includes the right provenance that points to data sources and explains methods of calculation is necessary to build trust in emissions statements and to meet challenging new auditing requirements.
- OFP在原文中是跨行业数据模型、API及参考实现的目标。
  - 适用边界：when available为条件；不宣称当前项目已实现或符合OFP。
  - PDF 28：The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum’s goal is to produce such a model that is rich enough to be used across industries, as well as standard APIs, and a reference open-source implementation. This work also includes supporting the emission factors and calculations that are required to generate emissions estimates from activity data, such as calculating the Carbon Dioxide Equivalent (CO2e) emissions generated when using a server in a data center that can then be used in regulatory reporting. These standards will help to ease the data sharing challenges related to Scopes 1/2/3 GHG emissions.

### 原文内容单元

**G242:p0028:c000 · PDF 28**

2.3. The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum

**G242:p0028:c001 · PDF 28**

There is a need for organizations to use well-founded data models for capturing emissions-related data, performing calculations on this data, and producing emission statements and insights that can be used for decision-making and regulatory reporting.

**G242:p0028:c002 · PDF 28**

This is a non-trivial exercise as producing an emissions data model from scratch requires a lot of specialist domain knowledge, and as mapping data into this model, especially for complex, asset-heavy organizations, is challenging.

**G242:p0028:c003 · PDF 28**

In addition, making sure that emissions data includes the right provenance that points to data sources and explains methods of calculation is necessary to build trust in emissions statements and to meet challenging new auditing requirements.

**G242:p0028:c004 · PDF 28**

The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum’s goal is to produce such a model that is rich enough to be used across industries, as well as standard APIs, and a reference open-source implementation. This work also includes supporting the emission factors and calculations that are required to generate emissions estimates from activity data, such as calculating the Carbon Dioxide Equivalent (CO2e) emissions generated when using a server in a data center that can then be used in regulatory reporting. These standards will help to ease the data sharing challenges related to Scopes 1/2/3 GHG emissions.

**G242:p0028:c005 · PDF 28**

These standards, when available, could be used complementary to the ICT footprint measure approach described in Section 3.6.

</details>

<a id="3"></a>
## 能力

原文：3. Capabilities。PDF 物理页：[29]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 标准采用“能力”这一概念，表示企业、人员或系统能够做到的事情。架构开发方法描述了一种识别、评估和改进能力的系统化途径 [C220]。

在能源与自然资源曾经充裕的世界里，组织不必担心其信息系统的可持续性。因此，对大多数组织而言，数字可持续性仍是一项成熟度很低的能力。此外，当前的市场规则已不再可持续。ICT 确实是一个能效改进很重要的行业（参见摩尔定律，脚注见本节末），但这些改进只是为了支撑更大的消费。这些改进仍主要聚焦于使用阶段的能耗，而忘记了构建日益高效的 ICT 资产所需的巨大环境足迹。

改变这种状况，需要高度重视部署可持续信息系统政策，以推动短期、中期和长期的转型。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 可持续信息系统政策同时牵引短期和长期变化。
  - 适用边界：采购工具或一次培训不能替代长期组织能力。
  - PDF 29：Changing this situation requires a strong focus on deploying a sustainable information system policy to drive short, medium, and long-term transformation.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0029:c000 · PDF 29**

3. Capabilities

**G242:p0029:c001 · PDF 29**

The TOGAF Standard employs the concept of capabilities to indicate the things that an enterprise, person or system can do. The Architecture Development Method describes a systematic approach to identifying, evaluating, and improving capabilities [C220].

**G242:p0029:c002 · PDF 29**

In a world where energy and natural resources used to be abundant, organizations did not have to worry about the sustainability of their information systems. Therefore, digital sustainability is a capability that remains at a very low level of maturity for most organizations. In addition, the current rules of the market are no longer sustainable. ICT is indeed an industry where energy efficiency improvements are important (cf. Moore’s Law[1]), but only to enable greater consumption. These improvements are still mostly focused on energy consumption during the usage phase, forgetting the significant environmental footprint required to build increasingly efficient ICT assets.

**G242:p0029:c003 · PDF 29**

Changing this situation requires a strong focus on deploying a sustainable information system policy to drive short, medium, and long-term transformation.

</details>

<a id="3.1"></a>
## 数字可持续性能力的目的

原文：3.1. The Purpose of the Digital Sustainability Capability。PDF 物理页：[29, 30]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本章描述一个框架，用于实现组织部署可持续信息系统政策的能力。该政策应有助于组织更广泛的企业社会责任（CSR）战略，并成为 ICT 战略的关键支柱之一。

以下效益清单并非穷尽，应结合各企业的具体语境加以调整。这些效益已在启动转型的组织中被观察到，也可以从本文档提出的方法中预期：

- 减少温室气体排放与自然资源（原材料、水等）的使用；
- 提升运营效率、降低信息系统成本——通过更节俭、通过实施限制用量与“可持续设计”（sustainable by design）原则的方法、通过最小化低附加值的数字资源、通过减少终端用户设备等途径实现。

  事实上，减少无用的信息、应用与机器的数量，自然能带来更高效率并降低协作者的脑力负担。迄今为止，拥有财务上的商业论证就足以购买一项新技术或批准一个架构；随着我们向前推进，整体的环境商业论证将必须用基于科学的度量来检验。

- 对未来的监管变化具有更高的适应能力；
- 降低因原材料稀缺而影响信息系统设备供应与成本的风险敞口；
- 为影响运营的严重气候灾害做好准备；
- 对员工具有吸引力——他们得以通过职业活动支持重大的社会事业；
- 向客户、股东和投资者提供与其公民关切相一致的愿景与方法；
- 向股东和财务分析师准确报告温室气体与气候风险数据，符合现有及可预见的透明报告义务与指南。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 收益清单是非穷尽且应按企业语境裁剪的潜在收益。
  - 适用边界：成本下降、韧性提高或人才吸引力需项目证据，不自动标为已实现。
  - PDF 29：The following, non-exhaustive list of benefits should be adapted to the specific context of each enterprise. The following benefits have been observed among organizations that started the transformation and can be expected from the proposed approach:

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0029:c004 · PDF 29**

3.1. The Purpose of the Digital Sustainability Capability

**G242:p0029:c005 · PDF 29**

This chapter describes a framework to implement the capability of an organization to deploy a sustainable information system policy. This policy should contribute to the wider Corporate Social Responsibility (CSR) strategy of the organization and be one of the key pillars of the ICT strategy.

**G242:p0029:c006 · PDF 29**

The following, non-exhaustive list of benefits should be adapted to the specific context of each enterprise. The following benefits have been observed among organizations that started the transformation and can be expected from the proposed approach:

**G242:p0029:c007 · PDF 29**

• Reduced GHG emissions and use of natural resources (raw materials, water, etc.)

**G242:p0029:c008 · PDF 29**

• Improved operational efficiency, reduced information system costs by being more frugal, by implementing approaches limiting volume and principles of sustainable by design, by minimizing low value-added digital resources, and by reducing end-user equipment, etc.

**G242:p0029:c009 · PDF 29**

Indeed, reducing volumes of useless information, applications, and machines can naturally lead to more efficiency and a lower mental workload for collaborators. Until now, having a financial business case was enough to purchase a new technology or to validate an architecture. As we move ahead, the overall environmental business case will have to be checked with science-based metrics.

**G242:p0029:c010 · PDF 29**

• Higher adaptability to future regulatory changes

**G242:p0029:c011 · PDF 29**

• Lower exposure to raw material scarcity impacting information system equipment supply and cost

**G242:p0029:c012 · PDF 29**

• Readiness for serious climatic hazards impacting operations

**G242:p0029:c013 · PDF 29**

• Attractiveness to employees, allowed to support significant societal causes through their professional activities

**G242:p0030:c000 · PDF 30**

• Provision to customers, shareholders, and investors of a vision and an approach aligned with their civic concerns

**G242:p0030:c001 · PDF 30**

• Accurate reporting of GHG and climate risk data to shareholders and financial analysts comply with existing and foreseeable transparent reporting duties and guidelines

</details>

<a id="3.2"></a>
## 如何部署可持续信息系统政策

原文：3.2. How to Deploy a Sustainable Information System Policy。PDF 物理页：[30, 31, 32, 33, 34]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

最大化数字部门对碳中和的贡献，需要最小化能源与自然资源的使用。因此，ICT 部门应在各处减少消耗，而实现这一目标的有效策略是聚焦**价值最大的领域**。

这些“价值最大的领域”必须通过可持续性棱镜仔细界定。事实上，当业务本身不可持续、而 ICT 只是使其更高效时，ICT 的影响在系统层面必然不可持续——因为它助推了不可持续活动的增长。

架构政策需要反映业务的可持续性政策，并需要适当的治理框架确保其被恰当应用。TOGAF 标准 [C220]“基础内容：企业架构能力与治理”提供了此类指导，需按企业文化与运营模式裁剪。

迄今为止，只有少数组织对其 ICT 系统采取环境视角。视企业而定，该视角除其他内容外应包括：

- 外包服务及其间接温室气体排放；
- 数字设备制造过程的影响；
- 数字设备制造与运行所在地电网的环境绩效；
- 支持并演进许可软件的提供方。

由于组织缺乏这种完整视野，关于众多用途的决策与权衡会存在盲点，可能隐藏本已显著、足以影响决策与结果的环境影响。

本文档提出的框架旨在支持组织向可持续 ICT 系统转型。它提出一个参考框架，为多学科专业人员定义共同语言，使 ICT 专业人员与企业业务能就可持续数字举措更好地协作。

部署可持续信息系统政策需要实施新活动或改变现有活动。下述活动对所有组织通用：它们可降低 ICT 系统的环境足迹，并影响 IT 市场走向可持续，因而具有系统性影响。

这套 ICT 系统的“健身计划”（fitness program）旨在作为起点，应对困扰组织的信息肥胖（infobesity）。它可用于对已开展的活动分类，评估其成熟度、优势、改进领域，以及与市场上其他企业的比较情况；应适配各组织的具体语境。

图 3-1 的参考模型描述了在任何组织中建立并部署有效可持续 ICT 系统政策所需的**七个高层业务功能**。本章给出这七项活动的高层描述，后续各节提供更多细节。

**发展可持续数字文化**

对复杂的能源—气候问题以及数字技术环境影响缺乏认知，是我们难以处理这些主题的根本原因之一。这是其他活动取得成功的前提。要走到决定启动向可持续信息系统转型的那一步，高管与员工需要认识到环境危机与转型的紧迫性。随后，计划的执行需要全体员工的支持——他们必须接受教导与培训，从而开始体现一种节制与可持续的文化，使其成为组织长期 DNA 的一部分。

该业务功能见 3.3 节。

**制定并推展可持续信息系统战略**

要变得更可持续，信息系统需要持续的长期计划。这需要企业领导层的支持，以及强有力的发起人，例如运营总监、IT 部门或可持续发展部门。它涉及制定并共享向可持续信息系统转型的动机与目标。这些目标必须成为组织全球可持续战略的一部分，并反映在 ICT 战略与企业架构中。

可持续 ICT 战略一旦确定，就需要建立一个团队，负责通过下述各项活动领导转型。

该业务功能见 3.4 节。

**架构并构建可持续信息系统**

数字工具的创建方法应加以调整，以在其生命周期的所有阶段（从设计到报废）最小化环境影响。这一新框架将为用于创建 ICT 解决方案的敏捷方法注入新的可持续性需求。架构的选择应依据其环境影响。所有架构层都相关：

- **业务单元必须质疑数字解决方案的必要性。** 在确实提供真实业务价值的方案中，它们应考虑从制造到消费的端到端价值链，选择环境效率最高的方案。
- **新系统的实施应与其所替代的旧系统退役及时协调**；必须有计划和终止日期——应尽量减少影响加倍的“双跑期”。
- **应用必须在组织或提供方的服务器上、以及在终端用户终端上都具有资源效率。**
- **存储的数据必须以严谨且经济的方式管理，其存在须以可量化的效用为理由。** 数据项在创建时就应有到期与删除日期，随后可配置自动删除。应利用现有法律政策的指导；对于某些缺失标准的数据，ICT 行业应向政府寻求新标准。
- **技术方案的选择必须考虑其可维修性、耐用性、能效、组件来源（例如是否依托循环经济）以及是否不含危险物质（参见 [European Commission A]）**——总体而言，技术方案必须适用（fit-for-purpose）；参见表 3-7 原则 5。
- **应优先选择或贡献开源与开放标准**，因为它们是应对过时的解决方案，可使设备使用期限显著延长 [Mobbs, 2012]。

该业务功能见 3.5 节。

**从摇篮到坟墓度量信息系统环境影响**

度量是推动组织做出基于科学的决策、以量化方式降低信息系统环境影响的必要手段。评估其实际减排的过程必须覆盖硬件组件的整个生命周期，即“从摇篮到坟墓”。事实上，硬件的制造生产造成了其一半以上的排放。正如 Paul Mobbs 在《可持续 IT 实用指南》中所说：“尽管许多人主要关注用电，数字电子产品的制造对全球环境同样有重大影响。”[Mobbs, 2012] 它消耗自然资源、产生废弃物，并污染土壤和水。对用电优化的追求正导致硬件更新率居高不下 [Shift Project, 2019]。要构建可持续信息系统，必须考虑完整的端到端影响，包括《温室气体核算体系》所定义的碳核算方法中的“范围 3”。关于在组织中实施此类度量，3.6 节提出了一个框架。CO2e 排放的第一数量级可用合理的投入和最少的工具算出，更高的精度或粒度则需要更多投入。

该业务功能见 3.6 节。

**与客户、提供方、伙伴及主管部门互动，共同实现可持续的解决方案与服务**

迄今为止，信息系统的特征是快速过时。它处于该行业当前经济模式的核心。这是不可持续的，应朝着“有计划的可持续”演进。此外，ICT 员工与服务提供方所在国家的能源结构，很少被作为选择服务的参数加以考虑，尽管它可能使环境影响相差数个数量级。

这一转型需要 ICT 行业不同参与方的集体动员——无论是设备制造商、服务提供方，还是使用这些服务的企业。事实上，解决方案与数字服务往往是向第三方购买的。

组织周围的生态系统对其转型具有战略意义。为实现可持续性目标，组织需要创建相关的伙伴生态系统，使其能够与环境战略对齐，并在共同的转型举措上协作。

最后，即使组织并不自行实施其所使用的服务（例如外购服务、云等），它仍对自己产生和处理的数据量、网络使用以及服务供应商的选择负有责任。不能把外部供应商当作“碳垃圾桶”，由组织向其倾倒以缩小自身义务的范围。必须与各类分包商紧密协作，以确保度量的完整性，并落实减少第三方间接排放的行动。

该业务功能见 3.7 节。

**运营可持续信息系统**

ICT 运营应加以调整，以最小化其环境影响。以下活动必须组织或加强：

- ICT 设备清单应准确维护，且不仅覆盖服务器，还应覆盖工作场所资产，包括外设、显示器、打印机、电视、会议室视频设备等；
- 必须发展 ICT 设备维修活动；
- 必须监控数字消费；
- 数据中心必须设计削峰（load-shedding）能力，可在电力短缺时按网络运营商的要求启动。

  其目的是维持电网的完整性，防止整个网络离线并导致长期停电。

该业务功能见 3.8 节。

**治理向可持续信息系统的转型**

转型需要长期承诺及相应的治理：环境标准必须处于推动信息系统演进的决策过程的核心。

该业务功能见 3.9 节。

> **脚注**：摩尔定律是指，密集集成电路（IC）中的晶体管数量大约每两年翻一番。摩尔定律是对历史趋势的观察与预测；它不是物理定律，而是与生产经验收益相关的经验关系。来源：en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moore%27s_law。

图3-1：数字可持续性参考模型（高层版）；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-1](../figures/G242/fig3-1.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 先限制资源与能源使用，再将ICT集中到价值最大的业务领域。
  - 适用边界：不能仅优化现有技术而跳过用途必要性和业务模式判断。
  - PDF 30：Maximizing the digital sector’s contribution to carbon neutrality requires minimizing the use of energy and natural resources. Consequently, ICT departments should reduce consumption everywhere and an efficient strategy to achieve this is to focus on the areas of greatest value.
- 外包服务及其间接排放属于完整视角的一部分。
  - 适用边界：外包不会把相关环境影响移出决策边界。
  - PDF 30：• Outsourced services, including their indirect GHG emissions
- 完整评估需要设备制造影响。
  - 适用边界：以运行用电替代全生命周期足迹会遗漏制造。
  - PDF 30：• The impact of digital equipment’s manufacturing process
- 制造地与运行地的电力结构都影响结果。
  - 适用边界：默认同一国家电力因子或当前单一电网因子可能造成比较失真。
  - PDF 30：• The environmental performance of the electrical grid of the sites where the digital equipment is manufactured and operated
- 新系统上线应协调及时下线其替代的旧系统。
  - 适用边界：目标图上删除旧系统不是实际退役；应有迁移及下线证据。
  - PDF 32：• Implementation of new systems should be coordinated with the removal of older systems they replace in a very timely fashion; there must be a plan and an end date - double run period with double impact should be minimized
- 数据存储应有可量化用途依据，尽早设定寿命和删除安排。
  - 适用边界：过期/删除需同时满足保留法规、业务历史与授权，不直接执行删除。
  - PDF 32：• Stored data must be managed in a rigorous and economical way, and its presence justified by quantifiable utility
  - PDF 32：Data items should have an expiry and deletion date when initiated. Deletion automation can then be configured. Guidance from existing legal policies should be leveraged, and the ICT industry should seek new standards from the governments when missing for some data.
- 技术选择考虑适配用途、可维修、耐久与能源效率。
  - 适用边界：源文指向原则5存在错配，不能将知识共享原则当硬件适配规范。
  - PDF 32：• Technical solutions must be chosen considering their repairability, their durability, their energy efficiency, the origin of their components (for instance, if they rely on a circular economy), the absence of hazardous substance (refer to European Commission A) – overall, technical solutions must be fit-for-purpose; see Table 3-7, Principle 5
- 开源及开放标准是减缓过时、促进透明共享的选项。
  - 适用边界：开源许可本身不证明更低碳足迹或足够安全；需评估服务功能和生命周期。
  - PDF 32：• Open source and open standards choices or contribution should be favored as they are solutions to obsolescence and can allow significant longer usage of equipment [Mobbs, 2012]
- 购买服务仍应要求供应商为组织可持续目标作出贡献。
  - 适用边界：供应商承诺、认证与服务实际排放报告应分开。
  - PDF 33：Finally, even if the organization does not implement the services it uses (e.g., purchased services, cloud, etc.), it remains nonetheless responsible for the amount of data it produces and processes, its network usage, and the choice of service suppliers. External suppliers cannot be treated as a “carbon trash can” that an organization fills up to reduce the scope of its own obligations. Intense collaboration with the entire range of subcontractors is necessary to ensure the completeness of the measurement as well as the implementation of actions to reduce indirect emissions from these third parties.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0030:c002 · PDF 30**

3.2. How to Deploy a Sustainable Information System Policy

**G242:p0030:c003 · PDF 30**

Maximizing the digital sector’s contribution to carbon neutrality requires minimizing the use of energy and natural resources. Consequently, ICT departments should reduce consumption everywhere and an efficient strategy to achieve this is to focus on the areas of greatest value.

**G242:p0030:c004 · PDF 30**

These areas of greatest value must be defined carefully through the sustainability prism. Indeed, when a business itself is unsustainable but ICT makes it more efficient, the impact of ICT will systemically be unsustainable because it contributes to the growth of unsustainable activities.

**G242:p0030:c005 · PDF 30**

There is a need for Architecture Policies to reflect the Business Policies for Sustainability; and the need for an appropriate Governance Framework to ensure that they are appropriately applied. The TOGAF Standard [C220] “Fundamental Content, Enterprise Architecture Capability and Governance” provides such a guidance, which needs to be tailored to suit the culture and the operating model of the enterprise.

**G242:p0030:c006 · PDF 30**

So far, only a few organizations have adopted an environmental view of their ICT system. Depending on the enterprise, such a view should include, among other things:

**G242:p0030:c007 · PDF 30**

• Outsourced services, including their indirect GHG emissions

**G242:p0030:c008 · PDF 30**

• The impact of digital equipment’s manufacturing process

**G242:p0030:c009 · PDF 30**

• The environmental performance of the electrical grid of the sites where the digital equipment is manufactured and operated

**G242:p0030:c010 · PDF 30**

• The providers that support and evolve the licensed software

**G242:p0030:c011 · PDF 30**

As organizations do not have this complete vision, decisions and trade-offs regarding the numerous uses will suffer from weak points that may hide environmental impacts which could, if visible, be significant enough to affect decisions and outcomes.

**G242:p0030:c012 · PDF 30**

The framework presented here aims to support organizations in their transformation towards a sustainable ICT system. It proposes a reference framework to define a common language for multi- disciplinary professionals, allowing ICT professionals and businesses of an enterprise to better collaborate on sustainable digital initiatives.

**G242:p0030:c013 · PDF 30**

New activities or changes to existing ones must be implemented to deploy a sustainable information system policy. Those described below are common to all organizations. They allow reduction of the environmental footprint of the ICT system, and influence the IT market towards sustainability, and thus have a systemic impact.

**G242:p0031:c000 · PDF 31**

This “fitness program” of the ICT system is intended to serve as a starting point to tackle the infobesity that strikes organizations. It can be used to classify activities already undertaken, to evaluate their maturity, their strengths, their areas of improvement, and how they compare across companies in the market. It should be adapted to the specific context of each organization.

**G242:p0031:c001 · PDF 31**

The reference model in Figure 3-1 describes the seven high-level business functions required to set up and deploy an effective sustainable ICT system policy in any organization. A high-level description of these seven activities is provided in this chapter and then more details are provided in the ones that follow.

**G242:p0031:c002 · PDF 31**

Figure 3-1: Digital Sustainability Reference Model (High-Level Version): The Information System Fitness

**G242:p0031:c003 · PDF 31**

Program (Source: The Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group)

**G242:p0031:c004 · PDF 31**

Developing a Sustainable Digital Culture

**G242:p0031:c005 · PDF 31**

The lack of knowledge about complex energy-climate issues and the environmental impact of digital technology is one of the root causes of our difficulty in dealing with these subjects. It is a prerequisite for the other activities to succeed. To reach the point where it is decided to launch a transformation towards a sustainable information system, executives and staff need to be aware of the environmental crisis and the urgency to transform. Then, to be executed, this program needs the support of all employees, who must be taught and trained so that they can start to embody a culture of sufficiency and sustainability that will become part of the long-term DNA of the organization.

**G242:p0031:c006 · PDF 31**

This business function is described in Section 3.3.

**G242:p0032:c000 · PDF 32**

Developing and Rolling Out a Sustainable Information System Strategy

**G242:p0032:c001 · PDF 32**

To become more sustainable, the information system needs an ongoing long-term program. This requires the support of the company’s leadership and a strong sponsor, such as the director of operations, IT department, or sustainability department. It involves formulating and sharing motivations and objectives for transformation towards a sustainable information system. These objectives must be part of the global sustainability strategy of the organization and reflected in the ICT strategy and Enterprise Architecture.

**G242:p0032:c002 · PDF 32**

Once the sustainable ICT strategy has been defined, it is necessary to set up a team that will be responsible for leading the transformation through the activities described below.

**G242:p0032:c003 · PDF 32**

This business function is described in Section 3.4.

**G242:p0032:c004 · PDF 32**

Architecting and Building a Sustainable Information System

**G242:p0032:c005 · PDF 32**

Methods for creating digital tools should be adapted to minimize their environmental impact at all stages of their lifecycle, from design to end-of-life. This new framework will feed the Agile methodologies used for the creation of ICT solutions with new sustainable requirements. Architectures should be chosen depending on their environmental impact. All architecture levels are concerned:

**G242:p0032:c006 · PDF 32**

• Business units must question the need for digital solutions

**G242:p0032:c007 · PDF 32**

Among the offers that do provide real business value, they should select the most environmentally efficient, considering the end-to-end value chain, from manufacturing to consumption.

**G242:p0032:c008 · PDF 32**

• Implementation of new systems should be coordinated with the removal of older systems they replace in a very timely fashion; there must be a plan and an end date - double run period with double impact should be minimized

**G242:p0032:c009 · PDF 32**

• Applications must be resource-efficient on the organization’s or provider’s servers and on end users’ terminals

**G242:p0032:c010 · PDF 32**

• Stored data must be managed in a rigorous and economical way, and its presence justified by quantifiable utility

**G242:p0032:c011 · PDF 32**

Data items should have an expiry and deletion date when initiated. Deletion automation can then be configured. Guidance from existing legal policies should be leveraged, and the ICT industry should seek new standards from the governments when missing for some data.

**G242:p0032:c012 · PDF 32**

• Technical solutions must be chosen considering their repairability, their durability, their energy efficiency, the origin of their components (for instance, if they rely on a circular economy), the absence of hazardous substance (refer to European Commission A) – overall, technical solutions must be fit-for-purpose; see Table 3-7, Principle 5

**G242:p0032:c013 · PDF 32**

• Open source and open standards choices or contribution should be favored as they are solutions to obsolescence and can allow significant longer usage of equipment [Mobbs, 2012]

**G242:p0032:c014 · PDF 32**

This business function is described in Section 3.5.

**G242:p0033:c000 · PDF 33**

Measuring the Environmental Impact of the Information System from Cradle to Grave

**G242:p0033:c001 · PDF 33**

Measurement is mandatory to drive organizations’ science-based decisions toward a quantitative reduction of the Information System’s environmental impact. The process to assess its effective reduction must be developed on the whole lifecycle of hardware components “from cradle to grave”. Indeed, the manufacturing production of hardware is responsible for more than half of its emissions. As Paul Mobbs states in A Practical Guide to Sustainable IT: “While many may look primarily at power consumption, the manufacture of digital electronics also has a major impact on the global environment.” [Mobbs, 2012] It uses natural resources, generates waste, and pollutes the soil and water. The search for the optimization of power consumption is leading to intense hardware renewal rates [Shift Project, 2019]. To build a sustainable information system, full end-to-end impacts must be considered, including the “Scope 3” of the carbon accounting method defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol. For the implementation of such measurements in an organization, a framework is proposed in Section 3.6. A first order of magnitude of CO2e emissions can be calculated with reasonable effort and minimum tooling, then more precision or higher granularity will require more investments.

**G242:p0033:c002 · PDF 33**

This business function is described in Section 3.6.

**G242:p0033:c003 · PDF 33**

Engaging with Customers, Providers, Partners, and Authorities Towards Sustainable Solutions and Services

**G242:p0033:c004 · PDF 33**

To date, the information system is characterized by rapid obsolescence. It is at the core of the current economic model of the sector. This is unsustainable and should evolve toward planned sustainability. Furthermore, the energy mix of countries in which ICT employees and service providers operate is rarely considered as a parameter regarding the choice of services, even though it can change the environmental impact by several orders of magnitude.

**G242:p0033:c005 · PDF 33**

This transformation requires a collective mobilization of the different actors from the ICT sector, whether they are device manufacturers, service providers, or companies using those services. Indeed, solutions and digital services are most often purchased from third parties.

**G242:p0033:c006 · PDF 33**

The ecosystem surrounding an organization is strategic for its transformation. To achieve sustainability objectives, the organization needs to create the relevant ecosystem of partners which can align on its environmental strategy and collaborate on a common set of transformational initiatives.

**G242:p0033:c007 · PDF 33**

Finally, even if the organization does not implement the services it uses (e.g., purchased services, cloud, etc.), it remains nonetheless responsible for the amount of data it produces and processes, its network usage, and the choice of service suppliers. External suppliers cannot be treated as a “carbon trash can” that an organization fills up to reduce the scope of its own obligations. Intense collaboration with the entire range of subcontractors is necessary to ensure the completeness of the measurement as well as the implementation of actions to reduce indirect emissions from these third parties.

**G242:p0033:c008 · PDF 33**

This business function is described in Section 3.7.

**G242:p0034:c000 · PDF 34**

Operating a Sustainable Information System

**G242:p0034:c001 · PDF 34**

ICT operations should be adapted to minimize their environmental impact. The following activities will have to be organized or reinforced:

**G242:p0034:c002 · PDF 34**

• ICT equipment inventories should be maintained accurately and should cover not only servers but also workplace assets, including peripherals, monitors, printers, televisions, meeting room video tools, etc.

**G242:p0034:c003 · PDF 34**

• ICT equipment repair activities will have to be developed

**G242:p0034:c004 · PDF 34**

• Digital consumption will have to be monitored

**G242:p0034:c005 · PDF 34**

• Data centers must be designed with load-shedding capabilities that can be activated upon request from network operators in case of power shortages

**G242:p0034:c006 · PDF 34**

The purpose is to maintain the integrity of the electric grid and to prevent the entire network from going offline and leading to long-term power outages.

**G242:p0034:c007 · PDF 34**

This business function is described in Section 3.8.

**G242:p0034:c008 · PDF 34**

Governing the Transition Towards a Sustainable Information System

**G242:p0034:c009 · PDF 34**

The transformation requires long-term commitment and associated governance: the environmental criteria must be at the core of the decision-making process driving the evolution of the information system.

**G242:p0034:c010 · PDF 34**

This business function is described in Section 3.9.

<a id="3.3"></a>
## 发展可持续数字文化

原文：3.3. Developing a Sustainable Digital Culture。PDF 物理页：[34, 35]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

为抵消当今不可持续且不断增长的数字产品与服务使用，需要培育一种以节制为驱动的新数字化文化。既然我们知道每一项数字产品或服务都依赖日益稀缺的不可再生资源，每个组织就必须发展数字可持续文化，以获得更好的韧性。这是一次重大的文化转变，必须加以推进，以确保气候与环境议题被采纳并融入内部与外部的使用方式和工作方法。

面对这种紧迫性，组织内部需要一场文化转型。转型应从小胜利开始，逐步凝聚同事，并使方法结构化，共同走向数字可持续。

这一转型可以按照描绘数字节制共同目标的四个主要维度来推进和评估；见图 3-2。

图3-2：数字可持续性的关键文化轴；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-2](../figures/G242/fig3-2.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 文化转型应从小举措开始并逐步改变习惯。
  - 适用边界：培训计划、参与记录、行为变化和实际减排是不同证据层次。
  - PDF 34：Facing this urgency, a culture transformation within organizations is required. Transformation should start with small victories, gradually rally co-workers and structuring the approach to move towards digital sustainability together.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0034:c011 · PDF 34**

3.3. Developing a Sustainable Digital Culture

**G242:p0034:c012 · PDF 34**

To counterbalance today’s unsustainable and ever-growing use of digital products and services, there is a need to develop a new digitalization culture driven by sufficiency. Now that we know that each digital product or service is enabled by non-renewable resources that are becoming scarce, each organization must develop a culture of digital sustainability for better resilience. This is a major cultural shift that must be conducted to ensure the adoption and integration of climatic and environmental issues into both internal and external uses and work methods.

**G242:p0034:c013 · PDF 34**

Facing this urgency, a culture transformation within organizations is required. Transformation should start with small victories, gradually rally co-workers and structuring the approach to move towards digital sustainability together.

**G242:p0034:c014 · PDF 34**

This transformation can be conducted and evaluated according to four main dimensions depicting the digital sufficiency common goal; see Figure 3-2.

**G242:p0035:c000 · PDF 35**

Figure 3-2: Digital Sustainability Key Culture Axes (Source: The Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group)

</details>

<a id="3.3.1"></a>
## 组织、管理与工作文化

原文：3.3.1. Organization, Management, and Work Culture。PDF 物理页：[35, 36]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

要开展具有正当性并持久的转型，气候危机与资源可得性风险必须在公司最高层面得到缓解，并在每一项决策中被考虑。CSR 所采取的原则与方向必须相应得到强化和调整。管理层通过承诺这一转型，鼓励员工积极投身于一场意义足够重大、足以超越组织本身的变革。

这种变革的动力必须由通常的管理工具来支撑，例如目标设定、利润分享等。每个参与者都必须了解战略性的碳减排目标，并将其纳入日常活动。在操作层面，目标必须在个人层面可得，但对全球气候目标的承诺，首先必须是集体的。

也可以考虑更非正式的方法来营造争先氛围。良好实践可通过实践社群/专业行会（guilds）与 ICT 社群分享——它们是强有力的认可与激励工具。

每个业务单元能够随时度量其活动的环境足迹，是这一过程的关键要素。它使每个团队、敏捷项目、协作者、业务活动或 ICT 专业人员都能通过定义自己的目标与管理指标，在本层级采取行动；见 3.6 节。

发展一种能够通过战略性气候目标把同事凝聚起来的工作文化是必要的。这种文化越是得到以身作则的管理团队的支持与承载，就越能感召所有同事。它尤其应促成以节俭、以身作则的领导力、犯错的权利（并从中学习）、简单、人的因素、批判性思维等坚实价值观为基础的原则与习惯反射的形成。ICT 社群必须成为组织内的驱动力，引领并支撑向数字可持续的转型。

在数字领域工作的专业人员在可持续数字文化的发展中扮演关键角色。所有信息系统参与者，尤其是专业水平最高者，都必须参与其中。因此，视其岗位而定，他们应该：

- 被纳入教育项目计划，并获得完成其使命所需的专门资源；
- 参与构建并跟踪用于监视信息系统影响的各种指标；
- 参与各类信息系统指导与治理委员会，确保项目与决策与节俭目标一致；
- 在不同层面（开发、业务活动等）以及整个信息系统生命周期中，担任新数字节制实践的参照人（referent）。

此外，由于产品负责人（product owner）在 ICT 与业务单元之间的关键作用，他们必须在两个差异极大的世界之间架起桥梁，以解决共同的问题。其日常工作必须演进，把可持续数字目标纳入其整个行动范围：由他们来质疑环境价值，并在业务价值与环境足迹之间建立平衡。

最后，使组织得以转型的流程——例如企业架构（尤其因其系统性方法而非常适合）——以及组织内建立的跟踪或治理机构，都必须加以调整，把数字可持续性作为一个目标纳入，如 3.4 节和 3.9 节所述。

总体而言，除 ICT 部门之外，还需要来自主管/高管层、作为公司战略一部分的强有力赞助。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 转型需获得组织正当性并在最高层考虑气候和资源风险。
  - 适用边界：legitimate不意味着已通过法律合规审查。
  - PDF 35：To develop a legitimate and long-lasting transformation, the climate crisis and resource availability risks must be mitigated at the highest level of the company and considered in every decision. The principles and orientations taken by the CSR must be reinforced and adapted accordingly. By committing to this transformation, management encourages their employees to actively contribute to a transformation meaningful enough to transcend the organization.
- 目标、资源、认可和管理工具应支持人员参与。
  - 适用边界：角色命名不能证明岗位获得资源或激励。
  - PDF 35：This motivation toward change must be supported by the usual management tools such as the definition of objectives, profit-sharing, etc. Each actor must be aware of the strategic carbon reduction objective and include it in their daily activities. Operationally, objectives must be available at an individual level, but the commitment to the global climate goal must be, more than anything, collective.
- 业务单元应能获知自身活动的环境足迹以支持决策。
  - 适用边界：业务分摊方法需与组织总量对账，不能为每部门重复记入共享排放总额。
  - PDF 35：The possibility for each business unit to measure the environmental footprint of its activity at any time is a key element of such a process. It enables each team, Agile project, co-worker, business activity, or ICT professional to act at their level by defining their own objectives and management indicators; see Section 3.6.
- 产品负责人连接业务需求与ICT节制实践。
  - 适用边界：不将所有责任转给架构师或可持续性专员。
  - PDF 36：Additionally, because of their key role between ICT and business units, product owners will have to bridge a gap between two vastly different worlds to solve a common issue. Their daily activities will have to evolve to include sustainable digital objectives across their entire action range: it is up to them to question the environmental value and to establish a balance between business value versus environmental footprint.
- 需要超出ICT部门的主管/高管支持。
  - 适用边界：director level不是必须为法定董事席位；具体授权由企业确定。
  - PDF 36：Overall, strong sponsorship from director level, as part of the company’s strategy, is required beyond the ICT department.
