### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0046:c010 · PDF 46**

3.5.2. A Dual Use-Based and Infrastructure-Based Architecture Approach to Drive Transformation

**G242:p0046:c011 · PDF 46**

Shifting towards a sustainable information system is a significant transformation that will impact the full scope of architecture work. After the aspirational vision, a concrete approach leveraging existing Enterprise Architecture capabilities is proposed in this chapter to enable this shift.

**G242:p0047:c000 · PDF 47**

Architecture Levers to Reduce ICT Environmental Footprint

**G242:p0047:c001 · PDF 47**

The Target Architecture for sustainable information systems must coherently reshape the business processes and uses of the associated systems, applications, and data, as well as the technical infrastructures supporting these components.

**G242:p0047:c002 · PDF 47**

Figure 3-4: Architecture Layers and Carbon Emissions

**G242:p0047:c003 · PDF 47**

1. The carbon footprint of the company’s ICT system consists of the ones of its technical infrastructures: equipment components, servers, storage units, network controllers, screens, fixed and mobile devices, and the technical software operating them

**G242:p0047:c004 · PDF 47**

It is not limited to electricity consumption during operation, but also includes energy consumption for construction, installation of components, maintenance, and end-of-life.

**G242:p0047:c005 · PDF 47**

It is not limited to locally produced emissions; it also includes foreign emissions due to the manufacturing of multiple components and operation of multiple servers.

**G242:p0047:c006 · PDF 47**

It is not limited to the components owned by the company, but also includes the emissions of the components of ICT (cloud, for example) and functional subcontractors (advertising agency, for example) who operate on their behalf.

**G242:p0047:c007 · PDF 47**

2. Technical infrastructures only exist to store, transfer, and process data, or run application software

**G242:p0047:c008 · PDF 47**

3. The ICT system is also characterized by business processes, which use information in oral, written, and digital forms in a controlled way; application software automates part of the procedures included in business processes

**G242:p0048:c000 · PDF 48**

Thus, the head of ICT can organize the reduction of the carbon footprint by adapting designs at several levels:

**G242:p0048:c001 · PDF 48**

• Informing departments of the environmental footprints of the digital services they request, and offering choices that favor services with high societal value

**G242:p0048:c002 · PDF 48**

• Optimizing business processes and automation procedures with application software

**G242:p0048:c003 · PDF 48**

• Optimizing the application level and how it calls the technical level

**G242:p0048:c004 · PDF 48**

• Optimizing the technical level

**G242:p0048:c005 · PDF 48**

Combining Top-Down and Bottom-Up Approaches to Capture Digital Sustainability Requirements

**G242:p0048:c006 · PDF 48**

A dual approach to identify sustainability requirements can be implemented combining simultaneously:

**G242:p0048:c007 · PDF 48**

• A business uses-based approach, close to business operators, with the goal of reducing the footprint of identified business uses (e.g., sending emails, sharing documents, or the real use of a business application as a whole)

**G242:p0048:c008 · PDF 48**

As explained in Section 3.6.3, initial footprint measurements aggregated at the level of business functions/business applications can designate priorities among business uses.

**G242:p0048:c009 · PDF 48**

• An infrastructure-based approach, whose goal is to reduce the footprint of ICT systems identified as consuming resources and data (and potentially carrying several uses)

**G242:p0048:c010 · PDF 48**

Likewise, initial footprint measurements of existing infrastructures should help guide the identification of highly emitting systems.

**G242:p0048:c011 · PDF 48**

Figure 3-5: Combining Top-Down and Bottom-Up Approaches to Capture Digital Sustainability Requirements

**G242:p0049:c000 · PDF 49**

These two approaches come together to identify a complete set of mitigation actions:

**G242:p0049:c001 · PDF 49**

• The business uses-based approach must deliver a prioritized list of business uses that could be renounced or optimized

**G242:p0049:c002 · PDF 49**

It requires:

**G242:p0049:c003 · PDF 49**

◦A strategic consideration of how it can be achieved, that may differ from one business to

**G242:p0049:c004 · PDF 49**

another, indeed, over the last 40 years, organizations have been in a continuous automation process, and it is a significant reversal

**G242:p0049:c005 · PDF 49**

◦A mandate from the top management to work on these topics with business units

**G242:p0049:c006 · PDF 49**

◦An exchange with business units, end users of the systems

**G242:p0049:c007 · PDF 49**

◦Initial metrics, by business unit or business application, to target uses that have the highest

**G242:p0049:c008 · PDF 49**

environmental impact

**G242:p0049:c009 · PDF 49**

• The infrastructure-based approach must deliver a prioritized list of digital equipment that should be disposed of or optimized

**G242:p0049:c010 · PDF 49**

It requires:

**G242:p0049:c011 · PDF 49**

◦A mandate from the top management to work on these topics with infrastructure teams

**G242:p0049:c012 · PDF 49**

◦An inventory of equipment supporting the information system of the organization

**G242:p0049:c013 · PDF 49**

◦Initial metrics about infrastructure (e.g., highest footprint or highest footprint per user) to

**G242:p0049:c014 · PDF 49**

target systems that have the highest environmental impact

**G242:p0049:c015 · PDF 49**

In both cases, decommissioning opportunities and optimizations may be found in the existing infrastructures, developments, software architecture, data, and in the real definition of the business uses of the system. The entry point is the only changing parameter in these two approaches: the optimization of a business use required to “go down” to the technical system, and the optimization of a technical system required to “go up” to its business uses (for example, to ensure the acceptability of the potential impact of a reduction or an optimization of the system by the business unit).

**G242:p0049:c016 · PDF 49**

The implementation of decommissioning and optimization opportunities identified from business uses or from infrastructure are the design backbone of an information system with lower emissions.

**G242:p0049:c017 · PDF 49**

Revising the Non-Functional Requirements

**G242:p0049:c018 · PDF 49**

Overall, the delivery of these mitigation and reduction opportunities must result in revising the non- functional requirements on the systems and in new ICT projects, with the main trends shown in Figure 3-6.

**G242:p0050:c000 · PDF 50**

Figure 3-6: Non-Functional Requirements Move in a Digital Sustainability Initiative (Source: The Shift Project

**G242:p0050:c001 · PDF 50**

Lean-ICT Group)

**G242:p0050:c002 · PDF 50**

Daring to constrain the information system and its uses requires the reduction of availability, storage capacity and energy needs. Requirements in terms of longevity, maintainability, dismantlability, repairability, portability, interoperability, and robustness of the systems will globally be increased.

**G242:p0050:c003 · PDF 50**

Beyond the individual use-by-use and system-by-system mitigation options, it is important to set constraints on the system to promote a low-carbon trajectory over time.

**G242:p0050:c004 · PDF 50**

Setting Constraints

**G242:p0050:c005 · PDF 50**

Setting constraints can be perceived as unpleasant and it must be combined with a culture-change approach and an attractive vision. It is a powerful and essential lever for the architect to drive the change towards sustainability:

**G242:p0050:c006 · PDF 50**

• The ecosystem moves towards increasing constraints on resources, regulations, etc.

**G242:p0050:c007 · PDF 50**

To avoid constraining the organization at the wrong time without being prepared, it is better to think in advance of a system under chosen constraints and adapt the organization, processes, uses, and systems accordingly.

**G242:p0050:c008 · PDF 50**

• Setting up a system of constraints also makes it possible to limit the rebound effect on the system, by explicitly limiting uses, infrastructures, and data (volumes, location)

**G242:p0051:c000 · PDF 51**

Figure 3-7: The Four Typologies of Constraints to Apply to be Sustainable

**G242:p0051:c001 · PDF 51**

To do so, the system must be constrained gradually. The constraints installed by the architecture must cover several forms:

**G242:p0051:c002 · PDF 51**

• Constraints on uses

**G242:p0051:c003 · PDF 51**

For example, limit the volumes associated with some specific uses (volumes of attachments sent per day and per person in an email, volumes of video consumed per day, etc.), limit the frequency of certain uses (number of web pages opened per day and per person), limit certain uses altogether (restrict access to social media, streaming sites, mobile network, high volume storage capabilities, etc.).

**G242:p0051:c004 · PDF 51**

• Constraints on data

**G242:p0051:c005 · PDF 51**

For example, set a maximum volume for the company, a maximum volume per user/per application, restrict the geographical perimeter of the physical location of data (favor data storage as close as possible to its end user or in a region where low-carbon electricity is distributed on the grid).

**G242:p0051:c006 · PDF 51**

• Constraints on applications and developments

**G242:p0051:c007 · PDF 51**

For example, limit/restrict the use of certain frameworks, certain libraries; restrain the use of high carbon footprint technology. Add constraints on development and test, challenge the need to “DevOps”, continuously running a heavy Continuous Integration and Continuous Delivery/Deployment CI/CD pipeline.

**G242:p0052:c000 · PDF 52**

• Constraints on Infrastructure/Devices

**G242:p0052:c001 · PDF 52**

For example, restrict the types of equipment purchased, the volume of servers, the Virtual Machines (VMs)/containers, the maximum number of technical objects used (by sizing intervals), and limit the energy consumption of certain infrastructures.

**G242:p0052:c002 · PDF 52**

The ICT architect must formalize and validate a path towards a system under selected constraints by involving all stakeholders. The exercise is of course more complicated than it seems. It is not about imposing “unwise” constraints. Being able to design an appropriately constrained system requires having metrics in place. This involves combining constraints at different levels while ensuring through culture and awareness that they are also acceptable and bearable for the organization’s business processes.

**G242:p0052:c003 · PDF 52**

The constraints must be explicitly expressed, justified, and reviewed periodically, within the framework of the governance activity, in the same way as the Architecture Principles.

**G242:p0052:c004 · PDF 52**

Table 3-3: Setting and Governing Constraints

**G242:p0052:c005 · PDF 52**

|  | Justification | Complexity in<br>Making or<br>Maintaining the<br>System<br>Compliant | Value to<br>Comply with<br>the Constraint | Can the<br>Constraint be<br>Automated?<br>(Automatic<br>blocking/alert<br>messages, etc.) | Compliance<br>Control Modality<br>(How do we check<br>that the constraint<br>is respected?) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| The volume of data<br>stored in the cloud must<br>not exceed xTO | … | … | … |  |  |
| The data must be<br>physically located in the<br>country of use |  |  |  |  |  |

</details>

<a id="3.5.3"></a>
## 重塑业务架构

原文：3.5.3. Reshape Business Architecture。PDF 物理页：[52, 53]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

业务架构师正在发现并记录业务需求，这些需求将成为设计应用能力（数据与处理）的输入，并与业务团队协商以满足业务单元的需要。依靠人还是依靠机器的决策，需要在 ICT 碳足迹与基于人的、低技术的或生物的解决方案的碳足迹之间取得平衡。涉及“什么应当数字化”之权衡的决策，是遏制有机增长、降低 ICT 碳足迹的杠杆。

业务架构旨在描述公司各业务单元特有的流程，以及跨职能流程，例如产品线数据共享、内部运营数据（消息、报表）等。此处的挑战是：

- 对每个范围和生命周期，依据所需基础设施的碳足迹，决定哪些信息应数字化、哪些不应数字化，哪些流程应自动化、哪些不应自动化；
- 识别那些在实践中并不服务于任何用途、或因过时、系统预期设计与用户体验之间的落差而不再使用的数字工具与功能；
- 度量数字化相对于不数字化的真实收益：灵活性、员工参与度、运营收益、模拟（非数字）足迹的减少、财务收益、成本降低、可访问性等。

最终，文化变革将激发业务单元聚焦于必要用途、消除不必要用途、调整流程以减少系统足迹的意愿，并促成向可持续信息系统的转变。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 业务架构应质询真实需求、审视使用，并考虑低技术方案。
  - 适用边界：业务价值与适用性需要业务参与者证据，不能直接删减功能。
  - PDF 52：Business Architects are discovering and documenting business requirements that will be the inputs to design application capacities – data and processing – in a negotiation with business teams to meet business units’ needs. The decision to rely on people or on machines will need to balance the ICT carbon footprint with the one or other people-based, low tech, or biologic solutions. Decisions involving trade-offs on what should be digitalized are levers to contain organic growth and to reduce ICT carbon footprint.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0052:c006 · PDF 52**

3.5.3. Reshape Business Architecture

**G242:p0052:c007 · PDF 52**

Business Architects are discovering and documenting business requirements that will be the inputs to design application capacities – data and processing – in a negotiation with business teams to meet business units’ needs. The decision to rely on people or on machines will need to balance the ICT carbon footprint with the one or other people-based, low tech, or biologic solutions. Decisions involving trade-offs on what should be digitalized are levers to contain organic growth and to reduce ICT carbon footprint.

**G242:p0052:c008 · PDF 52**

Business Architecture aims at describing processes specific to each business unit of the company, and cross-functional processes like sharing of product line data, internal operating data (messaging, reporting), etc. The challenge here is:

**G242:p0052:c009 · PDF 52**

• To decide, for each perimeter and lifecycle, which information should be digitized and which should not, and which processes should be automated and which should not, depending on the carbon footprint of the infrastructures required

**G242:p0053:c000 · PDF 53**

• To identify the digital tools and functionalities, which in practice do not serve any use, or are no longer in use because of obsolescence, discrepancy between intended design and user experience of the system

**G242:p0053:c001 · PDF 53**

• To measure the real gain of digitization when compared to no digitization: flexibility, staff involvement, operational gain, reduction of the analogue footprint, financial gain, costs reductions, accessibility etc.

**G242:p0053:c002 · PDF 53**

Ultimately, the culture change will fuel the desire of business units to focus on essential uses, to eliminate unnecessary ones, and to adapt processes to reduce the footprint of the systems and will enable the shift toward a sustainable information system.

</details>

<a id="3.5.4"></a>
## 重塑应用架构

原文：3.5.4. Reshape Application Architecture。PDF 物理页：[53]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

用户的大部分功能需求是在应用层实现的。软件架构要么来自：

- **软件/软件包公司**：在这种情况下，要求供应商提供必要的透明度与工具，以清晰呈现软件的碳足迹与开发过程。软件公司在代码优化、性能报告/不同配置的足迹方面所采用的最佳实践也应被记录在案。

要么来自：

- **组织内部的开发过程**。

必须研究缩小功能需求的覆盖范围。还应开展非功能需求的评审，特别是用户界面方面，以避免例如在新页面自动播放视频、限制动画等。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 应用选择标准应纳入可持续性。
  - 适用边界：相同功能、用户范围、负载、时间跨度与质量水平下才能比较方案。
  - PDF 93：| Business Function | Description<br>(Description of High-Level Functions is provided in Section 3.2 |
| --- | --- |
| Set Overall and Function-level<br>Goals | Define the goals of the digital sustainability transformation, and of the outcomes.<br>They will include environmental indicators like COe emissions, electricity<br>2<br>consumption in kWh, but also, for example, on financial gain, security risk<br>attenuation.<br>Depending on the organization, the high-level objectives can be split into sub-<br>objectives for each business unit or function. |
| Check the Business Model | Check if the current business model of the organization accepts a digital<br>sustainability transformation and define conditions for this change to happen.<br>This function is focused on continuous work on the compatibility between the<br>organization’s business model and the digital sustainability transformation.<br>One example: In the business model of the organization, if profitability is directly<br>proportional to the digital data volumes consumed or produced by the customers,<br>this business model could be incompatible with a digital sustainability initiative,<br>and it would need to be adapted. |
| Build and Launch the<br>Transformation Program | Build the transformation journey with well-defined transition architectures and<br>clear intermediate results to reach in each step. This plan should be agreed and<br>shared with all stakeholders. |
| Architecting and Building a Sustainable Information System |  |
| State the Vision of a Sustainable<br>Information System | Build the overall vision of what should be a low environmental impact<br>information system with the Business Architecture and Usage, Application and<br>Data Architecture, and Technology Architecture. Show the main concrete levers to<br>reduce this impact. |
| A Dual Use-Based and<br>Infrastructure-Based Architecture<br>Approach to Drive Transformation | A dual approach to identify sustainability requirements can be implemented<br>combining simultaneously: a business uses-based approach and an infrastructure-<br>based approach |
| Reshape Business Architecture | Act to regulate ICT business usage. Work proactively with the business. Adopt a<br>process and a mindset of minimization of ICT assets such as equipment, data,<br>applications, network, etc. Challenge the actual business needs to keep essential<br>components, consider low-tech solutions, and remove “nice to have”. |
| Reshape the Application<br>Architecture | Adapt solution choices criteria to integrate digital sustainability requirements.<br>Define data minimization as a principle for solution architecture design. All data<br>should be documented with proper complete metadata and owners to define end-<br>of-life dates by design.<br>The use of open-source development frameworks or applications is also a way to<br>mitigate technology obsolescence and increase the lifecycle of equipment. |

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0053:c003 · PDF 53**

3.5.4. Reshape Application Architecture

**G242:p0053:c004 · PDF 53**

Most of the users’ functional requirements are implemented at the application level. The software architectures come from either:

**G242:p0053:c005 · PDF 53**

• Software/software package companies: In this case, the supplier is required to provide the transparency and tools needed to give a clear vision of the software’s carbon footprint and development processes

**G242:p0053:c006 · PDF 53**

Best practices leveraged by the software company in terms of code optimization, performance report/footprints of different configurations should also be documented.

**G242:p0053:c007 · PDF 53**

Or:

**G242:p0053:c008 · PDF 53**

• An internal development process within the organization

**G242:p0053:c009 · PDF 53**

The reduction of the coverage of functional requirements will have to be studied. A review of non- functional requirements, in particular on user interfaces, should also be conducted to avoid for instance the automatic launch of videos on a new page, limit animations, etc.

</details>

<a id="3.5.5"></a>
## 重塑数据架构以减少数据量

原文：3.5.5. Reshape Data Architecture to Reduce Data Volumes。PDF 物理页：[53, 54]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

数据架构是一个重要杠杆。首席数据官（CDO）应在数据管理者的支持下，在其“数据战略”中对数据量的控制以及相关环境足迹的减少承担责任。由其负责的组织数据治理流程应演进为：

- **普遍推行“数据最小化”**。该原则在 GDPR 中针对个人数据被引用：“个人数据应当充分、相关，并限于处理目的所必需的范围”。这一原则必须推广到组织内的所有数据。应优先重视数据“质量”而非数据“数量”。
- **调整“数据管理”**。数据管理者需要把可持续性融入其业务实践。数据量控制应像质量控制和安全性控制一样被系统地纳入。
- **在设计阶段就管理数据寿命终止**（设定最长保留期限，且不仅限于个人数据）。
- **鼓励所有协作者减少其数据/文档量**；组织数据删除计划，使部分删除自动化，并组织“数据删除日”等活动。
- **质疑数据备份与历史化（historization）的相关性**。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 数据最小化需要数据责任与架构协作。
  - 适用边界：本节CDO为Chief Data Officer，区别于RACI中的Chief Digital Officer。
  - PDF 53：Data Architecture is a significant lever. The Chief Data Officer (CDO) with the support of Data Managers should take responsibility on the control of data volumes and the reduction of the associated environmental footprint in their “Data Strategy”. Organization’s data governance processes under their responsibility should evolve to:
- 指南主张将数据最小化设计原则扩展到全部数据。
  - 适用边界：GDPR法律覆盖个人数据，不能据此称全部企业数据均受同一法律最小化义务。
  - PDF 53：The principle is quoted in the GDPR concerning personal data: “personal data must be adequate, relevant, and limited to what is necessary for the purposes for which [it is] processed”. This principle must be generalized to all data across the organization. Data “quality” must be favored over data “quantity”.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0053:c010 · PDF 53**

3.5.5. Reshape Data Architecture to Reduce Data Volumes

**G242:p0053:c011 · PDF 53**

Data Architecture is a significant lever. The Chief Data Officer (CDO) with the support of Data Managers should take responsibility on the control of data volumes and the reduction of the associated environmental footprint in their “Data Strategy”. Organization’s data governance processes under their responsibility should evolve to:

**G242:p0053:c012 · PDF 53**

• Generalize “Data Minimization”

**G242:p0053:c013 · PDF 53**

The principle is quoted in the GDPR concerning personal data: “personal data must be adequate, relevant, and limited to what is necessary for the purposes for which [it is] processed”. This principle must be generalized to all data across the organization. Data “quality” must be favored over data “quantity”.

**G242:p0054:c000 · PDF 54**

• Adapt “Data Management”

**G242:p0054:c001 · PDF 54**

Data managers need to build sustainability into their practice. Data volume controls should be systematically integrated, as should quality controls and security controls.

**G242:p0054:c002 · PDF 54**

• Manage data at end-of-life by design (maximum retention duration, and not just for personal data)

**G242:p0054:c003 · PDF 54**

• Encourage all co-workers to reduce their data/document volumes; organize a data deletion program to automatize some deletion and organize events like a “data deletion day”

**G242:p0054:c004 · PDF 54**

• Challenge the relevance of data backup and historizations

</details>

<a id="3.5.6"></a>
## 重塑技术架构

原文：3.5.6. Reshape Technical Architecture。PDF 物理页：[54]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

ICT 的技术层由支撑某项技术服务（数据库、备份、应用软件执行、显示、数据传输等）的硬件与软件组件子集构成。技术架构优化示例如下：

- 应用层的削减与优化会降低硬件层的容量配置需求；
- 对每项技术服务（存储、网站托管等），识别碳足迹更低的技术架构模式，并将这些实现普遍化；
- 审慎选择供应商（见 3.7）：本地基础设施的服务器更新率、能够透明提供碳足迹度量与用电量的云服务供应商的选择；
- 就直接影响技术架构的非功能需求与业务方加强合作，进一步质询其必要性：“你们真的需要 99.99% 的可用性吗？真的需要保留两年历史数据吗？这带来了什么价值？”
- 若组织制造或维护数字设备，应追求“有计划的可持续使用”（而非快速过时），并选择更细粒度的生命周期管理，以便在组件级更换或维修给定设备，尤其要提高互操作性与耐久性要求、改善平均故障间隔时间（MTBF）；
- 与公共机构合作减少法定合规约束：某些情况下数据存储期限由法律而非企业选择决定。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 可用性、冗余与保留期应按真实需求重新审视。
  - 适用边界：99.99%、两年等是质询示例，不是通用SLA或保留上限。
  - PDF 54：• Strengthen work with the businesses on non-functional requirements that directly impact the technical architecture, to further challenge the need: “Do you really need 99.99% availability? Do you really need to keep a two-year history, for what value?”
- 对数据保留期的重审仍需考虑法规。
  - 适用边界：不为节能自动覆盖法定保留或业务合同要求。
  - PDF 54：• Work with public bodies to reduce legal compliance constraints: storage for data is in some cases defined by law and not by company choice

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0054:c005 · PDF 54**

3.5.6. Reshape Technical Architecture

**G242:p0054:c006 · PDF 54**

The technical level of ICT is made up of subsets of hardware and software components that support a technical service: database, backup, execution of application software, display, data transfer, etc.

**G242:p0054:c007 · PDF 54**

Some examples of optimization in technical architecture are listed below:

**G242:p0054:c008 · PDF 54**

• Reduction and optimization at the application level will lead to a decline in sizing requirements at the hardware level

**G242:p0054:c009 · PDF 54**

• For each technical service (storage, website hosting, etc.), identify technical architecture patterns with a lower carbon footprint, and generalize these implementations

**G242:p0054:c010 · PDF 54**

• Choose suppliers wisely (see Section 3.7): server renewal rate for on-premise infrastructure, choice of cloud services suppliers able to provide carbon footprint measurements and electricity consumption transparently

**G242:p0054:c011 · PDF 54**

• Strengthen work with the businesses on non-functional requirements that directly impact the technical architecture, to further challenge the need: “Do you really need 99.99% availability? Do you really need to keep a two-year history, for what value?”

**G242:p0054:c012 · PDF 54**

• If the organization manufactures or maintains digital material, strive for “planned sustainability” (as opposed to rapid obsolescence) and choose a lifecycle management at a more granular level to enable replacement or repairing at the component level of given equipment, in particular by increasing the requirements for interoperability and durability, improving Mean Time Between Failure (MTBF)

**G242:p0054:c013 · PDF 54**

• Work with public bodies to reduce legal compliance constraints: storage for data is in some cases defined by law and not by company choice

</details>

<a id="3.5.7"></a>
## 面向可持续性的敏捷方法

原文：3.5.7. Agile Methodologies for Sustainability。PDF 物理页：[54, 55, 56, 57]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

敏捷方法是一种基于持续改进的经验性方法，旨在适应变化的情境。项目团队必须对偶发情况和变化的现实做出反应。敏捷性通过把业务深度融入短开发周期，确保数字产品尽可能贴近其实际使用，从而避免交付的应用程序中有很大一部分最初期望的功能未被使用。这一方法已被形式化为四项价值观和十二条原则。

其中六条原则对于制定切实可行的解决方案似乎特别相关：

- **有用的价值处于方法的核心**。业务负责人（Business Owner）的角色已经改变：他们不再只是在开发过程之外提出请求，而成为交付物的共同责任人——除其他事项外，还要解释其需求的原因、量化其价值、为功能排定优先级，并度量业务影响。
- **重复性**：短周期、频繁发布到生产环境。交付检查必须是具体的：规格、文档、理论指标在展示出可运行的确凿证据之前没有价值。一旦出错，纠正也更容易。
- **简单性**：系统必须以最简单的方式满足需要，因为该系统在未来的迭代中还将被“重构”。这种简单性常与反浪费原则相关联——反浪费也是近年来在 IT 组织中推行的“精益”（Lean）实践的核心原则。
- **自主且负责的团队**：参与者可以自由选择解决方案与实现方式，并对之承担完全的集体责任。这种自由是在企业架构标准与需求所定义的框架内行使的。3.3 节所述的可持续数字文化是使其奏效的前提。
- **持续改进**：与重复性概念互补，生产借助用户反馈以及为实现这些改进所实施的实践，逐步得到改进。
- **度量**：度量“价值点”、复杂度、速度、质量等。为引导产品开发，所使用的许多指标被设定为事实性且公开的。

要交付可持续信息系统，环境影响的限制必须像使用价值一样得到业务单元的支持，并被纳入决策过程（验收、优先级排序等）。重复交付使得可以快速度量这些影响——在测试阶段将其自动化，并在持续改进过程中加以考虑，以便在后续迭代中降低碳足迹。把这些指标公开并纳入产品管理，可强化团队的责任感及其在开发低环境影响产品中的自主性。

**实施“节制型”敏捷方法的条件**

要实施这一新方法，需要若干工具与催化剂，它们源自公司实施可持续性的战略意愿；例如：

- 度量的手段；
- 可比较的基准；
- 由架构所刻画的各项需求与指南；
- 但首先且最重要的是，在 IT 高管的支持下提升团队对该主题的认识。

然而，成功的关键在于找到兼顾业务需求与环境需求的折中方案。这是立即启动生态设计方法的必要且充分条件——其反馈将有助于在更大范围内推展并调整部署。

关于持续度量的必要性，值得一提的是：根据对同一互联网浏览（即提供相同功能服务）所作的精确且可复现的度量，某些互联网浏览器在用户侧消耗的能量几乎是其他浏览器的两倍 [Greenspector]。

**DevOps：处于信息系统实施与运营的交叉点**

DevOps 是指把运营（生产与支持）所产生的约束与非功能需求，纳入 IT 的选择、优先级排序与开发过程，并在部署前确保系统的可运营性。

CI/CD 是 DevOps 战略的组成部分，它们提出了一个根本性问题：它们使源代码能够持续集成到信息系统中，提供端到端环境，以及 7×24 小时运行的多个处理链。它们为了更频繁、更高质量的交付而增加了能耗。

这引出了问题：如何控制这种因更强大的计算机机架和部署自动化软件的低成本可用性而产生的反弹效应？如何按关键性调整服务水平？DevOps 支持信息系统可持续性的条件是什么？

以下示例展示了某些方法如何设法在 IT 的敏捷性与碳韧性之间取得平衡。

> **示例 3-1**
>
> 以下成果交付于一家大型银行机构的网站，该网站每月有 1,400 万次连接。在该网站的一次重大版本升级期间，一次环境影响度量活动使得优化项被注入待办列表（backlog），并产生了以下结果：
>
> - 用户侧消耗的能量：**22%**（原文未说明该百分比是降幅还是剩余比例）；
> - 2G连接下的显示时间：**68%**（原文未明确写“减少”）（从 46 秒降至 15 秒）；
> - 对服务器的请求：**74%**（原文未说明比例口径），因此在同等服务水平下可大幅缩减服务器基础设施。

图3-8：可持续信息系统的交付；The Shift Project工作组产出。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-8](../figures/G242/fig3-8.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 敏捷交付需把环境考量融入需求、反馈与质量活动。
  - 适用边界：所列六项为指南选择及释义，不宣称它们是敏捷宣言完整原文。
  - PDF 55：Six of these principles seem particularly relevant to develop practical solutions:
  - PDF 54：The Agile method, an empirical approach based on continuous improvement, was developed to adapt to changing context. Project teams must react to contingencies and changing realities. Agility ensures that the digital product is produced as close as possible to its actual use thanks to the strong integration of the business into short development cycles. It prevents the delivery of applications with a large part of the initially desired functions unused. This approach has been formalized under four values and 12 principles.
  - PDF 55：To deliver a sustainable information system, limitation of the environmental impact must be supported by the business unit in the same way as the use value and be integrated into the decision- making processes (acceptance, prioritization, etc.). The repetitive deliveries enable rapid measurements of these impacts, by automating them during the testing phase, and by taking them into consideration during the continuous improvement process to reduce the carbon footprint in later iterations. Making these indicators public and integrating them into product management strengthens the responsibility of the team and its autonomy in the development of products with limited environmental impacts.
- 案例报告用户侧能耗22%的数字，但原文没有明确降幅符号。
  - 适用边界：不生成22%已证实减排结论，也不能据此设通用阈值。
  - PDF 57：• 22% energy consumed on the user side
- 案例显示2G显示时间由46秒至15秒，同时列68%。
  - 适用边界：可以识别显示时间减少；68%的取整与口径按源文保留，不把其替代CO2减幅。
  - PDF 57：• 68% display time in 2G connection (from 46 seconds to 15 seconds)
- 案例报告服务器请求74%的数字和潜在基础设施缩减。
  - 适用边界：百分比未明确方向；请求变化不直接证明等比例服务器或碳排放减少。
  - PDF 57：• 74% requests to servers and therefore a possibility of greatly reducing the server infrastructure for the same level of service

**G242:p0054:c014 · PDF 54**

3.5.7. Agile Methodologies for Sustainability

**G242:p0054:c015 · PDF 54**

The Agile method, an empirical approach based on continuous improvement, was developed to adapt to changing context. Project teams must react to contingencies and changing realities. Agility ensures that the digital product is produced as close as possible to its actual use thanks to the strong integration of the business into short development cycles. It prevents the delivery of applications with a large part of the initially desired functions unused. This approach has been formalized under four values and 12 principles.

**G242:p0055:c000 · PDF 55**

Six of these principles seem particularly relevant to develop practical solutions:

**G242:p0055:c001 · PDF 55**

• Useful value is at the center of the approach

**G242:p0055:c002 · PDF 55**

The role of the Business Owner has changed. They are no longer simply making requests outside of the development process. They become co-responsible for deliverables, among other things by explaining the reason for their needs, quantifying their value, prioritizing the functionalities, and measuring the business impact.

**G242:p0055:c003 · PDF 55**

• Repetitive aspect: short cycles with frequent releases to production

**G242:p0055:c004 · PDF 55**

The delivery check must be concrete: specifications, documentation, theoretical indicators have no value until tangible proof of operation is demonstrated. In case of error, it becomes easier to correct.

**G242:p0055:c005 · PDF 55**

• Simplicity: the system must meet needs in the simplest way as this system is also called to be “refactored” in future iterations

**G242:p0055:c006 · PDF 55**

This simplicity is often associated with the principle of anti-waste, a core principle of “Lean” practices also deployed within IT organizations in recent years.

**G242:p0055:c007 · PDF 55**

• Autonomous and responsible team: actors are free to choose solutions and implementations for which they have full collective responsibility

**G242:p0055:c008 · PDF 55**

It is a freedom exercised within a framework defined by Enterprise Architecture standards and requirements. The sustainable digital culture as described in Section 3.3 is a pre-requisite to make it work.

**G242:p0055:c009 · PDF 55**

• Continuous improvements: complementary to the repetition concept, production is improved incrementally using user feedback, as well as the practices implemented to achieve these improvements

**G242:p0055:c010 · PDF 55**

• Measurement: measures “value points”, complexity, velocity, quality, etc.

**G242:p0055:c011 · PDF 55**

To steer product development, many of the used indicators are intended to be factual and public.

**G242:p0055:c012 · PDF 55**

To deliver a sustainable information system, limitation of the environmental impact must be supported by the business unit in the same way as the use value and be integrated into the decision- making processes (acceptance, prioritization, etc.). The repetitive deliveries enable rapid measurements of these impacts, by automating them during the testing phase, and by taking them into consideration during the continuous improvement process to reduce the carbon footprint in later iterations. Making these indicators public and integrating them into product management strengthens the responsibility of the team and its autonomy in the development of products with limited environmental impacts.

**G242:p0056:c000 · PDF 56**

Figure 3-8: Sustainable Information System Delivery (Source: The Shift Project, Production of the Working

**G242:p0056:c001 · PDF 56**

Group)

**G242:p0056:c002 · PDF 56**

Conditions to Implement a “Sufficient” Agile Approach

**G242:p0056:c003 · PDF 56**

To implement this new approach, several tools and catalysts are necessary, stemming from the strategic desire of the company to implement sustainability; for example:

**G242:p0056:c004 · PDF 56**

• Means to measure

**G242:p0056:c005 · PDF 56**

• Comparative benchmarks

**G242:p0056:c006 · PDF 56**

• Requirements and guidelines characterized by the architecture

**G242:p0056:c007 · PDF 56**

• But also, and above all, raising the awareness of teams on the subject with the support of IT executives

**G242:p0056:c008 · PDF 56**

The key to success, however, lies in finding compromises to align business and environmental requirements. It is a necessary and sufficient condition to immediately initiate eco-design approaches, whose feedback will help rollout and adjust the deployment on a larger scale.

**G242:p0056:c009 · PDF 56**

Regarding the need for continuous measurement, let us mention that according to precise and reproducible measurements for the same internet browsing, meaning the same functional service provided, some internet browsers consume almost twice as much energy on the user side as others [Greenspector].

**G242:p0057:c000 · PDF 57**

DevOps at the Crossroads of IS Implementation and Operation

**G242:p0057:c001 · PDF 57**

DevOps consists of integrating the constraints and non-functional needs resulting from Operations (production and support) in the process of choosing, prioritizing, developing IT, and ensuring the operability of the system before deployment.

**G242:p0057:c002 · PDF 57**

CI/CD being components of a DevOps strategy, they raise a fundamental question. They enable continuous integration of the source code into the Information System, end-to-end environments, and multiple processing chains operating 24/7. They are increasing energy consumption for a more frequent and more qualitative delivery.

**G242:p0057:c003 · PDF 57**

This raises questions: how to control this rebound effect due to the low-cost availability of ever more powerful computer racks and deployment automation software? How to adapt the level of service according to the criticality? What is the condition for DevOps to support the sustainability of the Information System?

**G242:p0057:c004 · PDF 57**

The following example shows how some approaches manage to balance the agility of IT on the one hand, and its carbon resilience on the other hand.

**G242:p0057:c005 · PDF 57**

Example 3-1

**G242:p0057:c006 · PDF 57**

The following result was delivered on the website of a large banking institution with 14 million monthly connections. During a major version upgrade of the website, a campaign to measure the environmental impact allowed the injection into the backlog of optimizations that produced the following results:

**G242:p0057:c007 · PDF 57**

• 22% energy consumed on the user side

**G242:p0057:c008 · PDF 57**

• 68% display time in 2G connection (from 46 seconds to 15 seconds)

**G242:p0057:c009 · PDF 57**

• 74% requests to servers and therefore a possibility of greatly reducing the server infrastructure for the same level of service

</details>

<a id="3.5.8"></a>
## 以可持续方式开发

原文：3.5.8. Develop in a Sustainable Way。PDF 物理页：[57, 58]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

正如 Paul Mobbs 所言：“使计算机硬件不仅仅是一堆昂贵的电子电路的，是我们用来赋予机器目的的软件。”[Mobbs, 2012] 在信息系统的核心，我们既找到作为物质部分的数字设备，也找到使与人相关的流程得以交互的代码。

数字转型的加速带来了对加快代码生产、简化开发以使其可被更多参与者掌握，以及为合格人员短缺寻找替代方案的要求。这种加速往往以牺牲质量为代价，语言因此变得更易上手，但也更加消耗资源。然而，优化设备使用的关键就在代码之中——无论是数据存储、处理，还是网络流量。

首先要处理的议题是开发语言的选择，它是有影响的，如《跨编程语言的能效：能源、时间与内存如何相关？》[ResearchGate, 2017] 所述。

市场上已有一些良好的可持续开发实践，但远未完整，也未被系统性地采用。Tim Frick 的《为可持续而设计》[Frick, 2016] 或 Frédéric Bordage 的《Web 生态设计：115 项最佳实践》[Bordage, 2019] 提供了相关最佳实践；3.3 节则提出了一个旨在触达开发人员的方法。

实现可持续 IT，将要求在软件公司、数字服务提供方、使用数字技术的组织、开发团队，以及高等教育与终身学习领域中普遍推行可持续开发实践。

可持续开发实践将要求：

- **建立对代码生产及其使用的环境影响的度量**。它必须对开发人员与项目团队可及，以便持续管理与优化。
- **在着手新开发之前系统地考虑节俭性**：确保该功能尚未被编写过，且确实会被使用。
- **在代码使用期间实施并自动化环境影响测试**；为此，Greenspector 为移动应用提供了解决方案 [Greenspector]。
- **使用并贡献开源项目**，以避免不必要地增加软件制造的环境成本。
- **积累并积极分享良好实践**，不仅针对移动应用或 Web 前端的开发，也针对数据处理与 AI。

一个共识正在形成：高质量的代码通常就是可持续的代码，在成本和监控两方面都更轻。它为用户提供更好的服务质量，也更容易维护。

为支持高质量与可持续的编码（这类项目往往更耗时），必须着力展示其在运营期间（即超越项目成本、通常跨越数年）在能源、服务质量与维护方面的收益。最后，3.5.7 节所述的方法应减少需要开发的功能数量，并为其他工作释放资源。总之，我们必须“少开发，但开发得更好”。

原文措辞说明：本节“best practices for Section 3.3 proposes …”语句缺失衔接成分；上文按软件开发良好实践及第3.3节培训语境理解，未将缺失成分作为独立规范。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 开发应测量并优化计算、传输、存储和设备使用。
  - 适用边界：语言、框架或图像格式需结合具体服务评估，不将示例排序作为所有任务的强制技术选择。

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0057:c010 · PDF 57**

3.5.8. Develop in a Sustainable Way

**G242:p0057:c011 · PDF 57**

As Paul Mobbs says, “What makes computer hardware more than an expensive collection of electronic circuits is the software we use to give the machine a purpose” [Mobbs, 2012]. At the heart of the information system, we find digital equipment for material parts, and the code enabling interface with human processes.

**G242:p0057:c012 · PDF 57**

The acceleration of Digital Transformation came with requirements to speed up code production, simplify development to make it accessible to more players, and find alternatives to the shortage of competent profiles. This acceleration has often been done to the detriment of quality, and languages have therefore become more accessible but also more resource intensive. However, the key to optimize equipment use lies within the code, whether for data storage, processing, or network flows.

**G242:p0058:c000 · PDF 58**

The first topic to address is the choice of the development language that has an impact, as explained in “Energy Efficiency Across Programming Languages: How do Energy, Time, and Memory Relate?” [ResearchGate 2017].

**G242:p0058:c001 · PDF 58**

Some good sustainable development practices are available on the market but are far from being complete and used systematically. Tim Frick’s Designing for Sustainability [Frick, 2016] or Frédéric Bordage’s “Web Eco-Design: The 115 Best Practices” [Bordage, 2019] offer for example best practices for Section 3.3 proposes an approach designed to reach developers.

**G242:p0058:c002 · PDF 58**

Achieving sustainable IT will require the generalization of sustainable development practices among software companies, digital service providers, organizations using digital technologies, teams of developers, and those in higher education and engaged in lifelong learning.

**G242:p0058:c003 · PDF 58**

Sustainable development practices will call for:

**G242:p0058:c004 · PDF 58**

• The establishment of a measurement of the environmental impact of the code production and its use

**G242:p0058:c005 · PDF 58**

It must be accessible to developers and project teams for continuous management and optimization.

**G242:p0058:c006 · PDF 58**

• The systematic consideration of frugality before embarking on a new development: ensure that the functionality has not already been coded and that it will effectively be used

**G242:p0058:c007 · PDF 58**

• The implementation and automation of environmental impact tests of the code during its use; as such, Greenspector offers solutions for mobile applications [Greenspector]

**G242:p0058:c008 · PDF 58**

• The use of and the contribution to open source projects to avoid unnecessarily increases of the environmental costs of software manufacturing

**G242:p0058:c009 · PDF 58**

• The capitalization and the active share of good practices, not only for the development of mobile apps or web front ends, but also for data processing and AI

**G242:p0058:c010 · PDF 58**

A consensus emerges on the fact that quality code is generally sustainable code, lighter to operate both in cost and in supervision. It offers a better quality of service to users and is also easier to maintain.

**G242:p0058:c011 · PDF 58**

To favor quality and sustainable coding, which often tend to be more time-consuming projects, it is necessary to focus on demonstrating gains in energy, service quality and maintenance during operations (i.e., beyond the cost of the project, often over several years). Finally, the approach described in Section 3.5.7 should reduce the number of functions to be developed and free up resources for others. To sum up, we will have to develop less, but better.

</details>

<a id="3.6"></a>
## 从摇篮到坟墓度量信息系统的环境影响

原文：3.6. Measuring the Environmental Impact of the Information System from Cradle to Grave。PDF 物理页：[58, 59, 60]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

建立企业信息系统环境影响的**穷尽且量级正确**的度量，对正确实施战略并长期保持努力至关重要。应考虑 ICT 环境影响的完整范围；但经验表明，第一阶段完成包含用电与制造的、基于科学的完整碳足迹核算，已足以驱动大多数架构决策。随着企业在数字可持续转型中推进，并视业务模式而定，应定期检查是否需要增加其他指标。然而，在时间与财务资源同样有限的世界里，度量应尽可能简单，以提供基于科学的决策指导，并把资源留给激活减排杠杆——非常详细、覆盖全范围的环境足迹计算可能相当复杂。

碳足迹计算本身复杂，因此需要务实、迭代的方法，聚焦趋势与量级。它应使企业能快速启动有效的减排行动，而不是纠结于所报告信息的精确度。

减缓气候变化的时限与所需努力规模几乎没有犯错空间：度量确保新举措在数量上贡献于可持续目标；**度量是实施治理的前提**。

> **注（CO2e）**：CO2e（二氧化碳当量，CO2-eq）是一种度量指标，用于基于全球变暖潜能值（GWP）比较各种温室气体的排放，其方法是将其他气体的量换算为具有相同 GWP 的二氧化碳当量。参见：ec.europa.eu/eurostat/statistics-explained/index.php?title=Glossary:Carbon_dioxide_equivalent。

> **示例（法国）**：在法国，员工超过 500 人（法国本土）或超过 250 人（海外省）的私法法人必须提供碳足迹。强制范围涵盖受法国法律管辖的法人实体在法国领土上的活动，即“范围 1”（法人活动所需固定与移动源产生的直接排放）与“范围 2”（法人活动所需电力、热或蒸汽消费相关的间接排放）。“范围 3”（法人活动间接产生的其他排放）的申报自 2023 年起强制，但限于组织温室气体总排放中具有显著重要性的子类。参见：apc-climat.fr/bilan-ges/reglementation/。范围的定义另见图 3-9。

![图 3-9：GHG Protocol 的范围与价值链排放概览 [Value Chain]（据原文重绘示意）](../sources/retained/togaf-standard-10th-zh/figs/g242_fig3-9.svg)

支持组织信息系统的 ICT 设备环境评估具有以下特点：

- 对**范围 1** 直接排放影响较小（例如空调制冷剂泄漏、数据中心应急发电机燃烧的燃料）；
- 温室气体排放的很大部分与**范围 2** 电力相关排放有关，来自其数据中心与办公硬件的用电；
- 如《Lean-ICT：迈向数字节制》[Shift Project, 2019] 所示，**超过一半的碳足迹在范围 3**，即与从供应商采购商品和服务相关。该类别受监管义务影响较小、针对性较弱，且因由其他利益相关者运营而更不透明；
- 信息系统环境评估由温室气体排放、使用的金属（有限自然资源）、为开采金属而移动的土方量、水资源，以及产生的废弃物与污染构成。对多数组织而言，其所有其他影响的强度常与范围 3 温室气体排放高度相关。因此，彻底度量温室气体排放是触发目标与杠杆设定的前提；各组织在推进过程中将需要评估是否需要额外指标。

图3-9：GHG Protocol范围与价值链排放概览 [Value Chain]。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-9](../figures/G242/fig3-9.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 首轮碳基线可支持许多优先决策，之后应扩展其他环境影响。
  - 适用边界：碳指标只是起点，不能代表金属、水、污染的全部影响。
  - PDF 59：consideration. But in a first step, the experience shows that a complete and science-based carbon footprint calculation including electricity consumption and manufacturing is enough to drive most architecture decisions. As enterprises move ahead on the digital sustainability transformation and depending on their business model, they should check regularly if other indicators should be added. Nevertheless, in a world where time and financial resources are also finite, it is important to keep the measure as simple as possible to provide science-based guidance for decision and to keep said resources to activate levers to reduce the footprint. Indeed, environmental footprint calculation at a very detailed level and covering the full scope can be quite complex.
- 缺乏精确数据时可先做数量级估计，逐步提高质量。
  - 适用边界：估计值要标明方法、不确定性和缺数，不能伪装实测精度。
  - PDF 59：Calculating a carbon footprint is by itself complex, and thus requires a pragmatic iterative approach which focuses on trends and orders of magnitude. It should enable the enterprise to initiate effective reduction actions quickly, rather than working on the precision of the reported information.
- CO2e基于全球变暖潜势换算不同温室气体。
  - 适用边界：CO2与CO2e不直接互换；需记录气体范围、GWP版本及时间尺度。
  - PDF 59：CO2e or CO2 equivalent ( CO2-eq), is a metric measure used to compare the emissions from various GHGs based on their Global Warming Potential (GWP), by converting amounts of other gases to the equivalent amount of carbon dioxide with the same GWP.
- 法国人数门槛及2023 Scope3要求是指南的辖区例子。
  - 适用边界：当前适用法规需另行核实，不从本地指南自动判定企业已合规。
  - PDF 59：In France, for instance, providing a carbon footprint is mandatory for legal persons governed by private law employing more than 500 people for mainland France or more than 250 people for overseas France. The mandatory nature covers the activities of the legal entity subject to French law on French territories; i.e., the “Scope 1” perimeters (the direct emissions produced by fixed and mobile sources needed for the activities of the legal entity) and “Scope 2” perimeters (indirect emissions related to electricity consumption, heat, or steam, needed for the activities of the legal entity). The declaration of “Scope 3”, other emissions indirectly produced by the activities of the legal entity, is mandatory since 2023 but limited to the subcategories of significative importance amongst the organization’s total GHG emissions.
- Scope1可能包括应急发电燃料及制冷剂泄漏。
  - 适用边界：ICT Scope1通常较小不等于零，也不能遗漏备用设备。
  - PDF 60：• It has a minor impact on Scope 1 direct emissions (e.g., leaks of refrigerants from air conditioning, fuel burned by emergency power generators in data centers)
- Scope2为组织用电相关的间接排放。
  - 适用边界：供应商用电不能不分组织核算边界直接并入客户Scope2。
  - PDF 60：• An important part of its GHG emissions is related to Scope 2 electricity-related emissions, due to the consumption of its data centers and workplace hardware
- Scope3包括设备制造与外包服务等价值链影响。
  - 适用边界：相同活动的直接服务报告与支出估计不可重复相加。
  - PDF 60：• As demonstrated in “Lean-ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety” [Shift Project, 2019], more than half of its carbon footprint is in Scope 3, which means that it is related to the purchase of goods and services from suppliers

3.6. Measuring the Environmental Impact of the Information

**G242:p0058:c013 · PDF 58**

System from Cradle to Grave

Establishing a measure of the environmental impact of an enterprise’s information system that is exhaustive and with the right orders of magnitude is crucial to properly implement the strategy and to maintain the effort over time. The full scope of the ICT environmental impact should be taken into

consideration. As enterprises move ahead on the digital sustainability transformation and depending on their business model, they should check regularly if other indicators should be added. Nevertheless, in a world where time and financial resources are also finite, it is important to keep the measure as simple as possible to provide science-based guidance for decision and to keep said resources to activate levers to reduce the footprint. Indeed, environmental footprint calculation at a very detailed level and covering the full scope can be quite complex.

**G242:p0059:c001 · PDF 59**

Calculating a carbon footprint is by itself complex, and thus requires a pragmatic iterative approach which focuses on trends and orders of magnitude. It should enable the enterprise to initiate effective reduction actions quickly, rather than working on the precision of the reported information.

**G242:p0059:c002 · PDF 59**

The deadlines and magnitude of the effort required to mitigate climate change leave little room for error: measurement ensures that new initiatives contribute to sustainability goals quantitatively. Measurement is a prerequisite to implementing governance.

In France, for instance, providing a carbon footprint is mandatory for legal persons governed by private law employing more than 500 people for mainland France or more than 250 people for overseas France. The mandatory nature covers the activities of the legal entity subject to French law on French territories; i.e., the “Scope 1” perimeters (the direct emissions produced by fixed and mobile sources needed for the activities of the legal entity) and “Scope 2” perimeters (indirect emissions related to electricity consumption, heat, or steam, needed for the activities of the legal entity). The declaration of “Scope 3”, other emissions indirectly produced by the activities of the legal entity, is mandatory since 2023 but limited to the subcategories of significative importance amongst the organization’s total GHG emissions.

The environmental assessment of ICT equipment supporting the information system of an organization has the following specific features:

**G242:p0060:c002 · PDF 60**

• It has a minor impact on Scope 1 direct emissions (e.g., leaks of refrigerants from air conditioning, fuel burned by emergency power generators in data centers)

**G242:p0060:c003 · PDF 60**

• An important part of its GHG emissions is related to Scope 2 electricity-related emissions, due to the consumption of its data centers and workplace hardware

**G242:p0060:c004 · PDF 60**

This category is less affected by regulatory obligations, less targeted and is more opaque because it is operated by other stakeholders.

**G242:p0060:c006 · PDF 60**

For most organizations, the intensity of all their other impacts is often highly correlated to GHG emissions of Scope 3. Therefore, a thorough measurement of GHG emissions is a prerequisite to trigger the setting of targets and levers. <a id="3.6.1"></a>
## 建立并共享“摇篮到坟墓”的信息系统碳足迹度量

原文：3.6.1. Build and Share Information System Carbon Footprint Measure from Cradle to Grave。PDF 物理页：[61, 62, 63, 64]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

信息系统重塑项目群应从建立**年度环境评估**开始，考虑与以下各项相关的温室气体排放：

- 组织运营的数据中心的用电——考虑电力生产区域电力结构的排放强度（kgCO₂/kWh）；
- 组织运营的服务器与网络设备的制造；
- 云上可用服务的使用，既涉及用电，也涉及硬件制造；
- 数字服务的外包，例如营销活动、咨询、数据处理等；
- 公共互联网网络的使用；
- 特定网络供应商与移动运营商的使用；
- 为组织工作的员工与服务提供方所使用的设备（计算机、电话、屏幕等）的使用与制造。

对这套温室气体核算进行两到三年的管理，将实现动态管理，并可检验组织信息系统的环境影响是在上升（如市场上所观察到的）还是在下降。

上图的模型受到 AXA 集团 2020 年在数字可持续性项目群中为度量和管理 ICT 影响所做工作的启发。在该图中，“公共网络”也可指移动运营商的资产。

为务实起见，领导转型的团队需要做出近似，并在度量中区分优先级。事实上，某些数据（更常见于范围 3）可能难以获得。在这种情况下，一种变通方法是在等待供应商提供数据的同时先计算数量级。**缺少精确数据绝不应导致不做评估。** 例如，对于在碳披露项目 [CDP] 中公布其 CO2 排放的提供方，可以简单地依据其全球营收、全球排放以及从本组织获得的营收来估算范围 3 排放：

> 范围 3 排放 =（提供方来自本组织的营收 / 提供方全球营收）× 提供方全球排放

一旦组织达到这一成熟阶段，最好把 CO2 排放作为引导指标来聚焦。它代表一个核心指标，共享后可与其他组织及国家目标进行比较。它代表组织需在其全部范围内建立的第一个重要成果。清单一旦完成，若供应商数据可得，就很容易用关于金属使用、水资源或污染的新指标来完善这份首份报告。

管理碳抵消也是有意义的，但应单独进行。正确的做法是“能减少的减少，不能减少的再抵消”。监视减排的唯一方式，是单独跟踪碳排放。

这一端到端度量工作将揭示“范围悲剧”（the tragedy of the scopes）——它部分解释了为何尽管公布了碳足迹，组织仍未能控制其信息系统的环境影响。

- 用电的优化往往以非常密集的设备更新周期为代价：范围 2 下降，但范围 3 上升。
- 把服务从组织自营的数据中心迁移到公共云，同样会降低范围 2、提高范围 3。

最后，把数字行业的工作外包到劳动力成本更低的国家具有财务优势。然而，其中一些国家可能正处于能源转型的起步阶段，其以 kg CO2/kWh 计的电力结构会以数个数量级影响平衡。关于该主题的更多信息，见 3.7 节。

**数据资料库与度量方法示例**

以下是与计算机设备及电力的环境影响相关的一些公共数据来源。它们对于结合组织清单建立首批度量至关重要。

> **示例 3-2**
>
> ADEME（法国生态转型署）在其网站上免费提供以下数据：
>
> - 电力——范围 2：电网的电力结构、生产方式；
> - 采购的商品——范围 3：电气、电子与计算机设备等产品“从摇篮到坟墓”的排放。

> **示例 3-3**
>
> 数字环境参照标准（REN）[REN] 随《Lean-ICT：迈向数字节制》[Shift Project, 2019] 一同提供。REN 向所有人提供生产阶段与执行阶段的温室气体排放及金属使用数据。

> **示例 3-4**
>
> Ecoinfo 是一个服务集群通用数据服务（GDS），在法国汇集工程师、研究人员以及研究与高等教育学生，围绕一个共同目标：“行动起来，减少 ICT 的（负面）环境与社会影响。”除其他工作外，它与 Ecodia 一起提供一个免费可用的服务，可用于计算信息系统的碳足迹以及该服务所依据的数据。
>
> 在供应商一侧，即使方法尚未标准化、未经审计且不可审计，仍可提及以下信息：
>
> - Apple® 提供的设备数据（以 kg CO2-eq 计），涵盖生产、使用、运输与回收；
> - Dell™ 提供的服务器数据（以 kg CO2-eq 计），同样涵盖生产、使用、运输与回收；
> - HP™ 提供的工作站、打印机、屏幕、投影仪等设备数据（以 kg CO2 计）；
> - Microsoft® 提供的耐久性计算器，尽管主要用以突出 Azure® 产品，但提供了 Azure 服务的 MtCO2-eq，并区分抵消与云架构所产生的互联网网络流量的影响；
> - Google® 在其专门的可持续性网站上提供温室气体排放、材料等信息。

> **示例 3-5**
>
> NegaOctet 是一个面向 ICT 资产的碳数据库，用于评估和改进数字服务的环境绩效。该资料库由负责任数字领域的专家受法国环境机构 ADEME 委托共同构建。NegaOctet 提出一个数据库，包含 **1,500** 个组件与设备，按四个粒度级别分类。每台设备关联多达 **30** 个影响因子：从非生物资源枯竭到全球变暖与富营养化。

> **示例 3-6**
>
> Boavizta 是一个法国的开放式碳数据库与 ICT 资产工作组。它是一个非政府组织（NGO），提供开放数据库，并分享关于 ICT 资产环境足迹的最佳实践。

> **示例 3-7**
>
> 《ICT 行业指南》[GHG ICT] 为 ICT 产品与 ICT 服务的温室气体排放计算提供指南与核算方法。它建立在《温室气体核算体系》之上并与之保持一致。
>
> OFP 目前正在制定一套用于表示排放数据的标准草案；该标准发布后，将成为坚实的基础，使组织能够以一致、透明且可共享的方式建立其 ICT 与非 ICT 排放数据，如 The Open Group Open Footprint®（OFP）论坛所述。
>
> 在供应商一侧，即使方法尚未标准化、不可审计，仍可获得以下信息：
>
> - Apple、Dell 或 HP 等制造商提供其设备的 kgCO2e 数据，涵盖生产、使用、运输与回收；
> - Microsoft Azure 或 Google 等云服务提供方提供仪表板，呈现客户组织所采购服务相关的碳排放。

图3-10：信息系统的年度环境评估；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-10](../figures/G242/fig3-10.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 从年度评估开始建立信息系统温室气体基线。
  - 适用边界：年度量、一次性制造量和瞬时容量不可直接相加。
  - PDF 61：The information system reshaping program should start with establishing a yearly environmental assessment considering the GHG emissions related to:
- 电力消耗应关联电力生产区域的排放强度。
  - 适用边界：核算需单位、期间、地理、电力核算方法和因子来源一致。
  - PDF 61：• The electricity consumption of the data centers operated by the organization: taking into consideration emissions intensity of the electricity mix of the electricity production area in kg CO 2/kWh
- 公共云服务应考虑运行及建造影响。
  - 适用边界：云迁移可能改变Scope分类，不自动降低总生命周期排放。
  - PDF 61：• The use of services available on the cloud, related both to electricity consumption and manufacture of hardware
- 为组织工作的员工及服务提供方设备均需纳入适当范围。
  - 适用边界：组织所有权不是唯一边界；需避免与供应商总量重复。
  - PDF 61：• The use and manufacture of devices (computers, telephones, screens, etc.) used by employees and service providers working for the organization
- 应连续两至三年管理年度温室气体核算，观察趋势。
  - 适用边界：不是每两至三年才测一次；跨年变化还需解释边界和因子变化。
  - PDF 61：The management of this GHG accounting over two to three years will enable dynamic management and to check whether the environmental impact of the organization’s information system is increasing, as observed on the market, or decreasing.
- 缺供应商数据时可先以营收比例做数量级估计。
  - 适用边界：该方法为替代估计，不能标成客户服务直接测量。
  - PDF 62：To be pragmatic, the team leading the transformation needs to make approximations and to prioritize effort in the measurement. Indeed, some data, more often on Scope 3, can be difficult to obtain. In this case, a workaround is to calculate the orders of magnitude while waiting for the suppliers to provide the data. The absence of precise data should never result in no evaluation. For example, for providers publishing their CO2 emissions in the Carbon Disclosure Project [CDP], one can simply evaluate the Scope 3 emissions based on their global revenue, their global emissions, and the revenue derived from one’s organization:
- 供应商全球排放乘以客户营收占供应商全球营收的比例，得到粗略分配值。
  - 适用边界：营收需币种、期间、组织范围一致且分母大于零；比例不代表因果减排。
  - PDF 62：Scope 3 emissions = (Provider revenue from the organization / Provider Global revenue) * Provider Global emissions
- 碳排放与抵消必须分别跟踪。
  - 适用边界：抵消额不得藏在毛排放下降中，不把排放减抵消作为原始足迹。
  - PDF 62：It is also relevant to manage offsets, but this should be done separately. The right approach is “reduce what you can, offset what you cannot”. The only way to monitor reduction is to track carbon emissions separately.
- 更换设备可能降低Scope2却提高Scope3。
  - 适用边界：采购决策需比较制造与运行的全生命周期，不能只选更低运行功率。
  - PDF 62：• The optimization of electricity consumption often comes at the cost of very intense equipment renewal cycles: Scope 2 decreases but Scope 3 increases
- 自营迁移到云会改变Scope2/3归属。
  - 适用边界：迁移前后需统一组织边界和服务量再判断变化。
  - PDF 62：• Moving services from data centers operated by organizations to the public cloud also reduces Scope 2 and increases Scope 3
- OFP标准草案在原文中尚待可用。
  - 适用边界：保留历史状态；不能推导当前草案/标准或软件实现状态。
  - PDF 64：The OFP is currently working on a draft set of standards for representing emissions data which, when published, would be a firm foundation on which organizations can base their ICT and non- ICT emissions data in a consistent, transparent, and shareable way as explained in The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum.

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0061:c000 · PDF 61**

3.6.1. Build and Share Information System Carbon Footprint Measure from Cradle to Grave

**G242:p0061:c001 · PDF 61**

The information system reshaping program should start with establishing a yearly environmental assessment considering the GHG emissions related to:

**G242:p0061:c002 · PDF 61**

• The electricity consumption of the data centers operated by the organization: taking into consideration emissions intensity of the electricity mix of the electricity production area in kg CO 2/kWh

**G242:p0061:c003 · PDF 61**

• The manufacture of servers and network equipment operated by the organization

**G242:p0061:c004 · PDF 61**

• The use of services available on the cloud, related both to electricity consumption and manufacture of hardware

**G242:p0061:c005 · PDF 61**

• The outsourcing of digital services such as marketing campaigns, counseling, data processing, etc.

**G242:p0061:c006 · PDF 61**

• The use of the public Internet network

**G242:p0061:c007 · PDF 61**

• The use of specific network suppliers and mobile operators

**G242:p0061:c008 · PDF 61**

• The use and manufacture of devices (computers, telephones, screens, etc.) used by employees and service providers working for the organization

**G242:p0061:c009 · PDF 61**

The management of this GHG accounting over two to three years will enable dynamic management and to check whether the environmental impact of the organization’s information system is increasing, as observed on the market, or decreasing.

**G242:p0061:c010 · PDF 61**

Figure 3-10: Yearly Environmental Assessment of the Information System (Source: The Shift Project Lean-ICT

**G242:p0061:c011 · PDF 61**

Work Group)

**G242:p0062:c000 · PDF 62**

The model above is inspired by the work conducted by the AXA Group in 2020 as part of the digital sustainability program to measure and manage the impact of ICT. In this schema, “public network” can also refer to mobile operators’ assets.

**G242:p0062:c001 · PDF 62**

To be pragmatic, the team leading the transformation needs to make approximations and to prioritize effort in the measurement. Indeed, some data, more often on Scope 3, can be difficult to obtain. In this case, a workaround is to calculate the orders of magnitude while waiting for the suppliers to provide the data. The absence of precise data should never result in no evaluation. For example, for providers publishing their CO2 emissions in the Carbon Disclosure Project [CDP], one can simply evaluate the Scope 3 emissions based on their global revenue, their global emissions, and the revenue derived from one’s organization:

**G242:p0062:c002 · PDF 62**

Scope 3 emissions = (Provider revenue from the organization / Provider Global revenue) * Provider Global emissions

**G242:p0062:c003 · PDF 62**

Once organizations reach this maturity stage, it is better to focus on CO2 emissions as a steering indicator. They represent a central indicator which, when shared, enables comparison with others and with national objectives. It represents a first significant effort to be established by the organization over its full scope. Once the inventory is complete, if supplier data becomes available, it will be easy to refine this first report with new indicators regarding the use of metals, water resources, or pollution.

**G242:p0062:c004 · PDF 62**

It is also relevant to manage offsets, but this should be done separately. The right approach is “reduce what you can, offset what you cannot”. The only way to monitor reduction is to track carbon emissions separately.

**G242:p0062:c005 · PDF 62**

This end-to-end measurement exercise will reveal “the tragedy of the scopes” which partly explains the failure of organizations to control the environmental impact of their information system despite the publication of carbon footprints.

**G242:p0062:c006 · PDF 62**

• The optimization of electricity consumption often comes at the cost of very intense equipment renewal cycles: Scope 2 decreases but Scope 3 increases

**G242:p0062:c007 · PDF 62**

• Moving services from data centers operated by organizations to the public cloud also reduces Scope 2 and increases Scope 3

**G242:p0062:c008 · PDF 62**

Finally, the outsourcing of digital industry workers to countries where the cost of labour is lower presents financial advantages. However, some of these countries may be at the beginning of their energy transition and their electricity mix in kg CO2/kWh affects the balance by several orders of magnitude. For more information on this topic, see Section 3.7.

**G242:p0062:c009 · PDF 62**

Examples of Data Repositories and Measure Methodologies

**G242:p0062:c010 · PDF 62**

Here are some sources of public data related to the environmental impact of computer equipment and electricity. They are vital for establishing the first measurements by crossing them with the inventories of organizations.

**G242:p0063:c000 · PDF 63**

Example 3-2

**G242:p0063:c001 · PDF 63**

The ADEME (French Agency for Ecological Transition) provides freely accessible data on its website about:

**G242:p0063:c002 · PDF 63**

• Electricity – Scope 2: mix of electricity network, means of production

**G242:p0063:c003 · PDF 63**

• Purchased goods – Scope 3: emissions from the “cradle to grave” of products such as electrical, electronic, and computer equipment

**G242:p0063:c004 · PDF 63**

Example 3-3

**G242:p0063:c005 · PDF 63**

The Digital Referential Environmental Standard [REN] is made available with Lean-ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety [Shift Project, 2019]. The REN provides everyone with data on GHG emissions and metal use for the production and execution phases.

**G242:p0063:c006 · PDF 63**

Example 3-4

**G242:p0063:c007 · PDF 63**

Ecoinfo is a Services Cluster Generic Data Service (GDS) bringing together engineers, researchers, research, and higher education students in France around a common goal: “Acting to reduce the (negative) environmental and societal impacts of ICTs.” Among other things, with Ecodia, they provide a freely available service, which can be used to calculate the carbon footprint of an information system and the data on which the service is based.

**G242:p0063:c008 · PDF 63**

On the supplier’s side, even if the methods are not yet standardized, audited, and auditable, the following information is mentioned:

**G242:p0063:c009 · PDF 63**

• Data in kg of CO2-eq provided by Apple® on devices, including production, use, transport, and recycling

**G242:p0063:c010 · PDF 63**

• Data in kg of CO2-eq provided by Dell™ on servers, including also production, use, transport, and recycling

**G242:p0063:c011 · PDF 63**

• Data in kg of CO2 provided by HP™ on equipment such as workstations, printers, screens, projectors, etc.

**G242:p0063:c012 · PDF 63**

• The durability calculator provided by Microsoft®, although presented mainly to highlight the Azure® offer, provides the MtCO2-eq of Azure services, distinguishing between compensation and the impact of Internet network flows generated by cloud architectures

**G242:p0063:c013 · PDF 63**

• In its site dedicated to sustainability, Google® provides information on GHG emissions, materials, etc.

**G242:p0064:c000 · PDF 64**

Example 3-5

**G242:p0064:c001 · PDF 64**

NegaOctet is a carbon database for ICT assets assessing and improving the environmental performance of digital services. The repository was co-constructed by responsible digital experts on an order of ADEME, the French environmental agency. NegaOctet proposes a database containing 1,500 components and equipment classified according to four levels of granularity. Each piece of equipment is associated with up to 30 impact factors: from depletion of abiotic resources to global warming and eutrophication.

**G242:p0064:c002 · PDF 64**

Example 3-6

**G242:p0064:c003 · PDF 64**

Boavizta is a French open carbon database and working group for ICT assets. It is a Non- Governmental Organization (NGO) proposing an open database and the sharing of best practices on an environmental ICT assets’ footprint.

**G242:p0064:c004 · PDF 64**

Example 3-7

**G242:p0064:c005 · PDF 64**

The ICT Sector Guidance [GHG ICT] provides guidance and accounting methods for the calculation of GHG emissions for ICT products and ICT services. It is built on, and in conformance with, the GHG Standard.

**G242:p0064:c006 · PDF 64**

The OFP is currently working on a draft set of standards for representing emissions data which, when published, would be a firm foundation on which organizations can base their ICT and non- ICT emissions data in a consistent, transparent, and shareable way as explained in The Open Group Open Footprint® (OFP) Forum.

**G242:p0064:c007 · PDF 64**

On the supplier’s side, even if the methods are not yet standardized and auditable, the following information is available:

**G242:p0064:c008 · PDF 64**

• Manufacturers such as Apple, Dell, or HP provide data in kgCO2e about their devices, including production, use, transport, and recycling

**G242:p0064:c009 · PDF 64**

• Cloud Services Providers such as Microsoft Azure or Google provide dashboards presenting the carbon emissions linked to the services that the client organization is purchasing

</details>

<a id="3.6.2"></a>
## 开展信息系统环境影响度量活动

原文：3.6.2. Develop Activities to Measure the Information System Environmental Impact。PDF 物理页：[64, 65]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

要依靠内部技能完成这一计算，组织可以依靠其员工，和/或由专业的 ICT 咨询公司或专门从事碳足迹报告的公司协助。

为此，负责这些计算的团队除了温室气体数据资料库之外，还必须具备/获得关于信息系统的精确知识。必须获得以下数据：

- 硬件清单数据，例如利用 ICT 运营在信息技术基础设施库（ITIL®）流程框架内建立的 CMDB；
- 数据中心的用电，以及所采购数字服务相关的温室气体排放；
- 数字设备采购历史，最好覆盖两到三年，以便揭示趋势；
- 公共云提供方的数据与报告。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 核算需要设备清单与相关信息系统精确知识。
  - 适用边界：清单不完整时需明确缺口与估算，不输出“全覆盖已验证”。
  - PDF 65：• Data of hardware inventories by using, for example, the CMDB set up by ICT operations as part of Information Technology Infrastructure Library (ITIL®) procedures
- 采购和设备变化应覆盖数年的生命周期信息。
  - 适用边界：以当前设备快照替代制造购置或退役历史会失真。
  - PDF 65：• History of digital material purchase, ideally over two to three years to be able to reveal a trend

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0064:c010 · PDF 64**

3.6.2. Develop Activities to Measure the Information System Environmental Impact

**G242:p0064:c011 · PDF 64**

To perform this calculation with internal skills, the organization can rely on its employees, and/or be assisted by professional ICT consulting companies or companies specializing in carbon footprint reports.

**G242:p0065:c000 · PDF 65**

To achieve this, the team in charge of these calculations must have/acquire precise knowledge of the information system, in addition to the data repositories on GHG. The following data must be obtained:

**G242:p0065:c001 · PDF 65**

• Data of hardware inventories by using, for example, the CMDB set up by ICT operations as part of Information Technology Infrastructure Library (ITIL®) procedures

**G242:p0065:c002 · PDF 65**

• Electricity consumption of the data center and GHG emissions related to purchased digital services

**G242:p0065:c003 · PDF 65**

• History of digital material purchase, ideally over two to three years to be able to reveal a trend

**G242:p0065:c004 · PDF 65**

• Public cloud provider’s data and reporting

</details>

<a id="3.6.3"></a>
## 向利益相关者提供其环境影响度量

原文：3.6.3. Provide to Stakeholders a Measure of their Environmental Impact。PDF 物理页：[65, 66]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

一旦完成了技术组件的温室气体排放度量，就必须把它应用到组织的主要职能上：商品与服务生产的价值链、财务与人力资源等支持职能，以及客户关系职能。这一分解旨在识别影响最大的业务线，从而能够为每个职能确定减排目标。

为此，可以实施 ICT 专业人员所熟悉的中间度量与指标：温室气体排放的增长确实可以与存储量、网络流量以及所执行处理的增长相关联。对于云计算等由第三方交付的服务，当使用方企业不了解细节时，这些中间度量可以使管理层在等待此类数据的发布标准与义务落地期间先行采取行动。

为使利益相关者能够在其决策范围内提出杠杆，并评估相关的潜在减排量，提供一个公司用于计算其数字温室气体排放的通用数据资料库是有益的。这样的数据库不仅能帮助本地负责人节省收集这些输入的时间，还能确保整个组织使用统一的计算基础，从而便于同行评审并启发他人。它还有助于抵御市场上的漂绿传播，并避免过时数据继续在网上可得。

该文件应包含所考虑的每个范围的原始、未经解释的单位数据（单位量参数）及其来源：设备制造商的排放因子（运行与建造阶段）及相关用电、自营数据中心计算得出的排放与消费、公共云服务的排放。为在计算方法上完全透明，还必须传达其他物理数值，例如公司所采用的能源结构参考碳强度。该资料库应作为对下级组织更大范围赋能的一部分来呈现，帮助它们在该主题上实现自主，并支持其自下而上的举措。

**表 3-4：共享碳数据库中应包含的字段（非穷尽清单）**

| 字段名称 | 单位 | 来源类型 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 设备用电 | kWh/年 | 设备制造商 | 需针对公司的平均机型加以明确。 |
| 设备范围 3 排放 | kgCO2e/设备 | 设备制造商 | 同上。 |
| 自营数据中心消费 | kWh/vCPU、kWh/GB，或任何其他存储 KPI | 公司的数据中心管理 | 需依据数据中心能耗、PUE 以及按服务分配的 VM 计算。 |
| 公共云运行与建造排放 | kgCO2e/货币单位 | 云提供方输入（或 CDP 估算；见前文） | 货币指标更便于按实体分配。 |
| 电力生产的碳强度 | kgCO2e/kWh | 国家或国际机构 | 例如 IEA 或 ADEME。 |

图3-11：在不同层面度量信息系统环境影响；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-11](../figures/G242/fig3-11.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。
相关结构化资料：[完整结构化表格](../tables/G242.json)。保留官方表空白、单位、示例状态及异常；实施契约在独立解析层补足。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 环境影响应分配到业务及功能层以支持利益相关者行动。
  - 适用边界：分摊要保留母总量、分配键和共享资源规则。
  - PDF 65：Once the measurement of GHG emissions has been conducted for the technical components, it must be applied to the main functions of the organization, on the value chain for production of goods and services, on finance, human resources support functions, and on customer relations functions. This breakdown aims to identify the lines of business with the highest impact, making it possible to determine emission reduction goals for each function.
- 存储、网络与计算用量可作为易理解的运营指标。
  - 适用边界：这些是活动量/代理指标，未经因子和边界映射不等同kgCO2e。
  - PDF 65：To achieve this, intermediate measures and indicators familiar to ICT professionals can be implemented: the increase of GHG emissions can indeed be linked to the increase in volumes stored, network traffic, and treatments carried out. In the case of services delivered by third parties such as cloud computing, when the details are not known to the user company, these intermediate measures can enable management to act while waiting for publication standards and obligations of this data to be implemented.
- 共享资料库应包含原始单位参数、来源及计算方法。
  - 适用边界：最终汇总图必须可回溯输入、公式、版本及假设，不能只有漂亮图表。
  - PDF 66：This file should include the initial, uninterpreted unitary data with its source for each perimeter considered: emissions factors from device manufacturers (run and build phases) and related electricity consumption, calculated emissions and consumption from on-premise data centers, and emissions from public cloud services. To be fully transparent on the calculation methodologies, other physical values must be communicated, such as the reference carbon intensity of energy mixes considered by the company. This repository should be presented as part of a larger onboarding of suborganizations, to help them become autonomous on the subject and to support their own bottom- up initiatives.
- 表3-4列设备kWh/年、设备制造kgCO2e/台、数据中心kWh/vCPU或GB、云kgCO2e/货币及电力kgCO2e/kWh。
  - 适用边界：部分参数缺时间/分配基础；在填妥前阻止自动计算或横向排名。
  - PDF 66：| Field Name | Unit | Type of Source | Comments |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Device electricity<br>consumption | kWh/year | Device manufacturer | To specify for the company’s<br>average models. |
| Device Scope 3 emissions | kgCOe/device<br>2 | Device manufacturer | Idem. |
| On-premise data center<br>consumption | kWh/vCPU, kWh/GB,<br>or any other storage<br>KPI | Company’s data center<br>management | To be calculated from data<br>center energy consumption,<br>PUE and VMs allocation per<br>service. |
| Public cloud run and build<br>emissions | kgCOe/<br>2<br>monetary unit | Cloud provider inputs (or CDP<br>estimations; see previously) | A monetary indicator is easier<br>to manipulate for entity<br>allocation. |
| Carbon intensity of electricity<br>production | kgCOe/kWh<br>2 | National or international<br>agency | For example, IEA or ADEME. |

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0065:c005 · PDF 65**

3.6.3. Provide to Stakeholders a Measure of their Environmental Impact

**G242:p0065:c006 · PDF 65**

Once the measurement of GHG emissions has been conducted for the technical components, it must be applied to the main functions of the organization, on the value chain for production of goods and services, on finance, human resources support functions, and on customer relations functions. This breakdown aims to identify the lines of business with the highest impact, making it possible to determine emission reduction goals for each function.

**G242:p0065:c007 · PDF 65**

To achieve this, intermediate measures and indicators familiar to ICT professionals can be implemented: the increase of GHG emissions can indeed be linked to the increase in volumes stored, network traffic, and treatments carried out. In the case of services delivered by third parties such as cloud computing, when the details are not known to the user company, these intermediate measures can enable management to act while waiting for publication standards and obligations of this data to be implemented.

**G242:p0065:c008 · PDF 65**

Figure 3-11: Measuring the Environmental Impact of the Information System at Different Levels (Source: The

**G242:p0065:c009 · PDF 65**

Shift Project Lean-ICT Work Group)

**G242:p0065:c010 · PDF 65**

In order to give stakeholders the ability to propose levers on their decision perimeter and to assess the associated potential emissions reductions, it is useful to provide a common repository of the data used by the company to calculate its GHG digital emissions. Such a database will not only help local leaders gain time by not having to collect these inputs, but will also ensure a common calculation basis across

**G242:p0066:c000 · PDF 66**

the organization and therefore facilitate peer review and inspire others. It will also help to fight against greenwashing communications from the market and help avoid obsolete data remaining available online.

**G242:p0066:c001 · PDF 66**

This file should include the initial, uninterpreted unitary data with its source for each perimeter considered: emissions factors from device manufacturers (run and build phases) and related electricity consumption, calculated emissions and consumption from on-premise data centers, and emissions from public cloud services. To be fully transparent on the calculation methodologies, other physical values must be communicated, such as the reference carbon intensity of energy mixes considered by the company. This repository should be presented as part of a larger onboarding of suborganizations, to help them become autonomous on the subject and to support their own bottom- up initiatives.

**G242:p0066:c002 · PDF 66**

Table 3-4: Non-Exhaustive List of Fields to be Included in a Shared Carbon Database

**G242:p0066:c003 · PDF 66**

| Field Name | Unit | Type of Source | Comments |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Device electricity<br>consumption | kWh/year | Device manufacturer | To specify for the company’s<br>average models. |
| Device Scope 3 emissions | kgCOe/device<br>2 | Device manufacturer | Idem. |
| On-premise data center<br>consumption | kWh/vCPU, kWh/GB,<br>or any other storage<br>KPI | Company’s data center<br>management | To be calculated from data<br>center energy consumption,<br>PUE and VMs allocation per<br>service. |
| Public cloud run and build<br>emissions | kgCOe/<br>2<br>monetary unit | Cloud provider inputs (or CDP<br>estimations; see previously) | A monetary indicator is easier<br>to manipulate for entity<br>allocation. |
| Carbon intensity of electricity<br>production | kgCOe/kWh<br>2 | National or international<br>agency | For example, IEA or ADEME. |

## 与客户、提供方、伙伴及主管部门互动，共同实现可持续的解决方案与服务

原文：3.7. Engaging with Customers, Providers, Partners, and Authorities Towards Sustainable Solutions and Services。PDF 物理页：[66, 67]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

许多利益相关者参与数字解决方案的开发。为成功部署其政策，组织需要与可持续 ICT 生态系统中涉及的各类参与者建立伙伴关系。这些利益相关者可以是客户、服务或设备供应商、监管与信息机构（例如环境保护机构）、高等教育机构，或在企业资金稀缺时充当先行者并动员公民的非营利组织。

本节讨论与该生态系统互动的不同活动。

These stakeholders can be customers, service or equipment suppliers, regulatory and informational institutions, such as environmental protection agencies, higher education, or non-profit organizations acting as precursors and enabling the mobilization of citizens when corporate funding is scarce.
