<a id="3.7.1"></a>
## 要求服务提供方提供端到端环境足迹透明度

原文：3.7.1. Ask Service Providers for End-to-End Environmental Footprint Transparency。PDF 物理页：[67, 68]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

为能够满足选择标准，供应商必须提供其所供设备与服务全面、透明的环境足迹度量。

大多数大型企业在其年报的非财务业绩章节公开分享其运营的碳足迹，至少涵盖范围 1 与范围 2，也越来越多地涵盖范围 3。碳披露项目（CDP）[CDP] 是一个国际非政府组织（NGO），以标准化格式收集并公开分享上述碳足迹。它使组织能够轻松阅读这些报告，作为与供应商协作的第一步。

迈向透明度的另一步——可增强对提供方承诺的信心——是对行动计划的第三方验证，例如 SBTi 或 B Corp™ 认证³。

组织还需要了解其自身设备或服务消费的影响。一些硬件制造商提供覆盖“从摇篮到坟墓”全生命周期的度量。然而，尽管服务提供方会就用电优化进行沟通，却只有少数覆盖全生命周期，并单独公布抵消与可再生能源采购情况。事实上，把抵消纳入合并排放数字，是各类组织中的不良实践之一，会造成含糊并缺乏透明度。

必须尽快实现所有这些指标的透明度，并强调环境影响管理可以带来显著的竞争优势。优化是供应商的职责，而关于用途的决策是在各组织内部做出的，需要精确的数据。可以考虑以下行动：

- 通过发起人公开声明：透明度与环境影响削减计划会带来竞争优势；
- 识别 ICT 服务与设备的主要供应商，就可持续 ICT 议题建立对话，在附加值高、资源需求低的 ICT 解决方案上开展伙伴合作；
- 描述所期望的报告内容并传达给供应商：用电、温室气体排放、稀有金属消耗、移动的土方量等；对于服务，还包括数据量、处理量与网络流量；
- 动员所有与供应商接触的利益相关者（包括管理层、技术人员与业务单元），在其交流中要求这种透明度；
- 即使成本更高，也依据环境因素做出采购决策——这将向供应商发出强烈信号。

> **脚注**：参见：www.bcorporation.net/en-us/certification。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 应向服务提供方明确电力、GHG、金属及服务活动量等预期报告。
  - 适用边界：报告要求、供应商承诺、实际交付数据、独立核验是不同状态。
  - PDF 68：• Describe the expected reporting and communicate it to the supplier: electricity consumption, GHG emissions, consumption of rare metals, displaced earth, etc., and in the case of services: data volume, processing, network traffic
- 环保选择可能增加采购成本但仍具长期价值。
  - 适用边界：不是授权支出；需保留成本、收益、风险及采购决策。
  - PDF 68：• Decide to make a purchase based on environmental factors, even if costs are higher; this will send a strong signal to suppliers

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0067:c002 · PDF 67**

3.7.1. Ask Service Providers for End-to-End Environmental Footprint Transparency

**G242:p0067:c003 · PDF 67**

To be able to fulfill selection criteria, the supplier must give access to comprehensive and transparent environmental footprint measurements of the equipment and services provided.

**G242:p0067:c004 · PDF 67**

In the non-financial results chapter of their annual report, most large companies publicly share the carbon footprint of their operations, at least on Scope 1 and 2, but also increasingly on Scope 3. The Carbon Disclosure Project [CDP], an international Non-Governmental Organization (NGO), collects and publicly shares said carbon footprints in a standardized format. It enables the organization to read these reports easily, as a first step to collaborating with suppliers.

**G242:p0067:c005 · PDF 67**

Another step towards transparency, reinforcing confidence in a provider’s commitment, is external

**G242:p0067:c006 · PDF 67**

[3]

**G242:p0067:c007 · PDF 67**

validation of action plans like the SBTi or B Corp™ certifications.

**G242:p0067:c008 · PDF 67**

The organization will also need to know the impact of its own consumption of equipment or services. Some of the hardware manufacturers provide measurements over the entire lifecycle, from “cradle to grave”. However, although service providers communicate on the optimization of electricity consumption, only a few cover the full lifecycle and publish compensations and purchases of renewable energy separately. Indeed, including offsets in consolidated emissions figures is one of the bad practices across organizations, generating ambiguity and lack of transparency.

**G242:p0067:c009 · PDF 67**

Transparency on all these indicators must be reached quickly, by emphasizing that environmental impact management can yield a significant competitive advantage. Optimization being the supplier’s duty, decisions regarding uses are made within each organization and require precise data. The following actions may be considered:

**G242:p0068:c000 · PDF 68**

• Publicly announce via the sponsor that transparency and environmental impact reduction plans give competitive advantages

**G242:p0068:c001 · PDF 68**

• Identify the main suppliers of ICT services and equipment, set up a dialogue on the issue of sustainable ICT, work in partnership on ICT solutions with high added value and low need for resources

**G242:p0068:c002 · PDF 68**

• Describe the expected reporting and communicate it to the supplier: electricity consumption, GHG emissions, consumption of rare metals, displaced earth, etc., and in the case of services: data volume, processing, network traffic

**G242:p0068:c003 · PDF 68**

• Mobilize all stakeholders in contact with the suppliers to require this transparency during their exchanges, including management, technicians, and business units

**G242:p0068:c004 · PDF 68**

• Decide to make a purchase based on environmental factors, even if costs are higher; this will send a strong signal to suppliers

</details>

<a id="3.7.2"></a>
## 调整招标以及与供应商和伙伴合同的选择标准

原文：3.7.2. Adapt the Selection Criteria for Calls for Tenders and Contracts with Suppliers and Partners。PDF 物理页：[68, 69]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在招标中，应明确与环境影响相关的选择标准。为此，需要就可持续 ICT 的选择标准对采购人员进行培训：能够延长产品寿命的维护合同、采购稳健且可维修的设备、低消耗、对回收与再利用过程的描述与界定等。

与供应商签订的新合同必须反映这些承诺——它们往往繁琐且难以落实。必须与法律专家共同找到引入环境标准合规的方式：或修订与企业责任相关的条款，或在缺失处新增条款。

> **示例 3-8**
>
> 合同中的环境标准示例是法国的“matinfo”市场——高等教育与研究领域计算机设备的采购集团，其中已考虑了环境与社会要求。
>
> 参见：www.matinfo-esr.fr/ecoinfo 以及 ecoinfo.cnrs.fr/2018/12/04/enseignement-superieur-et-recherche-achats-informatiques-et-developpement-durable/。
>
> 另见欧盟清洁服务 GPP 标准：https://susproc.jrc.ec.europa.eu/product-bureau/sites/default/files/2020-02/cleaning.pdf。

例如，可以在招标文件或合同中加入以下内容：

- **手段义务**：开展员工宣导或培训、调整工作方法、承诺遵守特定良好实践、建立度量与指标等。这意味着供应商需要能够证明其员工受过良好培训，例如通过第三方证书或提供培训项目结果。
- **结果义务**：最大数据量、用户过程中消耗的能源、在低功耗设备上运行的能力等。

为促进长寿命设备的使用，可以逐步采取以下方法：

- 在设备（PC、智能手机、服务器等）采购中考虑覆盖全生命周期（直至废弃物处理）的**总拥有成本（TCO）**，这将有利于把环境标准纳入考量，而不只是考虑初始购置成本；
- 选择既能使设备材料最大化再利用、又能改变其产品消费方式的供应商，即优先提供数字服务的“获取”而非“购买”。此类报价仍属少见，但已开始出现在市场上——有参与者面向企业和个人提供智能手机、计算机与耳机的租赁，并承诺透明且生态负责的管理。在这类运营中，供应商不再有更新设备的利益，相反能从使设备尽可能可持续中获益；
- 明确贵公司使用之后设备的去向：翻新、报废、拆解等。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 手段义务涉及培训、方法与实践承诺。
  - 适用边界：不能仅因员工培训就判定结果排放目标已达成。
  - PDF 69：• Obligations of means: raising awareness or training staff, adapting work methods, committing to respect specific good practices, setting up measures and indicators, etc.
- 结果义务可涉及数据量、使用能源或低端硬件适用性。
  - 适用边界：合同指标需可测条件、阈值、服务范围及验收证据。
  - PDF 69：• Obligations of results: maximum amount of data, energy consumed during the user process, ability to operate on low-power equipment, etc.
- TCO应覆盖完整使用寿命直到废弃处理。
  - 适用边界：购买价低不自动等于全寿命成本低或环境影响低。
  - PDF 69：• Considering Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) over the full lifetime – until waste treatment – for the purchases of equipment (PC, smartphones, servers, etc.) will favor considering the environmental criteria, over only considering initial acquisition costs
- 采购需明确设备退出后再制造、报废、拆解等去向。
  - 适用边界：收集待回收不等于完成回收或材料实际再利用。
  - PDF 69：• Defining what is going to happen for equipment after your company has used it: refurbishment, end-of-life, dismantling, etc.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0068:c005 · PDF 68**

3.7.2. Adapt the Selection Criteria for Calls for Tenders and Contracts with Suppliers and Partners

**G242:p0068:c006 · PDF 68**

In calls for tenders, selection criteria related to environmental impact should be made explicit. To enable this, it will be necessary to train buyers on the criteria of sustainable ICT selection: maintenance contracts enabling extended product lifetime, purchases of robust and repairable equipment, low consumption, description, and qualification of the recycling and reuse process, etc.

**G242:p0068:c007 · PDF 68**

New contracts signed with suppliers must reflect these commitments which are often cumbersome and difficult to implement. Finding a way to introduce environmental criteria compliance will have to be worked out with legal experts, either by revisiting the clauses relating to corporate responsibility, or by adding them where they do not exist.

**G242:p0068:c008 · PDF 68**

Example 3-8

**G242:p0068:c009 · PDF 68**

An example of environmental criteria in contracts is the French “matinfo” market, which is the purchasing group for computer equipment for higher education and research, in which environmental and social requirements have been considered.

**G242:p0068:c010 · PDF 68**

Refer to: www.matinfo-esr.fr/ecoinfo and ecoinfo.cnrs.fr/2018/12/04/enseignement-superieur-et- recherche-achats-informatiques-et-developpement-durable/.

**G242:p0068:c011 · PDF 68**

See also the EU GPP Criteria for Cleaning Services: https://susproc.jrc.ec.europa.eu/product- bureau/sites/default/files/2020-02/cleaning.pdf.

**G242:p0069:c000 · PDF 69**

It is, for instance, feasible to add the following to requests for tenders or contracts:

**G242:p0069:c001 · PDF 69**

• Obligations of means: raising awareness or training staff, adapting work methods, committing to respect specific good practices, setting up measures and indicators, etc.

**G242:p0069:c002 · PDF 69**

This implies the need for suppliers to be able to prove that their staff are well trained, via third- party certificates or the availability of training program results.

**G242:p0069:c003 · PDF 69**

• Obligations of results: maximum amount of data, energy consumed during the user process, ability to operate on low-power equipment, etc.

**G242:p0069:c004 · PDF 69**

To promote the use of long-lasting equipment, the following approaches can gradually be adopted:

**G242:p0069:c005 · PDF 69**

• Considering Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) over the full lifetime – until waste treatment – for the purchases of equipment (PC, smartphones, servers, etc.) will favor considering the environmental criteria, over only considering initial acquisition costs

**G242:p0069:c006 · PDF 69**

• Choosing suppliers enabling both the maximum reuse of the material making up their equipment, and a change in the way their products are consumed, by favoring access to digital services over acquisition

**G242:p0069:c007 · PDF 69**

Such offers are still rare but are starting to appear on the market, with players offering rental of smartphones, computers, and headsets, for businesses and individuals, by committing to transparent and eco-responsible management. In this type of operation, the supplier no longer has an interest in renewing the equipment but, on the contrary, benefits from making it as sustainable as possible.

**G242:p0069:c008 · PDF 69**

• Defining what is going to happen for equipment after your company has used it: refurbishment, end-of-life, dismantling, etc.

</details>

<a id="3.7.3"></a>
## 定义采购战略

原文：3.7.3. Define the Procurement Strategy。PDF 物理页：[69, 70]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

设备或服务的采购面临两个战略选择：

- 能力是内包（insource）还是外包（outsource）；
- 选择资产所在地：在岸（onshore，例如组织所在国领土）、近岸（nearshore，取决于组织所在地）或离岸（offshore）。

由于各国能源结构的差异，这些选择可能使 CO2 排放相差数个数量级。

除碳足迹之外，还需要改变供应商选择，以把生产者与消费者之间的中间环节数量降到最低。目标将是限制物流流量、精确指出负外部性并避免遗忘它们，并与供应商的活动保持最大程度的接近与可及。从企业问责的角度看，供应商越远（无论是物理上还是通过中间环节），就越难确保其伦理水准。

最后，参与有关服务或供应商产品演进的决策过程的能力，也将是确保所提供服务可持续性的一个因素。因此，必须加强已有的用户组，并使其更好地参与供应商战略。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 内外包及地理位置选择会因电力结构改变排放。
  - 适用边界：单用工资或云账单比较无法判断环境收益。
  - PDF 69：These choices can influence CO2 emissions by several orders of magnitude due to variations in the energy mix of different countries.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0069:c009 · PDF 69**

3.7.3. Define the Procurement Strategy

**G242:p0069:c010 · PDF 69**

The procurement of equipment or services faces two strategic choices:

**G242:p0069:c011 · PDF 69**

• Insource or outsource the capacity

**G242:p0069:c012 · PDF 69**

• Choose the location of the assets: onshore (e.g., national territory of the organization), nearshore (depends on the organization location), or offshore

**G242:p0069:c013 · PDF 69**

These choices can influence CO2 emissions by several orders of magnitude due to variations in the energy mix of different countries.

**G242:p0069:c014 · PDF 69**

Beyond the carbon footprint, changes in the choice of suppliers will be necessary to reduce to a minimum the number of intermediaries between producer and consumer. The objective will be to limit logistics flows, to pinpoint negative externalities and avoid forgetting them, and to have maximum proximity and access to the supplier’s activities. From a corporate accountability perspective, the more distant the supplier (either physically or through intermediaries), the more difficult it is to ensure its ethics.

**G242:p0070:c000 · PDF 70**

Finally, the ability to participate in the decision-making process regarding the evolution of the service or of the supplier’s product will also be a factor to ensure the sustainability of the provided service. Thus, already existing user groups will have to be strengthened, and better involved in the supplier’s strategy.

</details>

<a id="3.7.4"></a>
## 遵守并超越主管部门要求的监管环境报告

原文：3.7.4. Comply and Go Beyond the Regulatory Environmental Reporting Requested by Authorities。PDF 物理页：[70, 71]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

组织必须开展地方主管部门所规定的监管环境报告。如今，环境影响的度量已由组织应遵循的 ISO 标准加以规范，包括：

- **生命周期评估**：
  - ISO 14040:2006：环境管理——生命周期评估——原则与框架 [ISO 14040:2006]
  - ISO 14044:2006：环境管理——生命周期评估——要求与指南 [ISO 14044:2006]
  - ISO 14009:2020：环境管理体系——在产品设计与开发中引入材料循环的指南 [ISO 14009:2020]
- **碳核算**：
  - ISO 14064-1:2018：温室气体——第 1 部分：组织层面对温室气体排放与清除进行量化和报告的规范及指南 [ISO 14064-1]

在法国，企业必须在其监管温室气体报告 [Carbon Neutrality] 中记录其碳足迹（关于不同范围的细节见 3.6 节）。

在美国，证券交易委员会（SEC）[SEC, 2022] 提议更系统地纳入范围 3。

这些法规在法国、欧洲乃至最终全球应会继续加强，正如在个人数据管理相关法规（欧洲的 GDPR）中已经观察到的那样。在法国，REEN 法 [REEN Law] 于 2021 年 11 月获得通过，作为更严格法规的基线，旨在减少数字环境影响。

在等待这些监管变化期间，鉴于行动的紧迫性，组织提前行动并超越监管义务是有益的。它们应决定在透明度上先行，向客户公布并分享与其所提供服务相关的 ICT 设备的温室气体排放，以及减少它的工具。

ICT 环境影响的一半由制造驱动 [Shift Project, 2019]，而制造并不一定发生在资源被使用的国家。因此，符合监管报告要求并不必然使人能够走向可持续 ICT。使用数字托管服务替代组织直接运营的数据中心，会强化这一趋势。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 应遵守适用本地报告要求并参考ISO计量标准。
  - 适用边界：遵守报告规定不证明整体ICT已可持续；标准版本仍需按项目核实。
  - PDF 70：The organization will have to conduct the regulatory environmental reporting defined by the local authorities. The measure of the environmental impact is today normalized by the ISO standards that organizations should follow. These are:
- SEC材料为2022年提案案例。
  - 适用边界：不将提议义务写成已生效法律或本企业适用义务。
  - PDF 70：In the US, the Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) [SEC, 2022] proposes to include Scope 3 more systematically.

3.7.4. Comply and Go Beyond the Regulatory Environmental Reporting Requested by Authorities

**G242:p0070:c002 · PDF 70**

The organization will have to conduct the regulatory environmental reporting defined by the local authorities. The measure of the environmental impact is today normalized by the ISO standards that organizations should follow. These are:

**G242:p0070:c003 · PDF 70**

• Lifecycle assessment:

**G242:p0070:c004 · PDF 70**

◦ISO 14040:2006: Environmental Management – Lifecycle Assessment – Principles and

**G242:p0070:c005 · PDF 70**

Framework [ISO 14040:2006]

**G242:p0070:c006 · PDF 70**

◦ISO 14044:2006: Environmental Management – Lifecycle Assessment – Requirements and

**G242:p0070:c007 · PDF 70**

Guideline [ISO 14044:2006]

**G242:p0070:c008 · PDF 70**

◦ISO 14009:2020: Environmental Management Systems – Guidelines for Incorporating Material

**G242:p0070:c009 · PDF 70**

Circulation in Design and Development [ISO 14009:2020]

**G242:p0070:c010 · PDF 70**

• Carbon accounting:

**G242:p0070:c011 · PDF 70**

◦ISO 14064-1:2018: Greenhouse Gases – Part 1: Specification with Guidance at the Organization

**G242:p0070:c012 · PDF 70**

Level for Quantification and Reporting of Greenhouse Gas Emissions and Removals [ISO 14064- 1]

**G242:p0070:c013 · PDF 70**

In France, companies must record their carbon footprint [Carbon Neutrality] on a regulatory GHG report (see Section 3.6 for details about the different scopes).

**G242:p0070:c014 · PDF 70**

In the US, the Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) [SEC, 2022] proposes to include Scope 3 more systematically.

**G242:p0070:c015 · PDF 70**

These regulations should continue to be reinforced in France, in Europe, and eventually worldwide, as already observed in the regulations related to personal data management (GDPR in Europe). In France, the REEN Law [REEN Law] was adopted in November 2021 as a baseline for stronger regulations, aiming to reduce digital environmental impact.

**G242:p0070:c016 · PDF 70**

While awaiting these regulatory changes, and given the urgency to act, it is beneficial for organizations to anticipate and go beyond regulatory obligations. They should decide to move ahead on transparency, publish and share with their customers the GHG emissions of ICT equipment related to the service they provide and tools to reduce it.

**G242:p0071:c000 · PDF 71**

Half of ICT’s environmental impact is driven by manufacturing [Shift Project, 2019], which does not necessarily take place in the country where the resource is used. As a result, being compliant with regulatory reporting does not necessarily make it possible to move towards sustainable ICT. This trend is reinforced by using digital hosting services to replace data centers directly operated by organizations.

</details>

<a id="3.7.5"></a>
## 让客户参与数字可持续目标

原文：3.7.5. Involve Customers in the Goal of Digital Sustainability。PDF 物理页：[71, 72]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

社会对气候风险的认识，已促使许多企业在环境影响上采取有承诺的立场，以改善公众认知。

此类声明固然能够提高市场对气候环境风险的认识，但必须指出：仅通过谈论可再生电力消费或纳入碳抵消来宣传 CO2 减排，是适得其反的。它给人一种错觉，似乎可以扩大使用而不带来环境弊端——从集体走向可持续数字世界的角度看，这既不正确，也危险且无效。它还会给组织带来漂绿（greenwashing）的声誉风险。事实上，用电的优化是以非常频繁的硬件更新为代价的。只有当覆盖完整范围时，组织沟通其目标与结果才有意义。此外，一旦“电力温室气体净排放为零”，公司就无法再发展可持续性了，因为表面上已没有更多环境节约空间。这是一条死路。

> **注**：“绿色电力”（“基于市场”的核算）背后是一套复杂的证书机制，证明一定量的可再生能源被注入国家电网；然而，实际消费与所购买的电量并无关联，而是在物理上与当地能源结构相关（“基于地区”）。
>
> 这些证书依赖于市场条件，在可再生能源发展的融资中仍发挥次要作用，而后者强烈依赖国家政策。
>
> 参见《温室气体核算体系》[GHG Protocol] 以及《“绿色”电力：企业的有用工具？》[Carbone4, 2019]。

因此，必须按照净零倡议（Net Zero Initiative）[Carbone4, 2020] 建议的步骤来加强这项活动：

- 公布排除碳抵消的完整范围的目标与结果；
- 对计算方法与所使用的基准保持透明；
- 以透明的来源与方法分享与可持续 ICT 行动相关的数据。

除在公司层面沟通之外，还必须：

- 向客户提供其所使用数字服务（例如网站、在线销售应用、托管或计算服务等）环境影响的时间序列度量；这将使客户能够尽自己的一份力，依据该标准节制其使用，并选择最负责任、或至少最透明的供应商；
- 支持客户在数字可持续方面的努力（例如使客户能够对所提供的服务自行设定使用上限）。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 市场法凭证核算与所在地电力结构核算需区分。
  - 适用边界：两个Scope2结果是不同方法，不相加；购买绿电凭证不证明物理用电零排放。
  - PDF 71：Behind the green electricity (“market-based” accounting ) lies a complex mechanism of certificates proving that a certain volume of renewable energy is injected into the national grid; however, actual consumption is not linked to what is bought, but physically linked to the local energy mix (“location-based”).
- 对外发布的目标及结果应覆盖完整范围，并单列抵消。
  - 适用边界：避免通过净额、缩小边界或只报Scope2夸大减排。
  - PDF 71：• Publish objectives and results of the full scope excluding carbon offset
- 公开计算方法及基准，客户可随时间理解使用影响。
  - 适用边界：不能在无证据时生成认证或环保宣传结论。
  - PDF 71：• Be transparent about the computation method and the benchmarks used

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0071:c001 · PDF 71**

3.7.5. Involve Customers in the Goal of Digital Sustainability

**G242:p0071:c002 · PDF 71**

Societal awareness of climate risk has led many companies to take committed positions on environmental impact to improve public perception.

**G242:p0071:c003 · PDF 71**

If such announcements can increase environmental climate risk awareness on the market, it should nevertheless be noted that it is counterproductive to communicate on CO2 emissions reduction by only talking about consumption of renewable electricity or by including carbon offset. It gives the illusion that it is possible to extend uses without environmental drawbacks, which is incorrect, dangerous, and ineffective from the point of view of the collective evolution towards a sustainable digital world. It also poses a reputational risk from greenwashing of the organization. In fact, optimizations in electricity consumption come at the cost of very frequent hardware renewals. It is only useful for an organization to communicate its objectives and results if the full scope is taken into account. Furthermore, once “net electricity GHG emissions are zero”, the company cannot develop sustainability anymore, because there is apparently no more environmental saving. This is a dead end.

**G242:p0071:c004 · PDF 71**

Behind the green electricity (“market-based” accounting ) lies a complex mechanism of certificates proving that a certain volume of renewable energy is injected into the national grid; however, actual consumption is not linked to what is bought, but physically linked to the local energy mix (“location-based”).

**G242:p0071:c005 · PDF 71**

These certificates are dependent on market conditions, and still play a secondary role in financing the development of renewable energies, which strongly depends on national policies.

**G242:p0071:c006 · PDF 71**

Refer to the Greenhouse Gas Protocol [GHG Protocol] and “Green” Electricity: A Useful Tool for Businesses? [Carbone4, 2019].

**G242:p0071:c007 · PDF 71**

It will therefore be necessary to enhance this activity following the steps recommended by the Net Zero Initiative [Carbone4, 2020] to:

**G242:p0071:c008 · PDF 71**

• Publish objectives and results of the full scope excluding carbon offset

**G242:p0071:c009 · PDF 71**

• Be transparent about the computation method and the benchmarks used

**G242:p0071:c010 · PDF 71**

• Share data related to actions towards sustainable ICT with transparent sources and methodology

**G242:p0071:c011 · PDF 71**

In addition to communicating at the company level, it is important:

**G242:p0071:c012 · PDF 71**

• To provide customers with a measurement over time of the environmental impact of the digital services they use (e.g., website, online sales application, hosting or computation services, etc.); this will enable customers to play their part, to moderate their uses based on this criterion, and to choose the most responsible suppliers, or at least the most transparent

**G242:p0072:c000 · PDF 72**

• To support customer efforts towards digital sustainability (for example, by enabling customers to self-impose limits to their use of the provided services)

</details>

<a id="3.7.6"></a>
## 影响市场，通过投稿公开文章积累知识

原文：3.7.6. Influence the Market, Capitalize on Knowledge by Contributing to Public Papers。PDF 物理页：[72, 73]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

为赢得遏制 CO2 排放的与时间的赛跑，组织可以通过分享可持续 ICT 的良好实践作出贡献。目标是使其可自由获取，面向高等教育教师、所有使用数字解决方案的组织，以及所有 ICT 设备与服务供应商。这关乎在行业中营造争先氛围，实现集体加速走向可持续 ICT，以应对形势的紧迫性。

该活动可以分解为不同的行动：

- 为致力于积累可持续 ICT 良好实践的组织间工作组作出贡献；
- 分享与信息系统环境影响相关的数据，以增加该主题的公开可用数据；
- 为管理开放资源的非营利组织提供资金支持；
- 要求供应商参照这些出版物。

参与向可持续 ICT 转型的非营利组织示例包括：

**全球层面：**

- The Open Group：致力于数字世界标准的开发与部署的国际组织；
- 绿色软件基金会（Green Software Foundation）：旨在构建一个可信的人员、标准、工具与最佳实践生态，以创建和构建绿色软件。

**欧洲层面：**

- 欧洲可持续 IT 研究院网络（ISIT）：由法国、瑞士和比利时的分析机构组成的欧洲可持续 IT 研究院网络 [ISIT GR491]。

**法国层面：**

- The Shift Project：通过 Lean-ICT 工作组，由各组织赞助并开展相关出版工作；
- Cigref：代表法国最大型企业与公共行政机构的协会，这些机构专门使用数字解决方案与服务；协会支持其成员就数字议题开展集体思考，并设有一个“数字节制”工作组，致力于发布相关建议；
- 负责任数字技术研究院（INR）：法国智库，汇集并推广更再生、更包容、更合乎伦理的数字技术的实验与良好实践。

总之，数字生态系统已开始借助数字托管服务来优化资源、共享基础设施，这些服务将取代相当一部分由组织直接运营的数据中心。

现在，所有相关利益相关者都必须参与进来，使数字技术变得可持续，并使其朝着“有计划的可持续”与节俭工具的视角下的良性经济模式演进。它必须使所有利益相关者——客户、企业和开发人员——能够减少其使用并“尽自己的一份力”。该方法旨在发展当前的经济模式，使其能够在一个资源与设备更新不再无限可得、环境污染被最小化的环境中继续运转。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 分享实践可促进行业共同改进。
  - 适用边界：公开分享应有适用语境和数据依据，不自动对外发布企业数据。
  - PDF 72：To win the race against time to contain CO2 emissions, the organization can contribute by sharing good sustainable ICT practices. The objective is to make them freely available to higher education teachers, all organizations using digital solutions, and all suppliers of ICT equipment and services. It is about creating emulation in the sector, enabling a collective acceleration towards sustainable ICT that meets the urgency of the situation.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0072:c001 · PDF 72**

3.7.6. Influence the Market, Capitalize on Knowledge by Contributing to Public Papers

**G242:p0072:c002 · PDF 72**

To win the race against time to contain CO2 emissions, the organization can contribute by sharing good sustainable ICT practices. The objective is to make them freely available to higher education teachers, all organizations using digital solutions, and all suppliers of ICT equipment and services. It is about creating emulation in the sector, enabling a collective acceleration towards sustainable ICT that meets the urgency of the situation.

**G242:p0072:c003 · PDF 72**

This activity can be broken down into different actions:

**G242:p0072:c004 · PDF 72**

• Contribute to inter-organization working groups capitalizing on good sustainable ICT practices

**G242:p0072:c005 · PDF 72**

• Share data related to the environmental impact of the information system to increase publicly available data on this topic

**G242:p0072:c006 · PDF 72**

• Provide financial support to non-profit organizations managing open resources

**G242:p0072:c007 · PDF 72**

• Ask suppliers to refer to these publications

**G242:p0072:c008 · PDF 72**

Examples of non-profit organizations involved in the transformation towards sustainable ICT include:

**G242:p0072:c009 · PDF 72**

Worldwide:

**G242:p0072:c010 · PDF 72**

• The Open Group is an international organization dedicated to the development and deployment of standards in the digital world

**G242:p0072:c011 · PDF 72**

• The Green Software Foundation aims to build a trusted ecosystem of people, standards, tooling, and best practices for creating and building green software

**G242:p0072:c012 · PDF 72**

In Europe:

**G242:p0072:c013 · PDF 72**

• European Institutes for Sustainable IT (ISIT) is a European network of institutes for sustainable IT grouping French, Swiss, and Belgian analysts [ISIT GR491]

**G242:p0072:c014 · PDF 72**

In France:

**G242:p0072:c015 · PDF 72**

• The Shift Project through the Lean-ICT Work Group, sponsored by organizations and associated publication work

**G242:p0073:c000 · PDF 73**

• The Cigref is an association representing the largest French companies and public administrations which exclusively use digital solutions and services, supporting its members in their collective reflections on digital issues; a “Digital Sobriety” working group is working on the publication of recommendations on this subject

**G242:p0073:c001 · PDF 73**

• The Institute of Responsible Digital Technology (INR) is a French think tank bringing together experimentation and promotion of good practices for more regenerative, inclusive, and ethical digital technology

**G242:p0073:c002 · PDF 73**

To conclude, the digital ecosystem has already started to optimize resources and share infrastructure with digital hosting services, which will come to replace a sizeable portion of the data centers operated directly by organizations.

**G242:p0073:c003 · PDF 73**

All stakeholders in place must now weigh in to make digital technology sustainable, and make it evolve towards a virtuous economic model from the viewpoint of planned sustainability and of the tools of frugality. It must enable all stakeholders, customers, businesses, and developers to reduce their usages and “do their part”. The approach is intended to develop current economic models so that they can continue to function in an environment in which resources and equipment renewal are no longer infinitely available and where environmental pollution is minimized.

</details>

3.8. Operating a Sustainable Information System

<a id="3.8.1"></a>
## 管理数字设备清单

原文：3.8.1. Manage Digital Equipment Inventories。PDF 物理页：[73]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

组织所需数字设备的准确清单，是环境影响度量的基础之一。它应覆盖服务器与网络设备，也应覆盖显示器、笔记本电脑、台式机、平板电脑、智能手机、会议室视频设备、外设（键盘、鼠标、耳机等）、打印机、电视屏幕等。清单可以通过自动方式（针对联网设备）、通过采购与发票，或人工方式维护。ICT 资产清单管理是配置管理数据库（CMDB）的职能之一。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 清单包括服务器、网络、办公终端、会议室设备及外设。
  - 适用边界：只盘点云资源或联网服务器不满足资产覆盖。
  - PDF 73：An accurate inventory of digital equipment required by the organization is one of the foundations of the environmental impact measure. It should cover servers and network equipment, but also monitors, laptops, desktops, tablets, smartphones, meeting room video equipment, peripherals (keyboards, mice, headphones, etc.), printers, TV screens, etc. Inventories can be maintained automatically (for connected devices), through purchasing and invoices, or manually. ICT asset inventory management is one of the functions of a Configuration Management Database (CMDB).

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0073:c005 · PDF 73**

3.8.1. Manage Digital Equipment Inventories

**G242:p0073:c006 · PDF 73**

An accurate inventory of digital equipment required by the organization is one of the foundations of the environmental impact measure. It should cover servers and network equipment, but also monitors, laptops, desktops, tablets, smartphones, meeting room video equipment, peripherals (keyboards, mice, headphones, etc.), printers, TV screens, etc. Inventories can be maintained automatically (for connected devices), through purchasing and invoices, or manually. ICT asset inventory management is one of the functions of a Configuration Management Database (CMDB).

</details>

<a id="3.8.2"></a>
## 支持延长数字设备的使用期限

原文：3.8.2. Support the Increase in Usage Period of Digital Equipment。PDF 物理页：[73]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

延长数字设备的使用期限，还要求改变“总是购买更时尚、性能更好的全新设备，然后丢弃旧设备”的习惯。对企业而言，这意味着为设备保护与维修投入更多资源。维修意味着为服务台与技术员工建立能力与文化，使他们有能力也有意愿处理基本的常见问题，例如更换电池或维修屏幕。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 延长设备寿命需要维修资源、服务台能力与文化。
  - 适用边界：设备寿命目标不能仅靠修改资产台账日期证明。
  - PDF 73：Increasing the usage period of digital equipment also requires changes to the habit of always buying brand new equipment, which is more fashionable and better performing, and then dispose of old devices. For enterprises, this means dedicating more resources to equipment protection and repair. Repairing means creating the ability and culture for the help desk and technical employees to have the ability and willingness to tackle basic recurrent problems, like changing batteries or repairing screens.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0073:c007 · PDF 73**

3.8.2. Support the Increase in Usage Period of Digital Equipment

**G242:p0073:c008 · PDF 73**

Increasing the usage period of digital equipment also requires changes to the habit of always buying brand new equipment, which is more fashionable and better performing, and then dispose of old devices. For enterprises, this means dedicating more resources to equipment protection and repair. Repairing means creating the ability and culture for the help desk and technical employees to have the ability and willingness to tackle basic recurrent problems, like changing batteries or repairing screens.

</details>

<a id="3.8.3"></a>
## 监控数字消费

原文：3.8.3. Monitor Digital Consumption。PDF 物理页：[73, 74]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

随着存储、网络、处理等所有 ICT 功能的优化以及软件即服务（SaaS）解决方案的发展，ICT 部门对服务器活动、数据量、连接数等指标，已丧失了过去曾采用的某些监控习惯。数字服务的消费与污染之间存在强关联。事实上，虽然技术效率的提升吸收了一部分数字污染影响，但效率边界很快就会到来。若用量继续增长，最坏的情形是影响也随之增长。

提供“按需”ICT 资源的云架构的发展，在 ICT 部门催生了一项名为 FinOps⁴ 的新活动。它是一种管理实践，促进对组织云计算基础设施与成本的共同责任。一种良好实践是把环境关键绩效指标（KPI）——例如用电、温室气体排放——加入 FinOps 监控。

> **脚注**：FinOps 是一门不断演进的云财务管理学科与文化实践，通过帮助工程、财务、技术和业务团队在数据驱动的支出决策上协作，使组织获得最大的业务价值。参见：www.finops.org/introduction/what-is-finops/。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- FinOps监控应加入用电和GHG等环境KPI。
  - 适用边界：成本、资源活动量和排放分别展示；价格下降不等于排放下降。
  - PDF 74：The development of cloud architecture that offers “on-demand” ICT resource has led to the creation of a new activity in ICT departments called FinOps[4]. It is a management practice that promotes shared responsibility for an organization’s cloud computing infrastructure and costs. A good practice is to add the environmental Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) – such as electricity consumption, GHG emissions – to FinOps monitoring.

### 原文内容单元

**G242:p0073:c009 · PDF 73**

3.8.3. Monitor Digital Consumption

**G242:p0073:c010 · PDF 73**

With the optimization of all ICT functions like storage, network, processing, and the development of Software as a Service (SaaS) solutions, ICT departments have lost some of the monitoring habits they formerly adopted for indicators like server activity, data volumes, and number of connections. There is a strong link between consumption of digital services and pollution. Indeed, while improvement of technical efficiency has allowed absorption of part of the digital pollution impact, efficiency

**G242:p0074:c000 · PDF 74**

boundaries will soon be reached. If the volumes continue to increase, the worst-case scenario is that impacts will increase as well.

**G242:p0074:c001 · PDF 74**

The development of cloud architecture that offers “on-demand” ICT resource has led to the creation of a new activity in ICT departments called FinOps[4]. It is a management practice that promotes shared responsibility for an organization’s cloud computing infrastructure and costs. A good practice is to add the environmental Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) – such as electricity consumption, GHG emissions – to FinOps monitoring.

</details>

<a id="3.8.4"></a>
## 应电网运营方要求对数据中心削峰

原文：3.8.4. Load-Shedding for Data Center on Demand of Electricity Operator。PDF 物理页：[74]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在电网紧张的背景下——由于物理约束与地缘政治冲突，预计这种紧张会更频繁地出现——组织可以与当地电网运营公司签订合同，使其设施能够脱离电网，从而避免大面积停电。

在企业侧，这意味着要能够：

- 关闭“锦上添花”的用电（例如信息电视屏、工作场所的双显示器等）；
- 关闭对组织核心业务非必需的用电；
- 推迟没有关键实时要求的密集型数字消费；
- 启动自有发电机（但这通常需要化石能源储备来运行，并产生碳密集的电力）；
- 监控自身用电。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 负荷削减需结合与电网运营方的安排。
  - 适用边界：生成建议不授权停机，需确认合同、核心业务约束、执行权限与恢复。
  - PDF 74：In a context of tensions on the electricity network, expected to occur more frequently due to physical constraints and geopolitical conflicts, a contract can be established between an organization and the local electrical network operating company so that its facilities can be disconnected from the grid, and therefore prevent a general power outage.
- 可推迟不具有关键实时要求的高强度计算。
  - 适用边界：不能以节能规则自动延后关键服务或所有批处理。
  - PDF 74：• Postpone intensive digital consumption with no critical real-time requests
- 自备发电常依赖化石燃料并产生高碳电力。
  - 适用边界：保障电网韧性的操作与减少自身排放可能存在权衡，需分别报告。
  - PDF 74：• Switch on its own electricity generator (but that often requires fossil energy stocks to run and produces carbon-intensive electricity)

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0074:c002 · PDF 74**

3.8.4. Load-Shedding for Data Center on Demand of Electricity Operator

**G242:p0074:c003 · PDF 74**

In a context of tensions on the electricity network, expected to occur more frequently due to physical constraints and geopolitical conflicts, a contract can be established between an organization and the local electrical network operating company so that its facilities can be disconnected from the grid, and therefore prevent a general power outage.

**G242:p0074:c004 · PDF 74**

On the enterprise’s side it means being able to:

**G242:p0074:c005 · PDF 74**

• Switch off “nice to have” electricity consumption (e.g., information TV screen, double monitors in the workplace, etc.)

**G242:p0074:c006 · PDF 74**

• Switch off electricity consumption that is not mandatory for the core business of the organization

**G242:p0074:c007 · PDF 74**

• Postpone intensive digital consumption with no critical real-time requests

**G242:p0074:c008 · PDF 74**

• Switch on its own electricity generator (but that often requires fossil energy stocks to run and produces carbon-intensive electricity)

**G242:p0074:c009 · PDF 74**

• Monitor its electricity consumption

</details>

<a id="3.9"></a>
## 治理向可持续信息系统的转型

原文：3.9. Governing the Transition Towards a Sustainable Information System。PDF 物理页：[74, 75]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实施信息系统可持续性战略，要求企业拓宽视角，把环境因素纳入其决策过程。企业随后将面对其演进方式的深刻变化，尤其是在如何定义经济模型、战略愿景、架构、伙伴关系或解决方案方面。这一战略的成功需要所有这些方面之间真正的协同，最终需要建立强有力的治理来使转型发生。

> **注**：治理是组织做出决策并执行以达成目标的系统。决策过程可以是正式的——依赖定义良好的流程，也可以是非正式的——依赖组织中个人的行动。

把环境方面纳入决策过程，需要使用新的指标，涉及温室气体排放潜力、稀有金属开采或水资源使用，这些指标将为决策提供信息，并使转型过程可被监视。

关键在于考虑整个业务生态系统，而不是保持狭窄的视角（狭窄视角可能威胁组织的平衡与可持续性）。换言之，这就是“系统思维”。

> **注**：系统思维是一种通过把世界看作整体与关系、而非将其拆分为部件来理解世界复杂性的方式。它被用作在复杂环境中探索和发展有效行动、实现系统变革的方法。在组织中，系统由人、结构和流程组成，它们共同作用使组织“健康”或“不健康”；参见 Donella Meadows 的《系统之美》（Thinking in Systems）[Systems Thinking]。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 决策应观察整个业务生态系统的物理约束和关系。
  - 适用边界：局部最优或成本最小方案不能自动标为整体可持续最优。
  - PDF 75：The point is to consider the entire business ecosystem instead of keeping a narrow point of view (which may threaten the balance and sustainability of the organization). In other words, it is about “systems thinking”.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0074:c010 · PDF 74**

3.9. Governing the Transition Towards a Sustainable

**G242:p0074:c011 · PDF 74**

Information System

**G242:p0074:c012 · PDF 74**

Implementing a strategy for information system durability requires an enterprise to broaden its point of view by including environmental factors in its decision-making process. Businesses then face deep changes in the way they evolve, especially in how they define their economic model, their strategic vision, their architecture, their partnerships, or their solutions. The success of this strategy requires real synergy between all these aspects, which eventually leads to setting up strong governance to make the transformation happen.

**G242:p0074:c013 · PDF 74**

Governance is the system through which an organization makes decisions and applies them to reach its goals. The decision-making process can be formal, relying on well-defined processes, or informal, relying on the individual actions of people in the organization.

**G242:p0075:c000 · PDF 75**

Including environmental aspects in the decision-making process requires the use of new indicators, related to GHG emissions potential, rare metals mining, or water usage, which will inform decisions and enable the monitoring of the transformation process.

**G242:p0075:c001 · PDF 75**

The point is to consider the entire business ecosystem instead of keeping a narrow point of view (which may threaten the balance and sustainability of the organization). In other words, it is about “systems thinking”.

**G242:p0075:c002 · PDF 75**

Systems thinking is a way of making sense of the complexity of the world by looking at it in terms of wholes and relationships rather than by splitting it down into its parts. It has been used as a way of exploring and developing effective action in complex contexts, enabling systems change. In organizations, systems consist of people, structures, and processes that work together to make an organization “healthy” or “unhealthy”; see “Thinking in Systems”, by Donella Meadows [Systems Thinking].

</details>

<a id="3.9.1"></a>
## 以环境为重做出决策

原文：3.9.1. Making Decisions with the Environment in Mind。PDF 物理页：[75, 76, 77]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在企业每个层级，无论正式与否，都会直接或间接做出影响环境的决策。要使这些决策与数字可持续性兼容，需要坚定且注重平衡的决策管理。

> **注**：决策是治理的关键要素，必须据此加以管理。决策对于设计选择至关重要，因为这些选择会对业务产生可度量的影响；此类选择通常被视为难以做出和/或更改成本高昂。

这一新的决策过程基于架构治理框架的两个基本概念：**决策日志**与**决策仓库**。

**决策日志**

决策日志可列出组织希望在决策中提出的各项标准——其中应包括环境标准。决策日志使企业能够跟踪决策、在员工之间共享信息并更新这些信息。

表 3-5 展示一个决策示例：某公司希望为客户建立新的移动应用。（注意这仅是示例，移动应用的开发需要逐案深入分析。）

**表 3-5：考虑环境标准的实施方案定义示例**

| 项目 | 方案 1 | 方案 2 | 方案 3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 描述 | 实施原生移动解决方案 | 实施混合移动解决方案 | 实施渐进式 Web 应用（PWA）解决方案 |
| 含义 | 为每个移动操作系统（iOS®、Android™ 等）分别开发解决方案 | 开发一个基于 Web 的公共核心，并与各操作系统相关解决方案集成 | 开发一个适用于所有操作系统的公共解决方案 |
| 托管 | 移动应用托管在组织自己的托管平台上 | 移动应用托管在组织自己的托管平台上 | 移动应用托管在组织自己的托管平台上 |
| 论证 | 未选择 | 未选择 | 体验接近原生移动应用；减少开发量、成本与环境影响；兼容持续部署 |
| 所有者 | 业务部门 | 业务部门 | 业务部门 |
| 利益相关者 | 业务、ICT 专业人员、财务、环境管理负责人 | 业务、ICT 专业人员、财务、环境管理负责人 | 业务、ICT 专业人员、财务、环境管理负责人 |
| 验证 | 执行委员会 | 执行委员会 | 执行委员会 |
| 适用范围 | 客户渠道 | 客户渠道 | 客户渠道 |
| 领域 | 业务与信息系统 | 业务与信息系统 | 业务与信息系统 |
| **环境标准** | | | |
| 温室气体（制造） | 1,200 tCO2e | 890 kgCO2e | 400 kgCO2e |
| 温室气体（使用） | 580 kgCO2e/年 | 360 kgCO2e/年 | 170 kgCO2e/年 |
| 稀有金属 | 2,050 kg | 1,400 kg | 960 kg |
| 饮用水 | 21,000 升 | 15,000 升 | 6,000 升 |
| **财务标准** |  |  |  |
| 业务价值 |  |  |  |
| **功能标准** |  |  |  |
| **非功能标准** |  |  |  |
| **用户体验标准** |  |  |  |

> 要将环境标准加入决策，重要的是拥有参考表，能够按公司的假设快速、高效地度量 CO2e 排放、稀有金属开采与饮用水使用。这些参考数据可来自多个来源。

在这里，公司把环境标准与财务、功能覆盖、客户体验等若干基本标准一起权衡。

**决策仓库**

决策日志可记录在组织内共享的**决策仓库**中，从而确保决策的可追溯性、共享性、可更新性与可用性。

图 3-13 展示企业架构治理委员会中数字可持续性评审模板的示例。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 决策日志与决策资料库承担不同作用：记录权衡与共享追溯。
  - 适用边界：生成日志不等于获得批准，资料库存在不等于实际执行。
  - PDF 75：This new decision-making process is based on two essential notions of the architectural governance framework: the decision log and the decision repository.

**G242:p0075:c003 · PDF 75**

3.9.1. Making Decisions with the Environment in Mind

**G242:p0075:c004 · PDF 75**

At every level of the company, formally or not, decisions impacting the environment are made, directly or indirectly. Making these decisions compatible with digital sustainability requires stalwart decision management that favors balance.

**G242:p0075:c005 · PDF 75**

Decision-making is the key element of governance and must be managed accordingly. Decision-making is critical to design choices having a measurable impact on business; those choices are usually seen as difficult to make and/or expensive to change.

**G242:p0075:c006 · PDF 75**

This new decision-making process is based on two essential notions of the architectural governance framework: the decision log and the decision repository.

**G242:p0075:c007 · PDF 75**

The Decision Log

**G242:p0075:c008 · PDF 75**

The decision log makes it possible to list the various criteria that the organization wishes to put forward in its decision-making – these should include the environmental criterion. The decision log enables the company to track decisions, share information among staff, and update this information.

**G242:p0075:c009 · PDF 75**

Table 3-5 shows a decision-making example where a company wishes to set up a new mobile application for its customers. (Note this is only an example as the development of a mobile application requires in-depth case-by-case analysis.)

**G242:p0076:c000 · PDF 76**

**G242:p0076:c001 · PDF 76**

| Meaning | Development of a<br>solution for each<br>mobile operating<br>system (iOS®,<br>Android™, etc.). | Development of a web-based<br>common core integrating with<br>operating system-dependent<br>solutions. | Development of a common<br>solution for all operating<br>systems. |
|  | Hosting of mobile applications on the organization’s hosting platform. |  |  |

To add the environmental criterion to decision-making, it is important to have reference tables enabling fast and efficient measurement of CO2e emissions, rare metals extraction, and drinkable water use, according to the company’s assumptions. This reference data can come from multiple sources.

**G242:p0076:c003 · PDF 76**

Here, the company weighed the environmental criterion across several fundamental criteria, such as finance, functional coverage, and customer experience.

**G242:p0077:c000 · PDF 77**

**G242:p0077:c001 · PDF 77**

The decision log can be recorded in a decision repository that is shared within the company, ensuring traceability, sharing, updatability, and availability of decisions.

<a id="3.9.2"></a>
## 建立与环境议题一致的决策验证机构

原文：3.9.2. Setting Up Decision Validation Bodies Aligned with the Environmental Issue。PDF 物理页：[78]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在能源转型背景下治理信息系统，需要调整决策机构，把具有重大环境影响的决策纳入其中。为此，必须有标准来判断某项决策是否应提交治理机构。表 3-6 给出了此类标准的示例。

> **注**：决策机构汇集所有利益相关者，通过考虑所有观点来促进决策。

**表 3-6：将决策提交决策委员会的标准示例**

| 标准 | 论证 |
|---|---|
| 新的应用托管服务 | 更换托管服务是关乎信息系统能耗、进而关乎其环境影响的关键选择。 |
| 新的应用服务 | 任何新的应用组件都会消耗额外资源。 |
| 排放超过 Y tCO2e | 公司认为超过该阈值后，环境影响即为显著。 |

视组织规模，可能存在多个治理层级。依据其环境影响，一项决策可以仅被记录、然后传达以供认可，或按所选标准上报至相关机构。这一过程类似于基于预算影响进行决策的过程。

图3-15：分布式治理模型；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

相关结构化资料：[独立图示解析 fig3-15](../figures/G242/fig3-15.json)。图示模型、标签、关系与视觉状态单独维护；章节文本校订不升级图示审查状态。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 提交治理委员会的条件是可裁剪示例，含Y tCO2e占位阈值。
  - 适用边界：不能把Y或示例条件当已生效企业规则。
  - PDF 78：| Criterion | Substantiation |
| --- | --- |
| New application hosting service | Changing the hosting service is a critical choice regarding the<br>information system’s energy consumption, and therefore its<br>environmental impact. |
| New application service | Any new application component consumes additional resources. |
| Emission greater than Y tCO2e | The company deems that beyond this threshold, the environmental<br>impact is significant. |
- 不同环境影响可对应记录、认可或升级委员会等治理层级。
  - 适用边界：recorded、endorsed和escalated不能合并成approved。
  - PDF 78：Several levels of governance are possible depending on the size of the organization. Depending on its environmental impact, a decision may just be recorded, then communicated for endorsement, or be escalated to the relevant body according to the chosen criteria. This process is like the one used in budget impact-based decision-making.

3.9.2. Setting Up Decision Validation Bodies Aligned with the Environmental Issue

**G242:p0078:c001 · PDF 78**

Governing the information system in a context of energy transition requires adjusting the decision- making bodies, by including decisions with a significant environmental impact. To achieve this, there must be criteria determining whether a decision should be submitted to a governance body. Table 3-6 shows examples of such criteria.

**G242:p0078:c002 · PDF 78**

A decision-making body assembles all stakeholders to facilitate decision-making by considering all viewpoints.

**G242:p0078:c003 · PDF 78**

Table 3-6: Example Criteria for Submitting a Decision to a Decision Committee

**G242:p0078:c004 · PDF 78**

| Criterion | Substantiation |
| --- | --- |
| New application hosting service | Changing the hosting service is a critical choice regarding the<br>information system’s energy consumption, and therefore its<br>environmental impact. |
| New application service | Any new application component consumes additional resources. |
| Emission greater than Y tCO2e | The company deems that beyond this threshold, the environmental<br>impact is significant. |

**G242:p0078:c005 · PDF 78**

Several levels of governance are possible depending on the size of the organization. Depending on its environmental impact, a decision may just be recorded, then communicated for endorsement, or be escalated to the relevant body according to the chosen criteria. This process is like the one used in budget impact-based decision-making.

<a id="3.9.3"></a>
## 制定各级生态责任决策的架构原则

原文：3.9.3. Setting up Architecture Principles for Eco-Responsible Decision-Making at All Levels。PDF 物理页：[79, 80, 81, 82]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

多数决策是非正式地、在运营团队的日常工作中做出的。指南有助于所有员工的决策向信息系统的可持续性战略收敛。

这些指南可以通过宣言、标准或流程图等方式传达。

> **注**：架构原则定义应适用于公司中每个系统的一般属性。

图 3-16 展示了公司架构原则的示例。

**表 3-7：可持续性的关键公司架构原则说明**

| 公司架构原则 | 描述 | 论证 |
|---|---|---|
| 1：培训、内部与外部沟通 | 为员工、管理者和股东提供关于能源/气候议题及数字技术环境影响的培训。严格遵循对外沟通流程，在内部与外部就可持续 ICT 方法进行沟通。 | 使所有利益相关者理解重大变革的必要性。影响市场，使其演进。品牌：对客户与员工的吸引力。 |
| 2：数字服务环境足迹的透明度量 | 度量组织数字服务的端到端环境足迹，并把结果传达给员工、供应商、客户、伙伴等。从覆盖整个产品生命周期的 CO2e 开始，必要时再转向其他指标，以提升认识并做出减排决策。 | 向客户展示范围 3 影响，使其具备减少自身环境影响的手段。外部透明度会强制产生更多成果。品牌：对客户与员工的吸引力。法规：符合国家法规的透明度要求。 |
| 3：负责任地使用数字服务与设备 | 避免任何不必要之事；避免急于采用新技术（它往往不过是炒作）。帮助用户在使用服务时更加节俭。通过配额（数据上限、网络使用上限等）设置约束与限制。 | 限制使用会降低对技术系统的需求，而这些系统的生产与使用会对环境产生影响。法规：法国参议院的一份报告建议对电话套餐的供给加以规范，并限制视频使用的影响 [French Senate]。 |
| 4：使用共享的、可持续的数字服务 | 建立新数字服务时，其设计与构建应最大化复用与共享。例如，视供给情况，鼓励从负责任的供应商处租用 ICT 设备与服务（计算机、电话、基础设施等）。 | 共享化降低了所需资源的总量。组织需要的是数字设备提供的服务，并不需要管理数字废弃物。选择承诺覆盖产品全生命周期的供应商，自然会推动供应商走向可持续。 |
| 5：知识共享 | 使用开源、开放数据、开放架构、创客实验室（fabrication laboratory）。鼓励并贡献于“开放”倡议。学会分享；灌输分享文化：编写文档；通过分离通用结构与具体内容来构建架构（TOGAF 标准的架构连续体原则）。参与标准化进程。 | 选择开源可以成为避免技术过时、延长硬件使用期限的解决方案 [Mobbs, 2012]。分享一切在无害于组织的前提下可以分享之物，以避免重复发明轮子的环境成本；把精力集中于可持续转型。更开放与共享将促进互操作；例如在底层组件上，从而促进可维修性。最后，优化（原则 7）只需做一次。品牌：对客户与员工的吸引力。 |
| 6：设备的再利用、维修与回收 | 追求高效：在设备仍可使用时继续使用，并在更换后确保将其关闭。倾向于循环经济。维修而非更换。鼓励再利用，尤其是可回收/已回收的组件。 | 再利用与维修：同时限制材料使用与能源消耗。运营效率：不购买新设备可节省成本。 |
| 7：资源优化、减少污染 | 不仅优化基础设施使用，也优化制造与维修：能效、使用低碳能源、限制材料使用、选择可再生材料等。 | 减少运营的环境影响。 |
| 8：设计可维修、可复用的硬件与服务 | 倾向于模块化设计，按 TOGAF 标准所述，以互操作性与构建块等概念组装可互操作、可复用的组件。 | 便于架构模式、设计、理念以及硬件或软件的复用、维护与共享。运营效率：不购买新硬件或新服务可降低成本。 |
| 9：选择应用这些原则的供应商 | 了解所购产品与服务的环境影响，以度量组织的范围 3 并采取行动减少它。促进本地生产与维修（即减少运输需求）。选择让其客户参与产品开发决策的供应商。 | 组织使用许多供应商与伙伴。价值链经常被重构，导致责任范围变化以及环境影响在国家之间、组织之间转移。要完全有效，这些原则必须应用于整个价值链（上游与下游）。物理上的接近减少了运输距离，从而减少能耗。参与服务与产品开发决策，可以保证其随时间推移对组织仍然相关。 |

这份清单旨在启发组织；它们必须将其内化并加以调整。它是为推动信息系统走向可持续状态而制定的，基于以下文献中所阐述的原则：

- TOGAF 标准 [C220]；
- 杠杆点：系统中的干预位置（Leverage points: places to intervene in a system）[Systems Thinking]；
- 《共生经济》（The Symbiotic Economy）[Delannoy, 2017]。

此外还有由致力于数字可持续性的参与者制定的参考原则：

- 由 Green IT 协会提出的软件生态设计原则⁶；
- 由美国环境保护署提出的绿色工程原则⁷。

基于原则与模式进行治理，是促进敏捷团队自我治理与自主性的若干良好实践之一。每个架构组织、每位架构师都有责任根据语境调整这些原则，将其与最相关的模式关联，并提出最有效的技术标准。

与决策一样，新的宣言、标准与流程图必须依据环境标准接受评估，并由专门机构批准。

> **脚注**：参见：www.greenit.fr。／参见：www.epa.gov。

图3-16：可持续信息系统的治理原则；The Shift Project Lean-ICT工作组。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 架构原则清单用于启发，组织应自行采用和调整。
  - 适用边界：示例原则不自动成为项目强制要求；需原则所有者、批准状态和例外。
  - PDF 82：This list is intended to inspire organizations; they will have to make it their own and adapt it. It was developed to move the information system towards a sustainable state. It is based on the principles explained in:
- 新的宣言、标准和流程应经过环境评价并由相应机构批准。
  - 适用边界：AI可生成草稿和影响分析，不得虚构委员会决议。
  - PDF 82：Just like decisions, new manifestos, standards, and flowcharts must be evaluated against environmental criteria, and validated by a dedicated body.

3.9.3. Setting up Architecture Principles for Eco-Responsible Decision-Making at All Levels

**G242:p0079:c001 · PDF 79**

Most decisions are made informally, during day-to-day work among operational teams. Guidelines help all employee decisions converge towards the information system’s sustainability strategy.

**G242:p0079:c002 · PDF 79**

These guidelines can be communicated, for instance, through manifestos, standards, or flowcharts.

**G242:p0079:c003 · PDF 79**

Architecture Principles define general properties that should apply to every system in the company.

Work Group)

Table 3-7: Description of Key Company Architecture Principles for Sustainability

**G242:p0080:c001 · PDF 80**

| Company Architecture<br>Principle | Description | Substantiation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1: Training, internal,<br>and external<br>communication | Provide employees, managers, and<br>shareholders with training on the<br>energy/climate issue and the environmental<br>impact of digital technology.<br>Rigorously communicate, internally and<br>externally, on the sustainable ICT approach<br>by following the external communication<br>processes. | Make all stakeholders understand the need for<br>significant change. Weigh on the market so it<br>evolves.<br>Brand: Attractiveness for customers and<br>employees. |
| 2: Transparent<br>measurement of the<br>environmental<br>footprint of digital<br>services | Measure the end-to-end environmental<br>footprint of the organization’s digital<br>services, and communicate the results to<br>employees, suppliers, customers, partners,<br>etc.<br>Start with COe over the entire product<br>2<br>lifecycle and move towards other indicators<br>when necessary to raise awareness and<br>make reduction decisions. | Making Scope 3 impact visible to customers<br>gives them the means to reduce their<br>environmental impact.<br>External transparency enforces more results.<br>Brand: Attractiveness for customers and<br>employees.<br>Regulations: Comply with transparency<br>requirements of national regulations |
| 3: Responsible use of<br>digital services and<br>equipment | Avoid anything that is not necessary; avoid<br>rushing into adopting new technology (which<br>often turns out to be nothing more than<br>hype). Help users be more frugal with their<br>use of services. Set constraints and limits via<br>quotas (data cap, network usage cap, etc.). | Limiting use reduces demand for technical<br>systems whose production and use have an<br>impact on the environment.<br>Regulations: A note from the French Senate<br>suggests regulating the offer of phone plans<br>and limiting the impact of video usage [French<br>Senate]. |
| 4: Use of shared,<br>sustainable digital<br>services | When a new digital service is put in place, it<br>should be designed and built to maximize<br>reuse and sharing.<br>For instance, encourage renting ICT<br>equipment and services (computers, phones,<br>infrastructure, etc.) from responsible<br>suppliers, depending on the offer. | Mutualization reduces the total amount of<br>resources required.<br>The organization needs the service provided<br>by digital equipment; it does not need to<br>manage digital waste.<br>Choosing suppliers committed to their<br>products’ entire lifecycle will naturally make<br>suppliers evolve towards sustainability. |

| Company Architecture<br>Principle | Description | Substantiation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 5: Knowledge sharing | Use open source, open data, open<br>architecture, fabrication laboratory.<br>Encourage and contribute to “open”<br>initiatives.<br>Learn to share; instill a culture of sharing:<br>document, build architectures by separating<br>generic structure from specifics (the<br>Architecture Continuum principle of the<br>TOGAF Standard). Get involved in<br>standardization processes. | Choosing open source can be a solution to<br>avoid technical obsolescence and allow longer<br>usage of hardware [Mobbs, 2012].<br>Share everything that can be shared without<br>harming the organization, to avoid the<br>environmental costs of reinventing the wheel;<br>focus efforts on sustainable transformation.<br>More openness and sharing will promote<br>interoperability; for example, on low-level<br>components, and therefore repairability.<br>Finally, optimization (Principle 7) will only<br>have to be done once.<br>Brand: Attractiveness for customers and<br>employees. |

Favor a circular economy.<br>Repair rather than replace. Encourage reuse,<br>in particular recyclable/recycled<br>components. | Reuse and repair: limit both the use of<br>materials and energy consumption.<br>Operational efficiency: not buying new<br>equipment saves costs. |

<a id="3.9.4"></a>
## 监控并评估架构对环境目标的合规性

原文：3.9.4. Monitoring and Assessing the Architecture’s Compliance with Environmental Goals。PDF 物理页：[83]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

监视并验证架构的合规性，可以及早发现偏离可持续性目标的偏差，从而要么向目标重新对齐，要么识别出“环境债务”（见 3.9.5 节）。

“验收标准”是通过提供证据来确认实施与架构决策相适配的手段。

> **示例**
>
> 验收标准：应用 X 中已不再使用的“交付”服务被下线，并在 CMDB 中被正确跟踪。
>
> 理由：符合“最大化功能与应用数据流的复用”原则——在处理完某项功能后，公司不得忘记将未使用的功能下线。

> **注**：某些检查也可以通过利用软件生产线实现自动化。例如在网站设计中：实现模式可以遵循“尽可能避免数据传输”原则，例如缓存数据或使用内容分发网络（CDN）。这些模式的使用可以通过软件生产线（CI/CD）自动化。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 验收标准要提供实施符合架构决定的证明。
  - 适用边界：设计意图、测试计划、实测结果和验收批准分开保存。
  - PDF 83：“Acceptance criteria” are means to confirm the adequacy of the implementation with the architectural decision, by providing proof.
- 未使用服务下线并在CMDB正确跟踪是验收例子。
  - 适用边界：只画目标状态或开退役工单不证明下线完成。
  - PDF 83：Acceptance criterion: The “delivery” service of application X that is no longer used is decommissioned and this is properly tracked in the CMDB.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0083:c000 · PDF 83**

3.9.4. Monitoring and Assessing the Architecture’s Compliance with Environmental Goals

**G242:p0083:c001 · PDF 83**

Monitoring and verifying the compliance of the architecture enables the early detection of deviations from sustainability goals, which then enables either a realignment towards the goal, or identification of an “environmental debt” (see Section 3.9.5).

**G242:p0083:c002 · PDF 83**

“Acceptance criteria” are means to confirm the adequacy of the implementation with the architectural decision, by providing proof.

**G242:p0083:c003 · PDF 83**

Acceptance criterion: The “delivery” service of application X that is no longer used is decommissioned and this is properly tracked in the CMDB.

**G242:p0083:c004 · PDF 83**

Justification: Conforming to the “Maximize reuse of functions and application data flow” principle, after having processed a functionality, the company must not forget to decommission unused features.

**G242:p0083:c005 · PDF 83**

Some checks can also be automated by leveraging the software production chain. For instance, with website design: implementation patterns can follow the “Avoid data transfer when possible” principle, such as caching data or using a Content Delivery Network (CDN). Use of these patterns can be automated via software production lines (CI/CD).

</details>

<a id="3.9.5"></a>
## 管理信息系统中的环境债务

原文：3.9.5. Managing Environmental Debt in the Information System。PDF 物理页：[83, 84]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在构建信息系统时未考虑环境因素，必然会导致债务。

> **注**：环境债务是与公司旨在建立可持续信息系统的轨迹不相一致的决策所导致的结果。

必须对债务加以管理，使其能够按照既定的可持续战略被妥善吸收。它可以按若干标准分类，例如原则、标准，或超过后即认为架构不可接受的阈值。信息系统的债务可以记录在“债务日志”（debt journal）中。

> **示例**
>
> 验收标准：信息系统中存在重复的功能。
>
> 理由：参见“最大化功能与应用数据流的复用”原则。

重要的是，在做出决策时要意识到有意债务的产生——这可能源于时间约束、法律约束等。它必须被识别、跟踪和度量。同样重要的是，在架构审计中要意识到无意债务的产生。

最后，债务必须系统性地导向一份消除计划的制定，说明它将如何随时间减少。应定期评审债务，以确保消除计划得到妥善执行。

**债务示例**

| 项目 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 决策引用 | #1 |
| 债务 | 交付发起（delivery initiation）在各类渠道中被重复实现。 |
| 描述 | 历史上，每个渠道都定义了自己的交付功能，使所需资源数量随渠道数量成倍增加。 |
| 消除计划 | 把交付功能合并为一个足够原子化、可满足所有特殊要求的服务。 |
| 环境成本 | 1,000 kgCO2e/年 |
| 风险 | 未能达成 CO2 排放减少 30% 的年度目标（碳税）。 |

> **注**：技术债务度量是大多数组织关注的问题。在此之上，我们现在又增加了环境债务度量。新的云原生架构与日益自动化的软件生产线，如今使用户能够设想基于功能冗余或基于数据流的债务识别。

第3章尾注[5]：一种通过Web交付的应用软件，能够向用户提供与原生应用或移动应用相同的界面。

相关结构化资料：[完整结构化表格](../tables/G242.json)。保留官方表空白、单位、示例状态及异常；实施契约在独立解析层补足。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 环境债务来自偏离组织可持续轨迹的决策。
  - 适用边界：债务需关联具体决定、影响、责任及处理计划，不自动等于技术债务。
  - PDF 83：Environmental debt is the consequence of a decision misaligned with the trajectory of the company aiming to establish a sustainable information system.
- 源文把重复功能列为“Acceptance criterion”的例子。
  - 适用边界：该文本实际上描述待治理的重复状态，不能据标签判定验收通过。
  - PDF 83：Acceptance criterion: Duplicated functionality in the information system.
- 1000 kg/年及年度30%目标是债务示例数据。
  - 适用边界：不能作为项目实测或通用目标；“碳税”残缺表述不产生现行税务义务。
  - PDF 84：| Decision Reference | #1 |
| --- | --- |
| Debt | Delivery initiation is duplicated across channel types. |
| Description | Historically, every channel has defined its own delivery functionality, multiplying the<br>number of required resources by the number of channels. |
| Elimination Plan | Coalesce delivery functionality into a service that is atomic enough to meet all specifics. |
| Environmental Cost | 1,000 kgCO2e/year |
| Risk | Failure to meet the annual target of a 30% reduction in CO2 emissions carbon tax. |

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0083:c006 · PDF 83**

3.9.5. Managing Environmental Debt in the Information System

**G242:p0083:c007 · PDF 83**

An information system built without considering the environmental aspect will inevitably lead to debt.

**G242:p0083:c008 · PDF 83**

Environmental debt is the consequence of a decision misaligned with the trajectory of the company aiming to establish a sustainable information system.

**G242:p0083:c009 · PDF 83**

Debt must be managed so that it can be properly absorbed in accordance with the established sustainability strategy. It can be classified along several criteria, such as the principles, standards, or thresholds beyond which the architecture is considered unacceptable. The information system’s debt can be recorded in the “debt journal”.

**G242:p0083:c010 · PDF 83**

Acceptance criterion: Duplicated functionality in the information system.

**G242:p0083:c011 · PDF 83**

Justification: See the “Maximize the reuse of functions and application data flows” principle.

**G242:p0083:c012 · PDF 83**

It is important to be aware of the creation of intentional debt when taking a decision – this may be due to time constraints, legal constraints, etc. It must be identified, tracked, and measured. It is also important to be aware of the creation of unintentional debt during architecture audits.

**G242:p0083:c013 · PDF 83**

Finally, debt must systematically lead to the creation of an elimination plan describing how it will be reduced over time. Debt should be reviewed on a recurring basis to ensure the proper execution of the elimination plans.

**G242:p0084:c000 · PDF 84**

Examples of Debt

**G242:p0084:c001 · PDF 84**

| Decision Reference | #1 |
| --- | --- |
| Debt | Delivery initiation is duplicated across channel types. |
| Description | Historically, every channel has defined its own delivery functionality, multiplying the<br>number of required resources by the number of channels. |
| Elimination Plan | Coalesce delivery functionality into a service that is atomic enough to meet all specifics. |
| Environmental Cost | 1,000 kgCO2e/year |
| Risk | Failure to meet the annual target of a 30% reduction in CO2 emissions carbon tax. |

**G242:p0084:c002 · PDF 84**

Technical debt measurement is a concern in most organizations. To this, we now add environmental debt measurement. New native cloud architectures and increasingly automated software production chains now enable the user to imagine functional redundancy-based or data flow-based debt identification.

**G242:p0084:c003 · PDF 84**

Endnotes

**G242:p0084:c004 · PDF 84**

[1] Moore’s law is the observation that the number of transistors in a dense Integrated Circuit (IC) doubles about every two years. Moore’s law is an observation and projection of a historical trend. Rather than a law of physics, it is an empirical relationship linked to gains from experience in production. Source: en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moore%27s_law. [2] Climate Fresk: climatefresk.org/; Digital Collage: digitalcollage.org/. [3] Refer to: www.bcorporation.net/en-us/certification. [4] FinOps is an evolving cloud financial management discipline and cultural practice that enables organizations to get maximum business value by helping engineering, finance, technology, and business teams to collaborate on data-driven spending decisions. Refer to: www.finops.org/introduction/what-is-finops/. [5] Type of application software delivered through the web, able to provide the user with the same interface as a native or mobile app. [6] Refer to: www.greenit.fr. [7] Refer to: www.epa.gov.

</details>

<a id="4"></a>
## 过程与方法：使用 TOGAF ADM 建立数字可持续性能力

原文：4. Process and Methodology: Using the TOGAF ADM to Build a Digital Sustainability Capability。PDF 物理页：[85]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本章的建议是利用完整的 TOGAF ADM，为向可持续信息系统的渐进式转型提供治理与决策支持。

本章描述如何利用 TOGAF ADM，以由过渡架构支撑的渐进方式，把数字可持续性能力从基线架构交付到目标架构。它把 ADM 各阶段与数字可持续性能力中所述的活动对应起来，并提出交付它们的顺序。这一对应关系是为了提出一条连贯的、在利用 TOGAF ADM 所提供工具的同时建立数字可持续性能力的路径。本章并非自成一体的描述，应与 TOGAF 标准结合阅读。

对每个阶段，本章重述其高层目标，并针对数字可持续性的特定主题加以定制；输入、步骤与输出仅在相关时提及。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ADM映射从基线到目标支持可持续能力的演进。
  - 适用边界：本章不是独立完整ADM替代，应结合TOGAF基础内容及项目裁剪。
  - PDF 85：This chapter describes how to leverage the TOGAF ADM to deliver a digital sustainability capability from a Baseline to a Target Architecture in an incremental approach supported by Transition Architecture. It maps ADM phases with activities described in the digital sustainability capability and it proposes a sequence to deliver them. The mapping was done to propose a coherent sequence that could be followed to set up a digital sustainability capability while leveraging the tooling provided by TOGAF ADM. This is not intended as a self-standing description, and it should be read in conjunction with the TOGAF Standard.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0085:c000 · PDF 85**

4. Process and Methodology: Using the TOGAF

**G242:p0085:c001 · PDF 85**

ADM to Build a Digital Sustainability Capability

**G242:p0085:c002 · PDF 85**

In this chapter, the proposition is to leverage the full TOGAF ADM to support governance and decision- making for the incremental transition towards a sustainable information system.

**G242:p0085:c003 · PDF 85**

This chapter describes how to leverage the TOGAF ADM to deliver a digital sustainability capability from a Baseline to a Target Architecture in an incremental approach supported by Transition Architecture. It maps ADM phases with activities described in the digital sustainability capability and it proposes a sequence to deliver them. The mapping was done to propose a coherent sequence that could be followed to set up a digital sustainability capability while leveraging the tooling provided by TOGAF ADM. This is not intended as a self-standing description, and it should be read in conjunction with the TOGAF Standard.

**G242:p0085:c004 · PDF 85**

For each phase, the high-level objectives are reminded and customized to the specific topic of digital sustainability while inputs, steps, and output are mentioned only when relevant.

</details>

## 预备阶段

原文：4.1. Preliminary Phase。PDF 物理页：[85, 86]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在每个组织中启动建立数字可持续性能力的转型过程，需要一个前提性的可持续文化；这种文化可以来自生态系统，和/或来自高层管理者与 CSR 团队所推动的内部文化变革。

这种文化在某一时点将使组织能够决定启动转型，以创建数字可持续性能力——这将成为 TOGAF ADM 预备阶段的起点。

**目标**

- 确定组织所期望的数字可持续性能力；
- 为组织建立该数字可持续性能力。

**输入**

- 企业外部的参考材料：
  - 评审主管部门的可持续性要求与现有可持续性标准；参见 3.7.4 节；
  - 核查可用的环境足迹度量标准，并选择适合企业业务者；参见 3.6.1 节。
- 非架构输入：
  - 企业可持续发展战略。

**步骤**

- 确认并细化可持续性架构原则，包括业务原则；指导见 3.9.3 节。

**输出**

- 值得正式化的“数字可持续性能力架构工作请求”（Request for Architecture Work）；它可作为对内以及最终对供应商和生态系统沟通的主题；
- 可持续性架构原则。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 预备阶段需审查外部要求并选择合适的环境计量标准。
  - 适用边界：未选择范围、版本、方法前不宣称核算符合标准。
  - PDF 85：◦Check available standards on environmental footprint measure and choose the one relevant to
  - PDF 85：◦Perform a review of authorities’ sustainability requirements and existing sustainability
  - PDF 85：standards; refer to Section 3.7.4
  - PDF 85：the enterprise business; refer to Section 3.6.1

## 阶段 A：架构愿景

原文：4.2. Phase A: Architecture Vision。PDF 物理页：[86, 87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**目标**

- 形成关于数字可持续性能力以及拟议企业架构所交付业务价值的高层、令人向往的愿景。

  即第 3 章所述数字可持续性能力各项活动在转型结束时预期达到的成熟度。

- 获得对架构工作说明书（Statement of Architecture Work）的批准，该说明书定义一个用于开发和部署架构愿景所述架构的工作计划。

**步骤**

- 建立架构项目；
- 识别利益相关者、关注点与业务需求：高管赞助的重要性见 3.4.3 节；
- 确认并细化业务目标、业务驱动与约束：理解组织的“气候战略”，并一致地定义数字可持续目标；
- 评估能力；
- 评估业务转型的就绪度；
- 定义范围；
- 确认并细化可持续性架构原则，包括业务原则；
- 制定可持续架构战略；3.4.3 节提供指导；
- 形成关于可持续信息系统在 **10 年**后应为何种形态的架构愿景，以促成具体行动、传达所需变革的规模并建立紧迫感；
- 定义目标架构的价值主张与 KPI：需要计算信息系统碳足迹的基线，并定义与企业承诺一致的高层改进目标；关于度量的指导见 3.6 节；
- 识别业务转型风险与缓解活动；关于风险评估的指导见 3.4.2 节；
- 编写架构工作说明书并取得批准。

**输出**

- 获批的架构工作说明书；
- 可持续信息系统战略：指导见 3.4 节；
- 可持续 ICT 架构愿景；
- 沟通计划；见 3.3.3 节。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 阶段A要求架构工作说明书获批准。
  - 适用边界：生成文档状态为草稿，批准只能来自实际授权者和记录。
  - PDF 86：• Obtain approval for a Statement of Architecture Work that defines a program of works to develop and deploy the architecture outlined in the Architecture Vision
- 长期愿景采用十年视角以支持变革准备。
  - 适用边界：不等于所有项目必须十年完成，需设置可检验过渡成果。
  - PDF 86：• Develop an Architecture Vision of what the sustainable information system should be in a 10-year delay to allow concrete action, give a sense of the change required and create a sense of urgency
- 目标价值主张与KPI需建立碳基线。
  - 适用边界：无基线时标记未评估，不生成实绩或百分比改善。
  - PDF 86：• Define the Target Architecture value propositions and KPIs: a baseline of the information system’s carbon footprint needs to be calculated with the definition of a high-level improvement target aligned with enterprise commitments; guidance on the measure is provided in Section 3.6

## 阶段 B：业务架构

原文：4.3. Phase B: Business Architecture。PDF 物理页：[87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**目标**

- 开发目标业务架构，描述企业为达成业务目标、响应架构愿景中提出的战略驱动应如何运作，并以处理架构工作说明书与利益相关者关注的方式加以表达；
- 基于基线与目标业务架构之间的差距，识别候选的架构路线图组件。

**步骤**

- 开发目标业务架构描述：细化阶段 A（架构愿景）所设定的高层环境目标。

**输出**

- 数字可持续教育计划，含可用培训清单与技能清单；指导见 3.3.2 节；
- 采购战略；见 3.7.3 节。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 阶段B包括教育项目、可用培训及技能清单，另含采购战略。
  - 适用边界：知识文章不等同组织已建立培训、岗位或采购能力。
  - PDF 87：• Digital sustainability Education Program with list of available trainings, list of skills; see Section 3.3.2 for guidance
  - PDF 87：• Procurement Strategy; see Section 3.7.3

## 阶段 C：信息系统架构——数据与应用架构

原文：4.4. Phase C: Information System Architecture – Data and Application Architecture。PDF 物理页：[87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**目标**

- 基于基线与目标信息系统（数据与应用）架构之间的差距，识别候选的架构路线图组件。


### 语义规则与适用边界

## 阶段 D：技术架构

- 开发目标技术架构，使架构愿景以及目标业务、数据与应用构建块能够通过技术组件与技术服务交付，并以处理架构工作说明书与利益相关者关注的方式加以表达；
- 基于基线与目标技术架构之间的差距，识别候选的架构路线图组件。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 技术架构差距支持路线图候选。

## 阶段 E：机会与解决方案

原文：4.6. Phase E: Opportunities and Solutions。PDF 物理页：[88]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

对数字可持续性而言，机会与解决方案阶段的分析可能特别复杂，因为它取决于生态系统的成熟度（供应商、用户、客户等）以及可用的外部解决方案。它需要与客户、提供方、伙伴及主管部门互动，以交付可持续的解决方案与服务。同时，扩大可用机会的数量、鼓励市场转向更可持续的解决方案也很重要。

更多细节参见 3.7 节。

**目标**

- 基于阶段 B、C、D 的差距分析与候选架构路线图组件，生成架构路线图的初始完整版本；
- 确定是否需要渐进方式；若需要，则识别能够持续交付业务价值的过渡架构；
- 基于 ABB 定义总体解决方案构建块（SBB），以最终确定目标架构：检查所选解决方案是否符合环境足迹度量的透明度要求，以及是否实现了环境影响的有效收缩。

  正是在这里，我们做出关于使用哪些技术以最大化可持续性目标收益的重要选择。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 是否需要增量实施及过渡架构应经过判断。
  - 适用边界：不强制固定数量过渡状态或按ADM阶段一一造图。
  - PDF 88：• Determine whether an incremental approach is required, and if so, identify Transition Architectures that will deliver continuous business value
- 从ABB确定SBB时检查方案足迹透明度及降低能力。
  - 适用边界：逻辑构建块不直接等于某个采购产品；需映射证据及比较。
  - PDF 88：• Define the overall Solution Building Blocks (SBBs) to finalize the Target Architecture based on the ABBs: check the compliance of the chosen solutions with the environmental footprint measure transparency requirement and an effective contraction of the environmental impact

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0088:c004 · PDF 88**

4.6. Phase E: Opportunities and Solutions

**G242:p0088:c005 · PDF 88**

For digital sustainability, the analysis of the Opportunities and Solutions phase could be particularly complex because it depends on the ecosystem maturity (suppliers, users, customers, etc.) and available external solutions. It requires engagement with customers, providers, partners, and authorities to deliver sustainable solutions and services. It is also important to multiply available opportunities and encourage a market shift towards more sustainable solutions.

**G242:p0088:c006 · PDF 88**

Refer to Section 3.7 for more details.

**G242:p0088:c007 · PDF 88**

Objectives:

**G242:p0088:c008 · PDF 88**

• Generate the initial complete version of the Architecture Roadmap, based upon the gap analysis and candidate Architecture Roadmap components from Phases B, C, and D

**G242:p0088:c009 · PDF 88**

• Determine whether an incremental approach is required, and if so, identify Transition Architectures that will deliver continuous business value

**G242:p0088:c010 · PDF 88**

• Define the overall Solution Building Blocks (SBBs) to finalize the Target Architecture based on the ABBs: check the compliance of the chosen solutions with the environmental footprint measure transparency requirement and an effective contraction of the environmental impact

**G242:p0088:c011 · PDF 88**

This is where we make the important choices about which technologies will be used to maximize the benefits to the sustainability objectives.

</details>

<a id="4.7"></a>
## 阶段 F：迁移规划

原文：4.7. Phase F: Migration Planning。PDF 物理页：[88, 89]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**目标**

- 最终确定架构路线图以及配套的实施与迁移计划；
- 确保实施与迁移计划与企业整体变革组合中管理和实施变革的方法相协调；
- 确保关键利益相关者理解工作包与过渡架构的业务价值与成本。

**步骤**

- 通过开展成本/效益评估与风险验证，为迁移项目排定优先级：环境足迹的度量引导优先级，并允许在影响规模与转型难度之间取得平衡；关于度量的指导见 3.6 节。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 迁移优先级应结合成本收益及风险验证。
  - 适用边界：应在同一生命周期、负载和边界比较，不能仅按单位碳强度排序。
  - PDF 89：• Prioritize the migration projects through the conduct of a cost/benefit assessment and risk validation: the measure of environmental footprint guide priorities and allow balancing the size of the impact and the difficulty of the transformation; guidance on the measure is provided in Section 3.6

## 阶段 G：实施治理

原文：4.8. Phase G: Implementation Governance。PDF 物理页：[89]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**目标**

- 确保实施项目符合目标架构；
- 对解决方案以及任何由实施驱动的架构变更请求，执行适当的架构治理职能。

**步骤**

- 开展企业架构合规审查，检查环境目标是否达成：指导见 3.9.4 节；
- 实施业务与 IT 运营：指导见 3.7.2 节。

**输出**

- 治理决策日志；参见 3.9.1 节。

- 实施治理检查环境目标是否实际交付。

  - PDF 89：• Perform Enterprise Architecture Compliance reviews to check that environmental targets are delivered: guidance is provided in Section 3.9.4

## ADM 架构需求管理

原文：4.10. ADM Architecture Requirements Management。PDF 物理页：[89, 90]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

需求管理阶段应基于具体的验证标准，其中碳足迹度量将是关键输入。事实上，度量对于对齐以及核查减排要求处于核心地位。关于度量的指导见 3.6 节。

**目标**

- 确保需求管理过程在所有相关 ADM 阶段得到持续运行；
- 管理在 ADM 周期或某一阶段的任何执行过程中识别出的架构需求；
- 确保相关架构需求在每一阶段执行时可供该阶段使用。

**输出**

- 可持续性度量框架；见 3.6 节。

每个企业都必须确定一份需求清单，但其中一些会出现在许多企业中，以实现可持续信息系统的目标，例如：

- 系统应允许度量其用电量；
- 系统应度量其来自用电与制造的温室气体足迹；
- 系统应稳定数据量、处理量、网络流量与所需设备数量的增长；
- 系统应向构建者提供准确的环境影响度量，使其能够选择影响更小的技术，并把高影响技术排除在范围之外；
- 系统应向其用户提供准确的环境影响度量，使其能够选择影响更小的使用方式并尽量减少使用；
- 系统不应包含成瘾性功能；
- 系统应鼓励其用户尽量减少使用。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 需求管理需具体可验证标准，碳计量是重要依据。
  - 适用边界：需求、设计、指标、测量和验收之间需双向追溯。
  - PDF 89：The Requirements Management phase should be based on concrete validation criteria, where the carbon footprint measure will be a key input. Indeed, measuring is central to alignment and to check reduction requirements. Guidance on the measure is provided in Section 3.6.
- 每个企业应确定自己的需求清单。
  - 适用边界：文中示例不是无条件适用的完整需求集；不得自动赋予批准或已实现状态。
  - PDF 90：A list of requirements will have to be determined for each enterprise, but some will appear in many of them to reach the goal a sustainable information system, such as:

4.10. ADM Architecture Requirements Management

**G242:p0089:c017 · PDF 89**

The Requirements Management phase should be based on concrete validation criteria, where the carbon footprint measure will be a key input. Indeed, measuring is central to alignment and to check reduction requirements. Guidance on the measure is provided in Section 3.6.

**G242:p0090:c000 · PDF 90**

Objectives:

**G242:p0090:c001 · PDF 90**

• Ensure that the Requirements Management process is sustained and operates for all relevant ADM phases

**G242:p0090:c002 · PDF 90**

• Manage architecture requirements identified during any execution of the ADM cycle or a phase

**G242:p0090:c003 · PDF 90**

• Ensure that relevant architecture requirements are available for use by each phase as the phase is executed

**G242:p0090:c004 · PDF 90**

Output:

**G242:p0090:c005 · PDF 90**

• Sustainability Measurement Framework; see Section 3.6

**G242:p0090:c006 · PDF 90**

A list of requirements will have to be determined for each enterprise, but some will appear in many of them to reach the goal a sustainable information system, such as:

**G242:p0090:c007 · PDF 90**

• The system should allow the measurement of its electricity consumption

**G242:p0090:c008 · PDF 90**

• The system should measure its GHG footprint from electricity and manufacturing

**G242:p0090:c009 · PDF 90**

• The system should stabilize the increase of data volume, processing, network traffic, amount of equipment required

**G242:p0090:c010 · PDF 90**

• The system should provide accurate measurements to those who build it on the environmental impact of allowing technical choices with less impact and put out of scope technologies with high impact

**G242:p0090:c011 · PDF 90**

• The system should provide its users with accurate measurements on the environmental impact of allowing usages choices with less impact and minimize usage

**G242:p0090:c012 · PDF 90**

• The system should be free of addictive features

**G242:p0090:c013 · PDF 90**

• The system should encourage its users to minimize their usage

</details>
