<a id="5.2"></a>
## 业务功能描述

原文：5.2. Business Function Descriptions。PDF 物理页：[91, 92, 93, 94, 95, 96, 97]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

除第 3.3 节至第 3.9 节外，本章还给出框架中每个详细功能的描述（高层功能描述见第 3.2 节）。

**表 5-1：详细参考模型功能描述**

| 高层功能组 | 详细业务功能 | 描述 |
|---|---|---|
| **发展可持续数字文化** | 组织并管理文化转型（Organize and Manage the Cultural Transformation） | 组织并管理组织的文化转型。例如，建立内部活跃社区，就数字可持续性主题思考与交流，或促使人们讨论数字可持续转型的各个方面；在团队中识别数字可持续性大使，从不同视角讨论该主题：开发与工程团队、架构与基础设施团队、ICT 采购团队、CSR 团队等。 |
| | 鼓励节俭创新（Encourage Frugal Innovation） | 数字可持续性需要节俭创新思维。鼓励协作者提出并分享改进数字可持续性的想法。 |
| | 推展沟通计划（Roll out a Communication Plan） | 就数字可持续性主题开展内部沟通：内部转型项目群；该主题的市场新闻（外部反馈、监管机构消息、供应商消息、市场愿景等）；组织跨部门人员活动，在团队之间建立联系与讨论。 |
| | 培养数字可持续性意识、管理技能、培训人员（Develop Digital Sustainability Awareness, Manage Skills, Train People） | 理解气候/能源/资源问题，是能够接受并执行转型的必要条件。所需变革重大且前所未有。必须组织关于气候与能源问题以及数字环境影响的总体培训。技能需按不同角色进行管理和培训：数字可持续性“公民”——组织中所有协作者应具备的通用技能；数字可持续性专家画像——数字碳度量专家、ICT 低碳设计专家（架构师）、ICT 资产管理/技术资产生命周期管理专家、低技术专家等。 |
| **制定并推展可持续信息系统战略** | 将风险正式化（Formalize the Risks） | 将数字可持续性项目群应缓解的风险正式化——气候风险、信息系统韧性、未来法规等。这些风险将决定是否应启动转型，以及若风险成真会导致转型失败。 |
| | 从高管层任命发起人（Nominate a Sponsor from Executive Management） | 识别发起人，理想情况是组织主管/高管与CIO。提高高管对 ICT 环境影响的认识。开展利益相关者分析与利益分析，明确识别组织中受该转型影响的利益相关者。 |
| | 对齐 CSR、业务与 ICT 部门（Align CSR, Business, and ICT Departments） | 数字可持续性主题位于 CSR（企业社会责任）、业务与 ICT 部门的交汇点。三方应对语境有共同认识，并就愿景达成一致。 |
| | 设定总体与职能级目标（Set Overall and Function-level Goals） | 定义数字可持续转型的目标与成果。目标将包括 CO2e 排放、用电量（kWh）等环境指标，也包括财务收益、安全风险缓释等。视组织而定，高层目标可拆分为各业务单元或职能的子目标。 |
| | 检查业务模式（Check the Business Model） | 检查组织当前业务模式是否接受数字可持续转型，并定义该变革发生的条件。该职能聚焦持续处理组织业务模式与数字可持续转型之间的兼容性。示例：若组织业务模式中盈利能力与客户消费或产生的数字数据量直接成正比，该业务模式可能与数字可持续举措不兼容，需要调整。 |
| | 构建并启动转型项目群（Build and Launch the Transformation Program） | 以定义良好的过渡架构与每一步应达到的清晰中间成果，构建转型旅程。该计划应与所有利益相关者达成一致并共享。 |
| **架构并构建可持续信息系统** | 陈述可持续信息系统的愿景（State the Vision of a Sustainable Information System） | 从业务架构与使用、应用与数据架构、技术架构三方面，构建低环境影响信息系统的总体愿景，并展示减少该影响的主要具体杠杆。 |
| | 以“基于用途”与“基于基础设施”的双重架构方法驱动转型（A Dual Use-Based and Infrastructure-Based Architecture Approach to Drive Transformation） | 可实施双重方法来识别可持续性需求，同时结合基于业务用途的方法与基于基础设施的方法。 |
| | 重塑业务架构（Reshape Business Architecture） | 采取行动规范 ICT 业务使用。主动与业务合作。采用最小化 ICT 资产（设备、数据、应用、网络等）的流程与思维。质询实际业务需求以保留必要组件，考虑低技术方案，并移除“锦上添花”。 |
| | 重塑应用架构（Reshape the Application Architecture） | 调整解决方案选择标准以纳入数字可持续性要求。将数据最小化定义为解决方案架构设计原则。所有数据都应通过适当完整的元数据与所有者记录，以便在设计时确定生命周期终止日期。使用开源开发框架或应用，也是缓解技术过时、延长设备生命周期的途径。 |
| | 重塑技术架构（Reshape Technical Architecture） | 与设备供应商合作延长基础设施生命周期，并妥善管理报废（从摇篮到坟墓）。优化基础设施设计以最小化设备数量。采购硬件时，提出延长使用、可维修与可回收要求。 |
| | 面向可持续性的敏捷方法（Agile Methodologies for Sustainability） | 以可持续性需求充实敏捷方法。 |
| | 可持续软件开发（Sustainable Software Development） | 软件开发能力应适应持续监控并最小化处理、网络流量、数据量、用电，最终降低环境影响（例如共享可持续发展指南，涉及应使用的框架、开源、图像格式使用等）。 |
| **从摇篮到坟墓度量信息系统环境影响** | 建立并共享从摇篮到坟墓的信息系统碳足迹度量（Build and Share Information System Carbon Footprint Measures from Cradle to Grave） | 度量应覆盖组织运营所需的全部 ICT 设备，无论由组织直接管理还是由其分包商管理：工作场所（笔记本电脑、智能手机、PC、显示器等）；用于存储、计算与交换组织数据的数据中心、物理机与网络设备；云服务（PaaS、SaaS、IaaS 等）。度量必须覆盖设备从摇篮到坟墓的全生命周期：建造阶段（所用物理资产工业生产过程的环境影响）；运行阶段（ICT 资产使用导致能耗所产生的环境影响）；回收阶段（用于回收机器的回收流程/技术的环境影响）。 |
| | 开展度量信息系统环境影响的活动（Develop Activities to Measure the Information System Environmental Impact） | 新项目/举措应通过适当度量清晰评估。该活动同时支持数字可持续性治理与 ICT 环境债务管理。 |
| | 向利益相关者提供其环境影响的度量（Provide to Stakeholders a Measure of their Environmental Impact） | 应让协作者在日常工作中意识到其数字行为的环境影响，例如所创建数据的影响。最终用户（包括协作者与客户）应得到支持，开发人员与架构师也应得到支持（例如按环境足迹标准评估其技术实现等）。该度量活动也是组织向可持续性文化转型的杠杆。 |
| **与客户、提供方、伙伴及主管部门互动，共同实现可持续的解决方案与服务** | 要求服务提供方提供端到端环境足迹透明度（Ask Service Providers for End-to-End Environmental Footprint Transparency） | 解决方案提供方、服务提供方与伙伴可能使用 ICT 设备提供组织购买的服务。由于其分散并连接在许多不同组织与系统中，必须要求其环境影响的透明度。没有所有参与者的透明度，就无法形成全球图景。组织必须要求该生态提供透明度，才能向客户与投资者呈现其价值链的完整环境足迹。 |
| | 调整招标与合同的选择标准（Adapt the Selection Criteria for Calls for Tenders and Contracts with Suppliers and Partners） | 调整提供方与伙伴的选择方式，包括其遵守与度量、降低环境影响相关的新合同条款的能力。 |
| | 定义采购战略（Define the Procurement Strategy） | 服务内包或外包的决策应考虑服务的环境影响。 |
| | 遵守并超越主管部门要求的监管环境报告（Comply and Go Beyond the Regulatory Environmental Reporting Requested by Authorities） | 遵守并尽可能超越所在国家/地区当前所有 ICT 环境足迹监管要求。利用国际标准进行环境影响计算。关注本地与国际正在制定的数字可持续性新法规。 |
| | 让客户参与数字可持续目标（Involve Customers in the Goal of Digital Sustainability） | 培养客户在使用组织服务或产品时对数字环境影响的认识。为其提供工具，调整行为/使用方式以降低环境影响。 |
| | 影响市场，通过贡献公开文章积累知识（Influence the Market, Capitalize on Knowledge by Contributing to Public Papers） | 通过参与该主题的活动与出版物等方式，对外沟通组织的数字可持续转型项目群。对外沟通是挑战自身方法、支持其他组织并借鉴他人良好实践的机会，也是让高管参与的好机会，可显著加速数字可持续性方面的具体成果。 |
| **运营可持续信息系统** | 管理数字设备清单（Manage Digital Equipment Inventories） | 组织所需数字设备的准确清单是环境影响度量的基础之一。应覆盖服务器与网络设备，也覆盖显示器、笔记本电脑、台式机、平板、智能手机、会议室视频设备、外设、打印机、电视屏幕等。清单可自动维护（联网设备）、通过采购与发票维护，或人工维护。ICT 资产清单管理是 CMDB 的职能之一。 |
| | 支持延长数字设备使用期限（Support the Increase in Usage Period of Digital Equipment） | 延长数字设备使用期限也要求改变总是购买全新设备（更时尚、性能更好）然后丢弃旧设备的习惯。对企业而言，这意味着为设备保护与维修投入更多资源。维修意味着为服务台与技术员工建立能力与文化，使其有能力与意愿处理电池更换、屏幕维修等基本常见问题。 |
| | 监控数字消费（Monitor Digital Consumption） | 随着存储、网络、处理等所有 ICT 功能的优化以及 SaaS 解决方案的发展，ICT 部门对服务器活动、数据量、连接数等指标的监控习惯有所丧失。数字服务消费与污染之间存在强关联。过去技术效率提升吸收了部分数字污染影响，但未来效率边界将会到来；在这种情况下，若用量继续增长，最坏情形是影响也随之增长。提供“按需”ICT 资源的云架构发展，催生了 ICT 部门称为 FinOps（财务运营）的新活动。它是一种促进组织云计算基础设施与成本共同责任的管理实践。良好实践可以是将环境 KPI（用电、GHG 排放）加入 FinOps 监控。 |
| | 应电网运营方要求对数据中心可中断削峰（Load-shedding for Data Centers on Interruptible Demand of Electricity Operator） | 在电网紧张（预计因物理约束与地缘冲突而更频繁）的背景下，组织可与当地电网运营公司签订合同，使其设施可脱离电网，从而避免大面积停电。企业侧需能够：关闭“锦上添花”的用电（如信息电视屏、工位双显示器等）；关闭“非强制”用电；推迟密集但非实时的数字消费；启动自有发电机（但通常需要化石能源储备运行，并产生高碳电力）；监控自身用电。 |
| **治理向可持续信息系统转型** | 定义治理原则与组织（Define Governance Principles and Organization） | 首先必须定义企业架构可持续性原则。然后，由于推动可持续变革的决策必须在不同层级做出，相关现有治理委员会必须正式更新其职权范围以遵守可持续性原则。若尚不存在，应创建新的治理机构以监控企业运营碳足迹；但最佳实践是尽可能依托现有治理。 |
| | 推动治理委员会运作（Animate Governance Committees） | 审查 ICT 治理委员会，确保：数字可持续性原则充分纳入 ICT 治理流程；信息系统环境债务像技术债务一样随时间跟踪；受数字可持续性主题影响的扩展利益相关者被纳入 ICT 治理，包括数字可持续性负责人、ICT 相关 CSR 负责人等。 |
| | 确保目标达成，协调举措与决策（Ensure that Objectives are Reached, Coordinate Initiatives and Decision-Making） | 通过不同工作流管理数字可持续转型项目群。定义数字可持续目标并获得利益相关者承诺。检查是否做出必要的转型决策，以达成目标〔原文为“read objectives”，疑为“reach objectives”〕或重新定义/更新目标。 |
| | 评估“提供可持续信息系统”能力的成熟度（Assess the Maturity of the Capability “Provide a Sustainable Information System”） | 构建数字可持续性成熟度评估模型（或使用现有模型）评估组织总体成熟度。应考虑参考模型的六个维度：战略；治理；业务、数据、应用与技术架构；文化；数字环境足迹度量；生态（提供方、伙伴、客户等）。 |
| | 评估可持续想法（Evaluate Sustainable Ideas） | 为交付“发展可持续数字文化”职能中引入的数字可持续创新能力，检查治理是否到位以考虑和评估新的创新想法。 |

- 成熟度模型可自行构建或使用现有模型，原文给六个维度。
  - 适用边界：六维不是七个高层功能；原文没有分级权重或分数，无证据不得编造成熟度。

**G242:p0091:c009 · PDF 91**

5.2. Business Function Descriptions

**G242:p0091:c010 · PDF 91**

In addition to Section 3.3 through Section 3.9, this chapter proposes a description of each detailed function of the framework.

**G242:p0092:c000 · PDF 92**

Table 5-1: Description of Detailed Reference Model Functions

**G242:p0092:c001 · PDF 92**

| Business Function | Description<br>(Description of High-Level Functions is provided in Section 3.2 |
| --- | --- |
| Developing a Sustainable Digital Culture |  |
| Organize and Manage the Cultural<br>Transformation | Organize and manage the cultural transformation of the organization. For<br>example, establish internal active communities to think and exchange ideas about<br>the topic or make people talk about the various aspects of the digital sustainability<br>transformation. Identify digital sustainability ambassadors in teams to talk about<br>the topic from different points of view: development and engineering teams,<br>architecture and infrastructure teams, ICT procurement teams, CSR team, etc. |
| Encourage Frugal Innovation | Digital sustainability needs a frugal innovation mindset. Encourage collaborators<br>to propose and share their ideas to improve the digital sustainability. |
| Roll out a Communication Plan | Communicate internally on the topic of digital sustainability:<br>About the internal transformation program<br>About market news on the topic: external feedback, regulator news, provider<br>news, market vision, etc.<br>Organize events with people from different departments to create contacts and<br>discussions among the teams. |
| Develop Digital Sustainability<br>Awareness, Manage Skills, Train<br>People | An understanding of the climate/energy/resources stakes is mandatory to be able<br>to accept the transformation and execute it. The required change is significant and<br>of an unprecedented nature. Overall training on climate and energy stakes and<br>digital environmental impact must be organized. Skills need to be managed and<br>trained according to different roles:<br>Digital sustainability “citizen”: common skills all collaborators should have in the<br>organization<br>Expert profiles on digital sustainability: digital carbon measure champions, ICT<br>low-carbon design experts (architects), ICT asset management/technical asset<br>lifecycle management experts, low-tech experts, etc. |
| Developing and Rolling Out a Sustainable Information System Strategy |  |
| Formalize the Risks | Formalize the risks that your digital sustainability program should mitigate<br>climate risks, information systems resilience, future regulations, etc.<br>These are the risks that will define whether you should launch the transformation<br>and, if realized, cause it to fail. |
| Nominate a Sponsor from<br>Executive Management | Identify sponsors, ideally a director of the organization and CIO. Raise awareness<br>among executives about ICT’s environmental impact.<br>Perform a stakeholder analysis and an interest analysis to clearly identify<br>stakeholders concerned by this transformation in the organization. |
| Align CSR, Business, and ICT<br>Departments | The digital sustainability topic is at the meeting point of the CSR (Corporate Social<br>Responsibility), Business, and ICT departments. The three parties should share the<br>same knowledge of the context and agree on the vision. |

<a id="6"></a>
## 集成方法：标准化云计算服务的环境数据共享

原文：6. Integration Methodologies: Standardizing Environmental Data Sharing about Cloud Computing Services。PDF 物理页：[98, 99]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

随着基础设施向超大规模数据中心迁移，对“云上”执行的服务环境影响进行准确报告的需求变得至关重要。事实上，获取硬件清单与用电量已不再可能。目前尚无标准支持服务提供方与消费者之间共享这些信息。本章提出一种用于此目的的数据标准建议（指南所提方案）。

以下拟议模型是依赖 ICT 设备的服务提供方应提供的最小数据集。它使使用此类服务的组织能够监控并减少其足迹。相关服务提供方包括基础设施即服务（IaaS）、平台即服务（PaaS）、软件即服务（SaaS）云提供方，以及更广泛地，任何依赖 ICT 设备销售的服务。

**表 6-1：服务提供方应提供的数据**

| 数据 | 描述 |
|---|---|
| 提供方名称（Provider name） | 服务提供方的名称。 |
| 提供方被评估的服务（Provider evaluated service） | 被评估的具体服务名称。 |
| 相关组织实体（Concerned organization entities） | 组织中使用该服务的实体清单。 |
| 期间（Period） | 度量期间：年/月。 |
| 用电量（kWh）（Electricity consumption） | 完整年度评估的用电量（kWh）。 |
| 采购的可再生电力（kWh）（Renewable electricity purchased） | 采购的可再生 kWh。 |
| 范围 2 市场法排放（kgCO2e）（Scope 2 Market-based emissions） | 按《温室气体核算体系》[GHG Protocol] 定义的市场法评估的完整年度范围 2 温室气体排放（kgCO2e）。 |
| 范围 2 地区法排放（kgCO2e）（Scope 2 Location-based emissions） | 按《温室气体核算体系》定义的地区法评估的完整年度范围 2 温室气体排放（kgCO2e）。 |
| PUE | 托管基础设施的电力使用效率（Power Usage Effectiveness）。 |
| 范围 2 数据计算说明（Comments on Scope 2 data calculation） | 提供方记录范围 2 排放评估的可选说明。 |
| 范围 3 排放总量（kgCO2e）（Total Scope 3 emissions） | 完整年度评估的范围 3 温室气体排放（kgCO2e）。包括提供方分配给客户组织服务的全球范围 3。 |
| 与数字设备相关的范围 3 排放（kgCO2e）（Scope 3 emissions linked to digital equipment） | 完整年度评估的范围 3 温室气体排放（kgCO2e）。仅包括提供方分配给客户组织服务的数字设备范围 3。 |
| 范围 3 数据计算说明（Comments on Scope 3 data calculation） | 提供方记录范围 3 排放评估的可选说明。 |
| 碳信用与抵消（Carbon credits and offsets） | 提供方因购买碳信用而从其排放中“折减”的排放量。 |
| 数字废弃物重量（kg）（Digital waste weight） | 完整年度内处置的数字设备重量（kg）。 |
| 回收的数字废弃物（kg）（Recycled digital waste） | 完整年度内收集用于回收的数字设备重量（kg）。 |
| 水消耗（体积）（Water consumption） | 制造阶段（采矿或工业活动所致）或使用阶段（例如冷却）消耗的水（升或立方米）。 |
| 存储数据量（GB）（Volumes of data stored） | 与服务相关的数据存储总量。如有复制，应提供包含复制的完整容量。 |
| 产生的网络流量（GB）（Network traffic generated） | 该期间服务产生的输入与输出网络流量总量。 |

相关结构化资料：[完整结构化表格](../tables/G242.json)。保留官方表空白、单位、示例状态及异常；实施契约在独立解析层补足。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 云服务环境数据共享是本章提出的建议方案。
  - 适用边界：“目前无标准”是源文时间语境，不是对当前市场的核实结论。
  - PDF 98：With the move of infrastructure to hyperscale data centers, the need for accurate reporting on the environmental impact of the services executed “on the cloud” is critical. Indeed, access to the hardware inventory and to the electricity consumption is no longer possible. There is currently no standard to support the sharing of this information between service providers and consumers. A data standard for that purpose is proposed in this chapter.
- 提供方报告模型为最低数据集合。
  - 适用边界：不是已经批准的通用OFP规范或充分的完整LCA数据模型。
  - PDF 98：The following proposed model is a minimal set of data to be provided by service providers that rely on ICT equipment. It would allow the organization that uses such services to monitor its footprint and reduce it. Services providers concerned are Infrastructure as a Service (IaaS), Platform as a Service (PaaS), Software as a Service (SaaS) cloud providers, and more extensively any service sold that relies on ICT equipment.
- 报告需标明服务提供方、服务及评估期间。
  - 适用边界：Period允许年/月而各指标描述完整年度，需显式统一期间，不能静默年化。
  - PDF 98：| Data | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Provider name | Name of the service provider. |
| Provider evaluated service | Name of the specific service evaluated. |
| Concerned organization entities | List of concerned entities in the organization using the service. |
| Period | Period of the measure: year/month. |
| Electricity consumption (kWh) | Consumed electricity in kWh evaluated for the complete year. |
| Renewable electricity purchased<br>(kWh) | Renewable kWh purchased. |
| Scope 2 Market-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the market-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |
| Scope 2 Location-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the location-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |
| PUE | Power Usage Effectiveness of the hosting infrastructure. |
| Comments on Scope 2 data calculation | Optional comments from the provider to document the Scope 2 emissions<br>evaluation. |
| Total Scope 3 emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 3) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year. Include<br>2<br>global Scope 3 of the providers that is allocated to the client organization’s<br>service. |
| Scope 3 emissions linked to digital<br>equipment (kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 3) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year. Include<br>2<br>only digital equipment Scope 3 of the providers that is allocated to the client<br>organization’s service. |
- 服务提供方Scope2市场法与地区法应各自记录。
  - 适用边界：不得相加；供应商视角Scope编号不直接照搬成客户视角Scope编号。
  - PDF 98：| Data | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Provider name | Name of the service provider. |
| Provider evaluated service | Name of the specific service evaluated. |
| Concerned organization entities | List of concerned entities in the organization using the service. |
| Period | Period of the measure: year/month. |
| Electricity consumption (kWh) | Consumed electricity in kWh evaluated for the complete year. |
| Renewable electricity purchased<br>(kWh) | Renewable kWh purchased. |
| Scope 2 Market-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the market-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |
| Scope 2 Location-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the location-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |

| Scope 3 emissions linked to digital<br>equipment (kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 3) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year. Include<br>2<br>only digital equipment Scope 3 of the providers that is allocated to the client<br>organization’s service. |
- 数字设备Scope3是提供方总Scope3的一个子范围。

| --- | --- |
| Provider name | Name of the service provider. |

- 表中Recycled digital waste定义为收集用于回收的重量。
  - 适用边界：收集量不等于已实际回收量或回收材料产出。

| --- | --- |

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G242:p0098:c000 · PDF 98**

6. Integration Methodologies: Standardizing

**G242:p0098:c001 · PDF 98**

Environmental Data Sharing about Cloud

**G242:p0098:c002 · PDF 98**

Computing Services

**G242:p0098:c003 · PDF 98**

With the move of infrastructure to hyperscale data centers, the need for accurate reporting on the environmental impact of the services executed “on the cloud” is critical. Indeed, access to the hardware inventory and to the electricity consumption is no longer possible. There is currently no standard to support the sharing of this information between service providers and consumers. A data standard for that purpose is proposed in this chapter.

**G242:p0098:c004 · PDF 98**

The following proposed model is a minimal set of data to be provided by service providers that rely on ICT equipment. It would allow the organization that uses such services to monitor its footprint and reduce it. Services providers concerned are Infrastructure as a Service (IaaS), Platform as a Service (PaaS), Software as a Service (SaaS) cloud providers, and more extensively any service sold that relies on ICT equipment.

**G242:p0098:c005 · PDF 98**

Table 6-1: Data to be Provided by Service Providers

**G242:p0098:c006 · PDF 98**

| Data | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Provider name | Name of the service provider. |
| Provider evaluated service | Name of the specific service evaluated. |
| Concerned organization entities | List of concerned entities in the organization using the service. |
| Period | Period of the measure: year/month. |
| Electricity consumption (kWh) | Consumed electricity in kWh evaluated for the complete year. |
| Renewable electricity purchased<br>(kWh) | Renewable kWh purchased. |
| Scope 2 Market-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the market-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |
| Scope 2 Location-based emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 2) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year, based on<br>2<br>the location-based methodology as defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol<br>[GHG Protocol]. |
| PUE | Power Usage Effectiveness of the hosting infrastructure. |
| Comments on Scope 2 data calculation | Optional comments from the provider to document the Scope 2 emissions<br>evaluation. |
| Total Scope 3 emissions<br>(kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 3) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year. Include<br>2<br>global Scope 3 of the providers that is allocated to the client organization’s<br>service. |
| Scope 3 emissions linked to digital<br>equipment (kgCOe)<br>2 | GHG emissions (Scope 3) in kgCOe evaluated for the complete year. Include<br>2<br>only digital equipment Scope 3 of the providers that is allocated to the client<br>organization’s service. |

**G242:p0099:c000 · PDF 99**

| Data | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Comments on Scope 3 data calculation | Optional comments from the provider to document the Scope 3 emissions<br>evaluation. |
| Carbon credits and offsets | Emissions that are “discounted” from the providers due to the purchasing of<br>carbon credits. |
| Digital waste weight (kg) | Weight in kg of digital equipment that has been disposed of during the<br>complete year. |
| Recycled digital waste (kg) | Weight in kg of digital equipment that has been collected for recycling during<br>the complete year. |
| Water consumption (volume) | Water consumed (in liters or cubic meters) during the manufacturing phase,<br>either due to mining or industrial activities or during the use phase for cooling,<br>for instance. |
| Volumes of data stored (GB) | Total volume of data stored associated to the service. In case of replication, the<br>full volume including replication should be provided. |
| Network traffic generated (GB) | Total network traffic input and output generated by the service in the period. |

</details>

## 附录A：缩略语

原文：A: Acronyms & Abbreviations。PDF 物理页：[100, 101, 102, 103]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

下表按原文附录 A（Acronyms & Abbreviations）顺序收录全部缩略语。原文中 CDO、SaaS 各有两个不同含义，分别列出。

|---|---|---|
| ADEME | Agence De l’Environnement et de la Maîtrise de l’Energie (French Agency for Ecological Transition) | 法国环境与能源管理署（法国生态转型署） |
| ADM | Architecture Development Method | 架构开发方法 |
| AI | Artificial Intelligence | 人工智能 |
| CapEx | Capital Expenditure | 资本支出 |
| CDN | Content Delivery Network | 内容分发网络 |

| CDO | Chief Digital Officer | 首席数字官 |
| CEO | Chief Executive Officer | 首席执行官 |
| CI/CD | Continuous Integration and Continuous Delivery/Continuous Deployment | 持续集成与持续交付/持续部署 |
| CIO | Chief Information Officer | 首席信息官 |
| CMDB | Configuration Management Database | 配置管理数据库 |
| CO2 | Carbon Dioxide | 二氧化碳 |
| CO2e | Carbon Dioxide Equivalent | 二氧化碳当量 |
| COP | Conference of the Parties to the United Nations Framework Convention on Climate Change | 《联合国气候变化框架公约》缔约方会议 |
| CSR | Corporate Social Responsibility | 企业社会责任 |
| FinOps | Financial Operations | 财务运营 |
| GB | Gigabyte = 10⁹ bytes | 吉字节（10⁹ 字节） |
| GDPR | General Data Protection Regulation | 《通用数据保护条例》 |
| GDS | Generic Data Service | 通用数据服务 |
| GEF | Green European Foundation | 绿色欧洲基金会 |
| GHG | Greenhouse Gas | 温室气体 |
| GWP | Global-Warming Potential | 全球变暖潜势 |
| I4CE | Institute for Climate Economics | 气候经济研究所 |
| IaaS | Infrastructure as a Service | 基础设施即服务 |
| IC | Integrated Circuit | 集成电路 |
| ICT | Information and Communication Technologies | 信息与通信技术 |
| IEA | International Energy Agency | 国际能源署 |
| INR | Institut du Numérique Responsible (French NGO: Institute Institute of Responsible Digital Technology)〔保留原文拼写及Institute重复〕 | 负责任数字技术研究所（法国非政府组织） |
| IPCC | Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change | 政府间气候变化专门委员会 |
| ISIT | (European) Institutes for Sustainable IT | （欧洲）可持续 IT 研究所 |
| ITIL | Information Technology Infrastructure Library | 信息技术基础设施库 |
| Kg | Kilogram | 千克 |
| KPI | Key Performance Indicator | 关键绩效指标 |
| kWh | Kilowatt-Hour | 千瓦时 |
| MOOC | Massive Open Online Course | 大规模开放在线课程 |
| MT | Metric Ton | 公吨 |
| MTBF | Mean Time Between Failure | 平均故障间隔时间 |
| NGO | Non-Governmental Organization | 非政府组织 |
| OpEx | Operational Expenditure | 运营支出 |
| PaaS | Platform as a Service | 平台即服务 |
| PUE | Power Usage Effectiveness | 电力使用效率 |
| RACI | Responsible, Accountable, Consulted, Informed | 执行负责、问责、征询意见、知会（责任分配矩阵） |
| REEN | Réduire l’Empreinte Environnementale du Numérique | 减少数字环境足迹（法国 REEN 法） |
| REN | Réferentiel Environnemental du Numérique (The Shift Project’s Reference Frame of Environmental Negative Impacts) | 数字环境参考框架（The Shift Project 环境负面影响参考框架） |
| SaaS | Software as a Service | 软件即服务 |
| SaaS | Solution as a Service | 解决方案即服务 |

| TCO | Total Cost of Ownership | 总拥有成本 |
| TSP | Territorial Sea Plan | 领海规划〔原文如此；与正文中The Shift Project的TSP语境不一致〕 |
| UNCTAD | United Nations Conference on Trade and Development | 联合国贸易和发展会议 |

| UNITAR | United Nations Institute for Training and Research | 联合国训练研究所 |
| VM | Virtual Machine | 虚拟机 |
| WEEE | Waste from Electrical and Electronic Equipment | 废弃电子电气设备 |
| XaaS | Everything as a Service | 一切皆服务 |


### 语义规则与适用边界

- Accountable为问责，不等于每项操作的批准者。
  - 适用边界：RACI不可自动替代实际授权矩阵。
  - PDF 102：Responsible, Accountable, Consulted, Informed
- 附录TSP展开为Territorial Sea Plan。
  - 适用边界：该词与正文The Shift Project语境冲突，保留原文并标歧义，不据此自动构建本体同义关系。
  - PDF 103：Territorial Sea Plan

        "quote_en": "A record 53.6 million Metric Tonnes (MT) of electronic waste (e-waste) – discarded products with a battery or plug – were generated worldwide in 2019, up 21% in just five years, according to the UN’s Global E-waste Monitor 2020 [E-Waste, 2020]. Get involved in<br>standardization processes. | Choosing open source can be a solution to<br>avoid technical obsolescence and allow longer<br>usage of hardware [Mobbs, 2012].<br>Share everything that can be shared without<br>harming the organization, to avoid the<br>environmental costs of reinventing the wheel;<br>focus efforts on sustainable transformation.<br>More openness and sharing will promote<br>interoperability; for example, on low-level<br>components, and therefore repairability.<br>Finally, optimization (Principle 7) will only<br>have to be done once.<br>Brand: Attractiveness for customers and<br>employees. |\n| 6: Reusing, repairing,<br>and recycling<br>equipment | Be efficient: use equipment while functional,<br>and make sure to shut it down after<br>replacement.   {
    "id": "G242:issue:percent-boundaries",
    "kind": "source-estimate-boundary-variance",
    "description_zh": "制造超过一半、Scope3 45%、Scope3超过一半、制造一半分别使用不同指标/范围。不能合成一个通用生命周期系数。",
    "evidence": [
      {
        "source_unit_id": "G242:p0033:c001",
        "pdf_page": 33,
        "source_block_ids": [
          "G242:p0033:b002",
          "G242:p0033:b003",
          "G242:p0033:b004",
          "G242:p0033:b005",
          "G242:p0033:b006",
          "G242:p0033:b007",
          "G242:p0033:b008",
          "G242:p0033:b009",
          "G242:p0033:b010",
          "G242:p0033:b011",
          "G242:p0033:b012",
          "G242:p0033:b013",
          "G242:p0033:b014"
        ],
        "quote_en": "Measurement is mandatory to drive organizations’ science-based decisions toward a quantitative reduction of the Information System’s environmental impact. The process to assess its effective reduction must be developed on the whole lifecycle of hardware components “from cradle to grave”. Indeed, the manufacturing production of hardware is responsible for more than half of its emissions. As Paul Mobbs states in A Practical Guide to Sustainable IT: “While many may look primarily at power consumption, the manufacture of digital electronics also has a major impact on the global environment.” [Mobbs, 2012] It uses natural resources, generates waste, and pollutes the soil and water. The search for the optimization of power consumption is leading to intense hardware renewal rates [Shift Project, 2019]. To build a sustainable information system, full end-to-end impacts must be considered, including the “Scope 3” of the carbon accounting method defined by the Greenhouse Gas Protocol. For the implementation of such measurements in an organization, a framework is proposed in Section 3.6. A first order of magnitude of CO2e emissions can be calculated with reasonable effort and minimum tooling, then more precision or higher granularity will require more investments.",
        "source_path": "togaf-standard-10th/G242e.pdf",
        "source_sha256": "517339a5e3f509f08699c10b929f5ae14b6bca356b55e76163ffe437d54d5d19"
      },
      {
        "source_unit_id": "G242:p0042:c003",
        "pdf_page": 42,
        "source_block_ids": [
          "G242:p0042:b008",
          "G242:p0042:b009",
          "G242:p0042:b010",
          "G242:p0042:b011",
          "G242:p0042:b012",
          "G242:p0042:b013"
        ],
        "quote_en": "Determining the ambition of this strategy requires a first and rather complex measurement exercise that aims to identify and quantify the company’s current digital environmental footprint in its entirety. The digital environmental impact is due to its direct electricity consumption (known as “Scope 2”) but also and above all to the upstream and downstream indirect impacts (known as “Scope 3”) – from hardware manufacture and delivery to its use in an external supplier’s data center, for example – which at a worldwide level represents 45% of ICT GHG emissions [Shift Project, 2019].",
        "source_path": "togaf-standard-10th/G242e.pdf",
        "source_sha256": "517339a5e3f509f08699c10b929f5ae14b6bca356b55e76163ffe437d54d5d19"
      },
      {
        "source_unit_id": "G242:p0060:c004",
        "pdf_page": 60,
        "source_block_ids": [
          "G242:p0060:b008",
          "G242:p0060:b009",
          "G242:p0060:b010"
        ],
        "quote_en": "• As demonstrated in “Lean-ICT: Towards Digital Sobriety” [Shift Project, 2019], more than half of its carbon footprint is in Scope 3, which means that it is related to the purchase of goods and services from suppliers",
        "source_path": "togaf-standard-10th/G242e.pdf",
        "source_sha256": "517339a5e3f509f08699c10b929f5ae14b6bca356b55e76163ffe437d54d5d19"
      },
      {
        "source_unit_id": "G242:p0071:c000",
        "pdf_page": 71,
        "source_block_ids": [
          "G242:p0071:b001",
          "G242:p0071:b002",
          "G242:p0071:b003",
          "G242:p0071:b004",
          "G242:p0071:b005"
        ],
        "quote_en": "Half of ICT’s environmental impact is driven by manufacturing [Shift Project, 2019], which does not necessarily take place in the country where the resource is used. As a result, being compliant with regulatory reporting does not necessarily make it possible to move towards sustainable ICT. This trend is reinforced by using digital hosting services to replace data centers directly operated by organizations.",
        "source_path": "togaf-standard-10th/G242e.pdf",
        "source_sha256": "517339a5e3f509f08699c10b929f5ae14b6bca356b55e76163ffe437d54d5d19"
      }
    ]
  },

    "description_zh": "3.4.4引述减排5–7%，未明确计算周期；不补写“每年”或推定企业法律目标。",

          "G242:p0042:b018",
          "G242:p0042:b019"
        ],
        "quote_en": "Modeling and redistributing this global footprint by value chain within the company will help in the second exercise of defining reduction objectives, quantifying them, and choosing the means to achieve them. Any transformation plan must be questioned regarding the ratio between economic gain and environmental impact. The global objectives can be compared with the Paris Agreement (COP21), which commits to a reduction in global emissions of 5% to 7% [Shift Project, 2019]. In France, the ambition of the National Low Carbon Strategy is to divide emissions by six by 2050 [SNBC, 2022].",

        "source_sha256": "517339a5e3f509f08699c10b929f5ae14b6bca356b55e76163ffe437d54d5d19"
      }

        "quote_en": "| Business Function | Description<br>(Description of High-Level Functions is provided in Section 3.2 |\n| --- | --- |\n| Set Overall and Function-level<br>Goals | Define the goals of the digital sustainability transformation, and of the outcomes.<br>They will include environmental indicators like COe emissions, electricity<br>2<br>consumption in kWh, but also, for example, on financial gain, security risk<br>attenuation.<br>Depending on the organization, the high-level objectives can be split into sub-<br>objectives for each business unit or function. |\n| Check the Business Model | Check if the current business model of the organization accepts a digital<br>sustainability transformation and define conditions for this change to happen.<br>This function is focused on continuous work on the compatibility between the<br>organization’s business model and the digital sustainability transformation.<br>One example: In the business model of the organization, if profitability is directly<br>proportional to the digital data volumes consumed or produced by the customers,<br>this business model could be incompatible with a digital sustainability initiative,<br>and it would need to be adapted. |\n| Build and Launch the<br>Transformation Program | Build the transformation journey with well-defined transition architectures and<br>clear intermediate results to reach in each step. This plan should be agreed and<br>shared with all stakeholders. |\n| Architecting and Building a Sustainable Information System |  |\n| State the Vision of a Sustainable<br>Information System | Build the overall vision of what should be a low environmental impact<br>information system with the Business Architecture and Usage, Application and<br>Data Architecture, and Technology Architecture. Show the main concrete levers to<br>reduce this impact. |\n| A Dual Use-Based and<br>Infrastructure-Based Architecture<br>Approach to Drive Transformation | A dual approach to identify sustainability requirements can be implemented<br>combining simultaneously: a business uses-based approach and an infrastructure-<br>based approach |\n| Reshape Business Architecture | Act to regulate ICT business usage. Work proactively with the business. Adopt a<br>process and a mindset of minimization of ICT assets such as equipment, data,<br>applications, network, etc. Challenge the actual business needs to keep essential<br>components, consider low-tech solutions, and remove “nice to have”. |\n| Reshape the Application<br>Architecture | Adapt solution choices criteria to integrate digital sustainability requirements.<br>Define data minimization as a principle for solution architecture design. All data<br>should be documented with proper complete metadata and owners to define end-<br>of-life dates by design.<br>The use of open-source development frameworks or applications is also a way to<br>mitigate technology obsolescence and increase the lifecycle of equipment. |",
        "source_path": "togaf-standard-10th/G242e.pdf",

|\n| Sustainable Software<br>Development | The software development capability should be adapted to continuously monitor<br>and minimize processing, network traffic, volumes of data, electricity<br>consumption, and, finally, environmental impact (e.g., share development<br>guidelines on sustainable development, around framework to use, open source,<br>image formats use, etc.). |\n| Measuring the Environmental Impact of the Information System from Cradle to Grave |  |\n| Build and Share Information<br>System Carbon Footprint<br>Measures from Cradle to Grave | Measures should cover all ICT equipment required for the operations of the<br>organization, whether managed directly by the organization or by its sub-<br>contractors:<br>• Workplace (laptops, smartphones, PC, ## 本指南术语约定

```json
[
  {
    "term_en": "Digital sustainability / Digital sufficiency",
    "preferred_zh": "数字可持续性 / 数字节制",
    "distinction_zh": "前者是目标和能力，后者限制不必要使用；效率提升不等于总量下降。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "IT for Green",
    "preferred_zh": "运用IT促进其他领域绿色化",
    "distinction_zh": "与ICT自身足迹分开，不自动抵销排放。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Capability / Business Function",
    "preferred_zh": "能力 / 业务功能",
    "distinction_zh": "能做什么与组织行为集合不同，不直接等于部门或软件。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Accountable / Responsible",
    "preferred_zh": "问责 / 执行负责",
    "distinction_zh": "A不是简单批准者，R不是所有者的同义词。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "CDO",
    "preferred_zh": "首席数据官或首席数字官",
    "distinction_zh": "按段落上下文区分，不做无条件单义替换。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Program",
    "preferred_zh": "项目群",
    "distinction_zh": "转型program是协调多项行动的机制，不只是计划文本。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Carbon footprint / Offset",
    "preferred_zh": "碳足迹 / 碳抵消",
    "distinction_zh": "毛排放、减排、抵消分别记录。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "CO2 / CO2e",
    "preferred_zh": "二氧化碳 / 二氧化碳当量",
    "distinction_zh": "气体范围、GWP基础不同，不自动互换。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Scope / Lifecycle stage",
    "preferred_zh": "排放核算范围 / 生命周期阶段",
    "distinction_zh": "两种分类轴不同；制造并不等于所有Scope3。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Market-based / Location-based",
    "preferred_zh": "市场法 / 地区法",
    "distinction_zh": "同一Scope2的不同核算方法，不能相加。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Recycled content / Recycling rate / Collected for recycling",
    "preferred_zh": "再生料含量 / 回收率 / 收集待回收量",
    "distinction_zh": "分子、分母和过程状态不同。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Data repository",
    "preferred_zh": "数据资料库",
    "distinction_zh": "不必是分析型数据仓库。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Environmental debt",
    "preferred_zh": "环境债务",
    "distinction_zh": "偏离目标轨迹的决定及其影响，需处理计划。"
  },
  {
    "term_en": "Decision recorded / endorsed / approved / implemented",
    "preferred_zh": "决策已记录 / 已认可 / 已批准 / 已实施",

  },
  {
